ālapanai gasañ‍ñāyaṃ –

132. sakhatau  gassai vā|

sakhatau gassa akāra ākāra ikāra īkāraaikārādaisā haunti| vāsaddaina añ‍ñasmāpi k‍vaci aikārau| yathā – bhaddantai isai iti|

a ca ā ca i ca ī ca ai cātipi ai, pubbassarānaṃ kamaina laupau|

bhau sakha bhau sakhā bhau sakhi bhau sakhī bhau sakhai, bhavantau sakhānau sakhāyau sakhinau|

‘‘sakhantassa, ārau cā’’ti ca vattatai|

133. sunamaṃsu vā|

sakhantassa ārau bhauti vā sunaṃ aṃic‍caitaisu paraisu| sakhāraṃ sakhānaṃ sakhaṃ, sakhānau sakhāyau sakhinau, sakhinā|

‘‘sakhantassā’’ti vattatai|

134. ārau himhi vā|

sakhantassa ārau hauti vā himhi vibhattimhi| sakhāraihi sakhāraibhi, sakhaihi sakhaibhi|

ikārādaisai ‘‘jhalatau sassa nau vā’’ti nau| sakhinau sakhissa, sakhārānaṃ sakhīnaṃ|

smāvacanassa nābhāvau| sakhinā, sakhāraihi sakhāraibhi sakhaihi sakhaibhi, sakhinau sakhissa, sakhārānaṃ sakhīnaṃ|

‘‘sakhatau’’ti ca vattatai|

135. smimai|

sakhatau smiṃvacanassa aikārau hauti| nic‍catthauyamārambhau| sakhai, sakhāraisu sakhaisu|

ātumasaddassa paṭhamādutiyāsu attasaddassaiva rūpanayau| ātumā, ātumānau, bhau ātuma bhau ātumā, bhavantau ātumānau, ātumānaṃ ātumaṃ, ātumānau, ātumaina ic‍cādi purisasamaṃ|

pumasaddassa bhaidau| puma si,

‘‘savibhattissā’’ti adhikārau|

136. pumantassā simhi|

pumasaddassa savibhattissa ākārādaisau hauti simhi vibhattimhi| antaggahaṇaina maghavayuvādīnamantassa ca| pumā|

‘‘pumantassā’’ti adhikārau|

137. yausvānau|

pumantassa savibhattissa ānauādaisau hauti yausu vibhattīsu| pumānau|

138. amālapanaikavacanai|

pumantassa savibhattissa aṃ hauti ālapanaikavacanai parai| hai pumaṃ, hai pumānau, pumaṃ, pumānau|

‘‘ā, vā’’ti ca vattatai|

139. u  nāmhi ca|

pumantassa āuādaisā haunti vā nāmhi vibhattimhi| casaddaina pumakammathāmantassa cu’kārau vā sasmāsu| pumānā pumunā pumaina vā|

‘‘ānai’’ti vattatai|

140. hivibhattimhi ca|

pumantassa hivibhattimhī ca ānaiādaisau hauti| vibhattiggahaṇaṃ savibhattiggahaṇanivattanatthaṃ| pumānaihi pumānaibhi|

casaddaina yuvamaghavādīnamantassa vā ānādaisau hauti sabbavibhattīsu| ‘‘u nāmhi cā’’ti aittha casaddaina pumantassukārau vā sasmāsu vibhattīsu| ‘‘jhalatau sassa nau vā’’ti nau| pumunau pumassa, pumānaṃ|

‘‘smā, nā’’ti vattatai|

141. jhalatau ca|

jhalaic‍caitaihi smāvacanassa nā hauti| caggahaṇaṃ k‍vaci nivattanatthaṃ| pumānā pumunā pumā pumasmā pumamhā, pumānaihi pumānaibhi pumaihi pumaibhi, pumunau pumassa, pumānaṃ|

142. ānai smiṃmhi vā|

pumantassa savibhattissa ānaiādaisau hauti vā smiṃmhi vibhattimhi| pumānai pumai pumasmiṃ pumamhi|

143. susmimā  vā|

pumantassa suic‍caitasmiṃ parai āādaisau hauti vā| pumāsu pumaisu|

yuvādīsu ‘‘yuva si’’ itīdha –

‘‘pumantassā simhī’’ti aittha antaggahaṇaina ākārau, ‘‘hivibhattimhi cā’’ti suttai casaddaina ānādaisau ca|

yuvā yuvānau, yuvānā yuvā, hai yuva hai yuvā hai yuvāna hai yuvānā, bhavantau yuvānā, yuvānaṃ yuvaṃ, yuvānai yuvai|

‘‘akammantassa cā’’ti aittha casaddaina yuvamaghavādīnamantassa ā hauti vā nāsuic‍caitaisūti āttaṃ|

yuvānā yuvaina yuvānaina vā, yuvānaihi yuvānaibhi yuvaihi yuvaibhi, yuvānassa yuvassa, yuvānānaṃ yuvānaṃ, yuvānā yuvānasmā yuvānamhā, yuvānaihi yuvānaibhi yuvaihi yuvaibhi, yuvānassa yuvassa, yuvānānaṃ yuvānaṃ, yuvānai yuvānasmiṃ yuvānamhi yuvai yuvamhi yuvasmiṃ, yuvānaisu yuvāsu yuvaisu|

aivaṃ maghavā maghavānau, maghavānā ic‍cādi yuvasaddasamaṃ|

akārantaṃ|

ākārantau pul‍liṅgau sāsaddau|

sā si, silaupau, sā sunakhau|

bahuvacanai –

144. aghau  rassamaikavacanayausvapi ca|

ghasañ‍ñakaākāravaj‍jitau liṅgassantau sarau rassamāpaj‍jatai aikavacanaisu yausu ca paraisūti rassattaṃ| apiggahaṇaṃ simhi nivattanatthaṃ| saisaṃ naiyyaṃ|

sā tiṭṭhanti, hai sa hai sā, hai sā, saṃ, sai, saina, sāhi sābhi, sassa sāya, sānaṃ, sā sasmā samhā, sāhi sābhi, sassa, sānaṃ, sai sasmiṃ samhi, sāsu|

aivaṃ pac‍cakkhadhammā gāṇḍīvadhanvāpabhutayau|

ākārantaṃ|

ikārantau pul‍liṅgau aggisaddau| syādyuppatti, aggi si,

‘‘antau, simhi, vā’’ti ca vattatai|

145. aggissini|

aggissa antau ini hauti vā simhi vibhattimhi| ‘‘saisatau laupaṃ gasipī’’ti silaupau| aggini aggi|

bahuvacanai ‘‘ivaṇṇuvaṇṇā jhalā’’ti jhalasañ‍ñāyaṃ –

‘‘jhalatau, vā’’ti ca vattatai|

146. ghapatau ca yaunaṃ laupau|

ghapasañ‍ñaihi itthivācakaihi ākārivaṇṇuvaṇṇaihi, jhalasañ‍ñaihi ca paraisaṃ yauvacanānaṃ laupau hauti vā| vavatthitavibhāsāyaṃ|

147. yausu  katanikāralaupaisu dīghaṃ|

liṅgassantabhūtā sabbai rassasarā yausu katanikāralaupaisu dīghamāpaj‍jantai| katā nikāralaupā yaisaṃ tai katanikāralaupā| aggī|

‘‘pañ‍cādīnamatta’’nti itau ‘‘atta’’miti vattatai|

148. yausvakatarassau jhau|

yausu paraisu akatarassau jhau attamāpaj‍jatai| aggayau|

jhauti kiṃ? rattiyau|

ālapanaipaivaṃ| hai aggi, hai aggī hai aggayau|

dutiyaikavacanai pubbassaralaupai sampattai –

149. aṃ mau niggahītaṃ jhalapaihi|

jhalapaic‍caitaihi parassa aṃvacanassa, makārassa ca niggahītaṃ ādaisau hauti| aggiṃ|

aggī aggayau, agginā, aggīhi aggībhi, ‘‘sunaṃhisu cā’’ti aittha caggahaṇaina katthaci dīghābhāvau| aggihi aggibhi|

‘‘jhalatau sassa nau vā’’ti nau| agginau aggissa, aggīnaṃ|

‘‘smā nā’’ti vattamānai ‘‘jhalatau cā’’ti vikappaina nā| agginā aggismā aggimhā, aggīhi aggībhi aggihi aggibhi, agginau aggissa, aggīnaṃ agginaṃ, aggimhi aggismiṃ, aggīsu aggisu|

aivamañ‍ñaipi –

jauti pāṇi gaṇṭhi muṭṭhi, kucchi vatthi sāli vīhi|

byādhi audhi baudhi sandhi, rāsi kaisi sāti dīpi||

isi  muni maṇi dhani yati giri ravi kavi,

kapi asi masi nidhi vidhi ahi kimi pati|

hari ari timi kali bali jaladhi gahapati,

urudhiti varamati nirupadhi adhipati||

añ‍jali sārathi atithi samādhi udadhippabhutayau|

ikārantaṃ|

īkārantau pul‍liṅgau daṇḍīsaddau|

‘‘daṇḍī si’’ itīdha –

‘‘aghau rassamaikavacanayausvapi cā’’ti rassattai sampattai aitthaivāpiggahaṇaina sismiṃ tadabhāvai siddhai niyamatthamāha|

‘‘rassa’’nti vattamānai –

150. na sismimanapuṃsakāni|

sismiṃ napuṃsakavaj‍jitāni liṅgāni na rassamāpaj‍jantaiti rassattābhāvau, silaupau| daṇḍī tiṭṭhati|

anapuṃsakānīti kiṃ? sukhakāri dānaṃ|

aittha ca –

visadāvisadākāra-vauhāraubhayamuttakā|

pumādijānanai haitu-bhāvatau liṅgamīritā||

yaulaupai daṇḍī tiṭṭhanti|

itaratra ‘‘aghau rassa’’mic‍cādinā rassattai katai –

‘‘jhatau, katarassā’’ti ca vattatai|

151. yaunaṃ  nau|

sabbaisaṃ yaunaṃ sālapanānaṃ jhatau katarassā paraisaṃ nauic‍cādaisau hauti| daṇḍinau tiṭṭhanti|

katarassāti kiṃ? aggayau|

adhikāraṃ vinā ‘‘yaunaṃ,

nau’’ti yaugavibhāgatau|

k‍vaci akatarassāpi,

nau sāramatinau yathā||

ālapanai ‘‘gai’’ti vattatai|

152. jhalapā rassaṃ|

jhalapaic‍caitai rassamāpaj‍jantai gai parai| bhau daṇḍi|

‘‘aghau rassa’’ntiādināva siddhaipi rassattai punārambhau niyamatthau, taina ‘‘bhau gau’’tiādīsu na bhavati| daṇḍī daṇḍinau|

dutiyāyaṃ rassattai katai ‘‘a’’miti vattatai, ‘‘ghapatau smiṃ yaṃ vā’’ti itau maṇḍūkagatiyā ‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

153. naṃ jhatau katarassā|

jhatau katarassā parassa aṃvacanassa naṃ hauti vā| daṇḍinaṃ daṇḍiṃ, daṇḍī daṇḍinau, daṇḍinā, daṇḍīhi daṇḍībhi, daṇḍinau daṇḍissa, daṇḍīnaṃ|

‘‘jhalatau cā’’ti nā| daṇḍinā daṇḍismā daṇḍimhā, daṇḍīhi daṇḍībhi, daṇḍinau daṇḍissa, daṇḍīnaṃ|

‘‘jhatau, katarassā’’ti ca vattatai, purai viya ‘‘vā’’ti ca|

154. smiṃ  ni|

jhatau katarassā parassa smiṃvacanassa niic‍cādaisau hauti vā| daṇḍini daṇḍismiṃ daṇḍimhi, daṇḍīsu|

aivamañ‍ñānipi –

dhammī saṅghī ñāṇī hatthī, cak‍kī pakkhī dāṭhī raṭṭhī|

chattī mālī vammī yaugī, bhāgī bhaugī kāmī sāmī||

dhajī gaṇī sasī kuṭṭhī, jaṭī yānī sukhī sikhī|

dantī mantī karī cāgī, kusalī musalī balī||

pāpakārī sattughātī, mālyakārī dīghajīvī|

dhammacārī sīghayāyī, sīhanādī bhūmisāyī –

ic‍cādīni īkārantanāmāni|

gāmaṇīsaddassa tu sattamiyaṃ bhaidau|

gāmaṇī, gāmaṇī gāmaṇinau, bhau gāmaṇi, bhauntau gāmaṇī bhauntau gāmaṇinau, gāmaṇinaṃ gāmaṇiṃ, gāmaṇī gāmaṇinau|

saisaṃ daṇḍīsamaṃ| niādaisābhāvauva visaisau| aivaṃ sainānī sudhīppabhutayau|

īkārantaṃ|

ukārantau pul‍liṅgau bhikkhusaddau|

tathaiva bhikkhusaddatau si, silaupau| sau bhikkhu|

bahuvacanai ‘‘ghapatau ca yaunaṃ laupau’’ti yaulaupau, ‘‘yausu kata’’ic‍cādinā dīghau| tai bhikkhū|

laupābhāvai ‘‘vā, yauna’’nti ca vattatai|

155. latau  vaukārau ca|

lasañ‍ñatau paraisaṃ yauvacanānaṃ vaukārādaisau hauti vā| kāraggahaṇaina yaunaṃ nau ca hauti| casaddaggahaṇaṃ katthaci nivattanatthaṃ| atha vā caggahaṇaṃ nauggahaṇānuvattanatthaṃ, taina jantusabbañ‍ñūāditau yaunaṃ nau ca hauti| vāsaddau vavatthitavibhāsatthau| taina –

bhikkhuppabhutitau nic‍caṃ, vau yaunaṃ haituāditau|

vibhāsā na ca vau nau ca, amuppabhutitau bhavai||

‘‘attaṃ, akatarassau’’ti ca vattatai|

156. vaivausu lau ca|

vai vauic‍caitaisu ca paraisu akatarassau lau attamāpaj‍jatai| bhikkhavau, bhau bhikkhu, bhavantau bhikkhū|

laupābhāvai –

157. akatarassā latau yvālapanassa vaivau|

akatarassā latau parassa ālapanai vihitassa yauic‍caitassa vai vauādaisā haunti| attaṃ| bhavantau bhikkhavai bhikkhavau| ‘‘aṃ mau niggahītaṃ jhalapaihī’’ti niggahītaṃ|

bhikkhuṃ, bhikkhū bhikkhavau, bhikkhunā, bhikkhūhi bhikkhūbhi bhikkhuhi bhikkhubhi, bhikkhunau bhikkhussa, bhikkhūnaṃ  bhikkhunaṃ, bhikkhunā bhikkhusmā bhikkhumhā, bhikkhūhi bhikkhūbhi bhikkhuhi bhikkhubhi, bhikkhunau bhikkhussa, bhikkhūnaṃ bhikkhunaṃ, bhikkhumhi bhikkhusmiṃ, bhikkhūsu bhikkhusu|

aivaṃ saitu kaitu rāhu bhānu paṅgu ucchu vaiḷu mac‍cu sindhu bandhu nairu mairu sattu kāru haitu jantu ruru paṭu ic‍cādayau|

haitujantusaddānaṃ paṭhamādutiyāsu visaisau|

haitu, haitū haitavau haituyau, bhau haitu, bhauntau haitū haitavai haitavau, haituṃ, haitū haitavau haituyau| saisaṃ bhikkhusamaṃ|

jantu, jantū jantavau| kāraggahaṇaina yaunaṃ nau ca hauti| jantunau jantuyau, bhau jantu, jantū jantavai jantavau, jantuṃ, jantū jantavau jantunau jantuyau ic‍cādi|

satthusaddassa bhaidau| ‘‘satthu si’’itīdha –

‘‘antau’’ti vattatai|

158. satthupitādīnamā sismiṃ silaupau ca|

satthu pitu mātu bhātu dhītu kattuic‍caivamādīnamantau āttamāpaj‍jatai sismiṃ, silaupau ca hauti| satthā|

‘‘satthu pitādīna’’nti adhikārau|

159. añ‍ñaisvārattaṃ|

satthu pitādīnamantau sivacanatau añ‍ñaisu vacanaisu ārattamāpaj‍jatai| ārattamitibhāvaniddaisaina katthaci aniyamaṃ dassaiti|

āraggahaṇamanuvattatai|

160. tatau yaunamau tu|

tatau ārādaisatau sabbaisaṃ yaunaṃ aukārādaisau hauti|

tuggahaṇaina añ‍ñaihipi catuubhanadīgavādīhi yaunamaukārau hauti, saralaupādi| satthārau|

ālapanai ‘‘akārapitādyantānamā’’ti āttaṃ| ‘‘gai rassa’’nti adhikic‍ca ‘‘ākārau vā’’ti vikappaina rassattaṃ, valaupau| bhau sattha bhau satthā, bhavantau satthārau, satthāraṃ, satthārai satthārau|

‘‘tatau’’ti ca vattatai|

161. nā ā|

tatau ārādaisatau nāvacanassa ākārādaisau hauti| satthārā, satthunāti ārattamitibhāvaniddaisaina siddhaṃ| satthāraihi satthāraibhi|

‘‘vā naṃmhī’’ti itau ‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

162. u  sasmiṃ salaupau ca|

satthu pituic‍caivamādīnamantassa uttaṃ hauti vā sasmiṃ, salaupau ca hauti| ārādaisāpavādauyaṃ| satthu, añ‍ñattha bhāvaniddaisainārābhāvau| satthussa satthunau|

‘‘āratta’’nti vattatai|

163. vā naṃmhi|

satthu pituādīnamantau ārattamāpaj‍jatai vā naṃmhi vibhattimhi| satthārānaṃ|

ārābhāvai ‘‘vā naṃmhī’’ti vattatai|

164. satthunattañ‍ca|

satthusaddantassa, pitādīnamantassa ca attaṃ hauti vā naṃmhi vibhattimhi, puna satthuggahaṇaṃ satthunau nic‍cavidhānatthaṃ| satthānaṃ|

‘‘amhatumhanturājabrahmattasakhasatthupitādīhi smā nāvā’’ti smāvacanassanābhāvau| satthārā, satthāraihi satthāraibhi, satthu satthunau satthussa, satthārānaṃ satthānaṃ|

‘‘āratau’’ti vattatai|

165. tatau smimi|

tatau ārādaisatau smiṃvacanassa ikārādaisau hauti| puna tatauggahaṇaina añ‍ñasmāpi smiṃvacanassa ikārau| yathā – bhuvi, divi|

166. ārau  rassamikārai|

ārādaisau rassamāpaj‍jatai ikārai parai, satthari, satthāraisu|

aivaṃ kattā, kattārau, bhau katta bhau kattā, bhavantau kattārau, kattāraṃ, kattārai kattārau, kattārā, kattāraihi kattāraibhi| ‘‘u sasmiṃ salaupau cā’’ti uttaṃ, salaupau ca| kattu kattunau kattussa, kattārānaṃ kattānaṃ kattūnaṃ kattunaṃ, kattārā, kattāraihi kattāraibhi, kattu kattunau kattussa, kattārānaṃ kattānaṃ kattūnaṃ kattunaṃ, kattari, kattāraisu, ārābhāvai kattūsu kattusu|

aivaṃ –

bhattu vattu naitu sautu, ñātu jaitu chaittu bhaittu| dātu dhātu nattu bauddhu, viñ‍ñāpaitu ādayaupi|

‘‘u sasmiṃ salaupau cā’’ti vattatai|

167. sakamandhātādīnañ‍ca|

sakamandhātuic‍caivamādīnamantau ca uttamāpaj‍jatai sasmiṃ, salaupau ca, nic‍caṃ punabbidhānā| sakamandhātu viya assa rājinau vibhavau, saisaṃ samaṃ| aivaṃ mahāmandhātuppabhutayau|

pitusaddassa bhaidau| simhi āttaṃ, silaupau, pitā|

yaumhi ‘‘ārau, rassa’’nti ca vattatai|

168. pitādīnamasimhi|

pitādīnamārādaisau rassamāpaj‍jatai asimhi vibhattimhi| sismiṃ ārādaisābhāvaipi asimhīti adhikavacanamatthantaraviñ‍ñāpanatthaṃ, taina tauādimhi pitādīnamikārau ca| yathā – pititau, mātitau, bhātitau, dhītitau, pitipakkhau, mātipakkhauti|

pitarau, saisaṃ kattusamaṃ| bhau pita bhau pitā, bhavantau pitarau, pitaraṃ, pitarai pitarau, pitarā pitunā, pitaraihi pitaraibhi| bhāvaniddaisaina ārādaisābhāvai pitūhi pitūbhi pituhi pitubhi, pitu pitunau pitussa, pitarānaṃ pitānaṃ pitūnaṃ, dīghābhāvai pitunaṃ vā, pitarā, pitaraihi pitaraibhi pitūhi pitūbhi pituhi pitubhi, pitu pitunau pitussa, pitarānaṃ pitānaṃ pitūnaṃ pitunaṃ, pitari, pitaraisu pitūsu pitusu|

aivaṃ bhātā, bhātarau ic‍cādi|

ukārantaṃ|

ūkārantau pul‍liṅgau abhibhūsaddau|

tathaiva syādyuppatti, silaupau| sau abhibhū, yaulaupai katai tai abhibhū|

‘‘aghau rassa’’ntiādinā rassattaṃ, vaukārau| katarassattā attābhāvau| abhibhuvau, bhau abhibhu, bhavantau abhibhū abhibhuvau|

katarassattā vaiādaisau na hauti| saisaṃ bhikkhusaddasamaṃ, rassattamaiva visaisau| abhibhuṃ, abhibhū abhibhuvau, abhibhunā, abhibhūhi abhibhūbhi, abhibhunau abhibhussa, abhibhūnaṃ ic‍cādi|

aivaṃ sayambhū, vaissabhū, parābhibhū, sahabhūādayau| sahabhūsaddassa yaunaṃ nauādaisauva visaisau| sahabhū, sahabhū sahabhuvau sahabhunau ic‍cādi|

tathā sabbañ‍ñūsaddassa yausvaiva visaisau| sau sabbañ‍ñū tai sabbañ‍ñū, yaulaupābhāvai rassattaṃ, ‘‘latau vaukārau cā’’ti aittha kāraggahaṇaina yaunaṃ nauādaisau| vādhikārassa vavatthitavibhāsattā na ca vaukārau| sabbañ‍ñunau, bhau sabbañ‍ñu, bhauntau sabbañ‍ñū sabbañ‍ñunau, sabbañ‍ñuṃ, sabbañ‍ñū sabbañ‍ñunau ic‍cādi|

aivaṃ maggañ‍ñū dhammañ‍ñū atthañ‍ñū kālañ‍ñū rattañ‍ñū mattañ‍ñū katañ‍ñū tathañ‍ñū viñ‍ñū vidū vaidagū pāragū ic‍cādayau|

ūkārantaṃ|

aikārantau appasiddhau|

aukārantau pul‍liṅgau gausaddau|

tatau syādyuppatti, silaupau, gau gacchati|

‘‘gāva sai’’ti itau ‘‘gau’’ti adhikārau, ‘‘āvā’’ti ca vattatai|

169. yausu ca|

gauic‍caitassa aukārassa āvādaisau hauti yausu| casaddaina nā smā smiṃsuic‍caitaisu ca| ‘‘tatau yaunamau tū’’ti aittha tuggahaṇaina yaunamaukārau, saralaupādi| gāvau tiṭṭhanti|

170. avaṃmhi  ca|

gauic‍caitassa aukārassa āva avaic‍caitai ādaisā haunti aṃmhi vibhattimhi| saiddaina yau nā sa smāsmiṃsuic‍caitaisu ca avādaisau hauti| gavau gacchanti, hai gau, hai gāvau hai gavau|

dutiyāyaṃ ‘‘aṃmhī’’ti vattatai|

171. āvassu vā|

āvaic‍caitassa gāvādaisassa antasarassa ukārādaisau hauti vā aṃmhi vibhattimhi| āvassa a āva, tassa āvasaddantassa| ‘‘aṃmau’’tiādinā niggahītaṃ| gāvuṃ gāvaṃ gavaṃ, gāvau gavau|

‘‘gauṇa, vā’’ti vattatai|

172. suhināsu ca|

su hi nāic‍caitaisu sabbassa gausaddassa gauṇādaisau hauti vā| casaddaina saisaisu ca| gauṇau, gauṇā, hai gauṇa hai gauṇā, gauṇaṃ, gauṇai, gauṇaina, gauṇaihi gauṇaibhi, gauṇassa|

173. gauṇa naṃmhi vā|

sabbassa gausaddassa gauṇādaisau hauti vā naṃmhi vibhattimhi| gauṇānaṃ, gauṇā gauṇasmā gauṇamhā, gauṇaihi gauṇaibhi, gauṇassa, gauṇānaṃ, gauṇai gauṇasmiṃ gauṇamhi, gauṇaisu|

gauṇādaisābhāvai gāvaina gavaina, gauhi gaubhi|

174. gāva  sai|

‘‘gau āva sai’’iti tipadamidaṃ| gaussa au gau, gausaddaukārassa āvādaisau hauti sai vibhattimhi| gāvassa gavassa|

naṃmhi ‘‘gau, avā’’ti ca vattatai|

175. tatau namaṃ patimhāluttai ca samāsai|

tatau gausaddatau parassa naṃvacanassa aṃādaisau hauti, gausaddaukārassa avādaisau ca patimhi parai aluttai ca samāsai| casaddaina asamāsaipi aṃ avādaisā| gavaṃpatissa thairassa, gavaṃ|

‘‘suhināsu cā’’ti aittha casaddaina naṃmhi guādaisau| ‘‘nau ca dvāditau naṃmhī’’ti suttai casaddaina nakārāgamau ca| gun‍naṃgaunaṃ vā|

gāvā gāvamhā gāvasmā gavā gavamhā gavasmā, gauhi gaubhi, gāvassa gavassa, gavaṃ gun‍naṃ gaunaṃ, gāvai gāvamhi gāvasmiṃ gavai gavamhi gavasmiṃ, gāvaisu gavaisu gausu|

aukārantaṃ|

purisau guṇavā rājā, sā’ggi daṇḍī ca bhikkhu ca|

satthā’bhibhū ca sabbañ‍ñū, gauti pul‍liṅgasaṅgahau||

pul‍liṅgaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

itthiliṅga

atha  itthiliṅgāni vuc‍cantai|

akārantau itthiliṅgasaddau appasiddhau|

ākārantau itthiliṅgau kañ‍ñāsaddau| ‘‘kañ‍ña’’iti ṭhitai –

176. itthiyamatau āppac‍cayau|

itthiyaṃ vattamānā akārantatau liṅgamhā parau āppac‍cayau hauti|

pakatyatthajautakā itthi-ppac‍cayā syādayau viya|

ṇādayau pac‍cayatthassa, sakatthassāpi vācakā||

‘‘saralaupau’’tiādinā pubbassarai luttai, paranayanai ca katai ‘‘dhātuppac‍cayavibhattivaj‍jitamatthavaṃ liṅga’’nti vuttattāva pac‍cayantassāpi aliṅgattā vibhattuppattiyamasampattāyaṃ ‘‘taddhitasamāsakitakā nāmaṃ vā’tavaitunādīsu cā’’ti aittha vaggahaṇaina itthippac‍cayantassāpi nāmabyapadaisau| purai viya syādyuppatti, ‘‘saisatau laupaṃ gasipī’’ti silaupau| sā kañ‍ñā|

bahuvacanai ‘‘ālapanai si gasañ‍ñau’’ti itau ‘‘sañ‍ñau’’, ‘‘tai itthikhyā pau’’ti itau ‘‘itthikhyā’’ti ca vattatai|

177. ā ghau|

liṅgassantau ākārau yadā itthikhyau, tadā ghasañ‍ñau hautīti ghasañ‍ñāyaṃ ‘‘ghapatau ca yaunaṃ laupau’’ti  vikappaina yaulaupau| tā kañ‍ñā kañ‍ñāyau|

ālapanai ‘‘sakhatau gassai vā’’ti itau ‘‘gassā’’ti vattatai|

178. ghatai ca|

ghatau parassa gassa aikārau hauti, saralaupādi| bhauti kañ‍ñai, bhautiyau kañ‍ñā kañ‍ñāyau|

aṃmhi saralaupapakatibhāvā kañ‍ñaṃ, kañ‍ñā kañ‍ñāyau|

tatiyādīsu ‘‘āya catutthaikavacanassa tū’’ti itau ‘‘āyau, aikavacanāna’’nti ca vattatai|

179. ghatau nādīnaṃ|

ghasañ‍ñatau liṅgassākārā paraisaṃ nādīnaṃ smiṃpariyantānaṃ aikavacanānaṃ vibhattigaṇānaṃ āyādaisau hauti| saralaupaparanayanāni| kañ‍ñāya, kañ‍ñāhi kañ‍ñābhi, kañ‍ñāya, kañ‍ñānaṃ, kañ‍ñāya, kañ‍ñāhi kañ‍ñābhi, kañ‍ñāya, kañ‍ñānaṃ|

smiṃmhi –

180. ghapatau smiṃ yaṃ vā|

ghapatau parassa smiṃvacanassa yaṃ hauti vā, añ‍ñatthāyādaisau| kañ‍ñāyaṃ kañ‍ñāya, kañ‍ñāsu|

aivamañ‍ñaipi –

saddhā maidhā pañ‍ñā vij‍jā, cintā mantā vīṇā taṇhā|

icchā mucchā aijā māyā, maittā mattā sikkhā bhikkhā||

jaṅghā  gīvā jivhā vācā,

chāyā āsā gaṅgā nāvā|

gāthā sainā laikhā sālā,

mālā vailā pūjā khiḍḍā||

pipāsā vaidanā sañ‍ñā, caitanā tasiṇā pajā|

daivatā vaṭṭakā gaudhā, balākā parisā sabhā||

ūkāsaiphālikā laṅkā, salākā vālikā sikhā|

visākhā visikhā sākhā, vacā vañjhā jaṭā ghaṭā||

jaiṭṭhā sauṇḍā vitaṇḍā ca, karuṇā vanitā latā|

kathā niddā sudhā rādhā, vāsanā siṃsapā papā||

pabhā sīmā khamā jāyā,

khattiyā sakkharā surā|

daulā tulā silā līlā,

lālai’lā maikhalā kalā||

vaḷavā’lambusā mūsā, mañ‍jūsā sulasā disā|

nāsā juṇhā guhā īhā, lasikā vasudhādayau||

ammādīnaṃ ālapanaiva rūpabhaidau| ammā, ammā ammāyau|

gassa ‘‘ghatai cā’’ti aikārai sampattai –

181. na ammāditau|

ammā an‍nāic‍caivamāditau parassa gassa ālapanaikavacanassa na aikārattaṃ hauti| ‘‘ākārau vā’’ti rassattaṃ|

bhauti amma bhauti ammā, bhautiyau ammā ammāyau| aivaṃ an‍nā, an‍nā an‍nāyau, bhauti an‍na bhauti an‍nā, bhautiyau an‍nā an‍nāyau| ambā, ambā ambāyau, bhauti amba bhauti ambā, bhautiyau ambā ambāyau ic‍cādi|

ākārantaṃ|

ikārantau itthiliṅgau rattisaddau|

tathaiva syādyuppatti, silaupau, ratti||

bahuvacanai ‘‘sañ‍ñā, ivaṇṇuvaṇṇā’’ti ca vattatai|

182. tai itthikhyā pau|

itthiyā ākhyā sañ‍ñā itthikhyā, liṅgassantā tai ivaṇṇuvaṇṇā yadā itthikhyā, tadā pasañ‍ñā hauntīti pasañ‍ñāyaṃ ‘‘ghapatau cā’’tiādinā yaulaupau, ‘‘yausu kata’’ic‍cādinā dīghau| rattī rattiyau ratyau vā, hai ratti, hai rattī hai rattiyau|

‘‘aṃmau’’tiādinā niggahītaṃ, rattiṃ, rattī rattiyau|

tatiyādīsu ‘‘aikavacanānaṃ, nādīna’’nti ca vattatai||

183. patau yā|

pasañ‍ñatau ivaṇṇuvaṇṇaihi paraisaṃ nādīnamaikavacanānaṃ vibhattigaṇānaṃ yāādaisau hauti| rattiyā, rattīhi rattībhi rattihi rattibhi, rattiyā, rattīnaṃ rattinaṃ|

pañ‍camiyaṃ –

184. amā patau smiṃsmānaṃ vā|

paic‍caitasmā paraisaṃ smiṃ smāic‍caitaisaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ aṃ āādaisā haunti vā| vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau, taina uvaṇṇantatau na haunti, ivaṇṇantataupi yathāpayaugaṃ|

‘‘sarai, yakārau’’ti ca vattatai, sīhamaṇḍūkagatīhi yauvacanaikavacanaggahaṇañ‍ca|

185. pasañ‍ñassa ca|

pasañ‍ñassa ivaṇṇassa yauvacanaikavacanavibhattīnamādaisai sarai parai yakārau hauti| aittha ca yakārassaivādhikāratau pasañ‍ñaggahaṇaina ivaṇṇauva gayhati, caggahaṇaṃ ‘‘rattau’’tiādīsu nivattanatthaṃ| ratyā rattiyā, rattīhi rattībhi rattihi rattibhi, rattiyā, rattīnaṃ rattinaṃ| smiṃvacanai amādaisayakārādaisā, ratyaṃ| ‘‘ghapatau smiṃ yaṃ vā’’ti yaṃādaisau, rattiyaṃ|

añ‍ñattha ‘‘aṃ, smiṃ, vā’’ti ca vattatai|

186. āditau au ca|

ādiic‍caitasmā smiṃvacanassa aṃ auādaisā haunti vā| casaddaina añ‍ñasmāpi ā aṃ auādaisā| ratyā rattiṃ rattau rattiyā, rattīsu rattisu|

aivamañ‍ñānipi –

patti yutti vutti kitti, mutti titti khanti kanti|

santi tanti siddhi suddhi, iddhi vuddhi buddhi baudhi||

bhūmi jāti pīti sūti, nandi sandhi sāṇi kauṭi|

diṭṭhi vuḍḍhi tuṭṭhi yaṭṭhi, pāḷi āḷi nāḷi kaiḷi|

sati mati gati cuti, dhiti yuvati vikati||

rati ruci rasmi asani vasani ausadhi aṅguli dhūli dundubhi dauṇi aṭavi chavi ādīni ikārantanāmāni|

ikārantaṃ|

īkārantau  itthiliṅgau itthīsaddau|

‘‘ittha’’itīdha ‘‘itthiyaṃ, pac‍cayau’’ti ca vattatai||

187. nadāditau vā ī|

nadāditau vā anadāditau vā itthiyaṃ vattamānā liṅgamhā īppac‍cayau hauti| vāggahaṇamanadādisampiṇḍanatthaṃ, taina puthugavāditau ca ī|

saralaupai ‘‘k‍vacāsavaṇṇaṃ luttai’’ti asavaṇṇai sampattai pakatibhāvau nāmabyapadaisau, syādyuppatti| itthī, itthī ‘‘aghau rassa’’ntiādinā rassattaṃ, itthiyau| sambaudhanai ‘‘jhalapā rassa’’nti rassattaṃ| bhauti itthi, bhautiyau itthī itthiyau|

dutiyaikavacanai ‘‘ghapatau smiṃ yaṃ vā’’ti itau ‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

188. aṃ yamītau pasañ‍ñatau|

pasañ‍ñatau īkāratau parassa aṃvacanassa yaṃ hauti vā| itthiyaṃ itthiṃ, itthī itthiyau, itthiyā, itthīhi itthībhi, itthiyā, itthīnaṃ, itthiyā, itthīhi itthībhi, itthiyā, itthīnaṃ, itthiyaṃ itthiyā, itthīsu|

aivaṃ nadī, nadī| yaulaupābhāvai ‘‘tatau yaunamau tū’’ti aittha tusaddaina yaunamaukārau ca, ‘‘pasañ‍ñassa cā’’ti īkārassa yakārau, ‘‘yavataṃ talana’’ic‍cādinā dyassa jakārau, dvittaṃ| naj‍jau sandanti, nadiyau|

aittha  caivaṃ sijjhantānaṃ naj‍jauādīnaṃ vuttiyaṃ ānattaggahaṇādinā nipphādanaṃ atraja sugatādīnaṃ viya nipphādanūpāyantaradassanatthanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

hai nadi, hai nadī hai naj‍jau hai nadiyau, nadiyaṃ nadiṃ, nadī naj‍jau nadiyau|

amādisuttai ā patauti yaugavibhāgaina k‍vaci nāsānañ‍cāttaṃ, taina na jac‍cā vasalau hauti, pathabyā aikaraj‍jaināti ādi ca sijjhati, purai viya yakārajakārādaisadvittāni|

naj‍jā kataṃ nadiyā, nadīhi nadībhi, naj‍jā nadiyā, nadīnaṃ, naj‍jā nadiyā, nadīhi nadībhi, naj‍jā nadiyā, nadīnaṃ, naj‍jaṃ nadiyaṃ nadiyā, nadīsu|

añ‍ñaipi –

mahī vaitaraṇī vāpī, pāṭalī kadalī ghaṭī|

nārī kumārī taruṇī, vāruṇī brāhmaṇī sakhī||

gandhabbī kin‍narī nāgī, daivī yakkhī ajī migī|

vānarī sūkarī sīhī, haṃsī kākī ca kuk‍kuṭī –

ic‍cādayau itthīsaddasamā|

tathaiva mātulasaddatau īppac‍cayai katai –

189. mātulādīnamānattamīkārai|

mātula ayyakavaruṇaic‍caivamādīnamantau ānattamāpaj‍jatai īkārai pac‍cayai parai, antāpaikkhāyaṃ chaṭṭhī, saralaupādi| mātulānī, aivaṃ ayyakānī, varuṇānī, saisaṃ itthīsaddasamaṃ|

anadādīsu  puthusaddatau īppac‍cayau| ‘‘au sarai cā’’ti aittha casaddaina ukārassa avādaisau| puthavī, puthaviyau| sasmā smiṃsu puthabyā puthaviyā, puthabyā puthaviyā, puthabyaṃ puthaviyaṃ puthaviyā ic‍cādi|

gausaddatau ‘‘nadāditau vā ī’’ti īppac‍cayau| mahāvuttinā vā ‘‘gāva sai’’ti aittha gāvaiti yaugavibhāgaina vā aukārassa āvādaisau| gāvī, gāvī gāviyau ic‍cādi itthīsaddasamaṃ|

‘‘mānava’’itīdha ‘‘itthiyaṃ, vā, ī’’ti ca vattatai|

190. ṇava ṇika ṇaiyya ṇantuhi|

ṇava ṇika ṇaiyya ṇantuppac‍cayantaihi itthiyaṃ vattamānaihi liṅgaihi īppac‍cayau hauti| vādhikārau katthaci nivattanatthau, saralaupādi| mānavī, aivaṃ nāvikī, vainataiyyī, gautamī|

‘‘guṇavantu ī’’itīdha ‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

191. ntussa tamīkārai|

sabbassaiva ntuppac‍cayassa takārau hauti vā īkārappac‍cayai parai, añ‍ñattha saralaupādi| guṇavatī, guṇavatī guṇavatiyau, guṇavantī, guṇavantī guṇavantiyau ic‍cādi itthīsaddasamaṃ|

aivaṃ kulavatī, sīlavatī, yasavatī, rūpavatī, satimatī, gauttamatī|

mahantasaddatau ‘‘nadāditau vā ī’’ti īppac‍cayau, ntubyapadaisau vikappaina takārādaisau| mahatī mahantī|

‘‘bhavanta ī’’itīdha ‘‘īkārai’’ti vattatai|

192. bhavatau  bhautau|

sabbassaiva bhavantasaddassa bhautādaisau hauti īkārai itthigatai parai| sā bhautī, bhautī bhautiyau, hai bhauti, hai bhautī bhautiyau ic‍cādi|

‘‘bhikkhu’’ itīdha ‘‘itthiya’’nti vattatai ‘‘vā’’ti ca|

193. patibhikkhurājīkārantaihi inī|

pati bhikkhu rāja ic‍caitaihi īkārantaihi ca itthiyaṃ vattamānaihi liṅgaihi inīppac‍cayau hauti|

‘‘saralaupau’mādaisa’’ic‍cādisuttai tuggahaṇaina k‍vaci pubbalaupassa nisaidhanatau ‘‘vā parau asarūpā’’ti saralaupau| bhikkhunī, bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyau ic‍cādi|

gahapatisaddatau inī, ‘‘atta’’miti vattatai|

194. patissinīmhi|

patisaddassa antau attamāpaj‍jatai inīppac‍cayai parai| tathaiva parasarai luttai ‘‘pubbau cā’’ti dīghau, gahapatānī|

tathaiva rājasaddatau inī, saralaupapakatibhāvā, rājinī| īkārantaisu daṇḍīsaddatau inī, saralaupādi, daṇḍinī, daṇḍinī daṇḍiniyau, aivaṃ hatthinī, maidhāvinī, tapassinī, piyabhāṇinī ic‍cādi|

‘‘paukkharinī’’ itīdha ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā ikāranakārānaṃ akāraṇakārādaisā, paukkharaṇī, paukkharaṇī| ‘‘tatau yaunamau tū’’ti suttai tuggahaṇaina yaunamaukārau  ca, īkārassa yakārau, ‘‘yavata’’mic‍cādisuttai kāraggahaṇaina ṇyassa ñakārau, dvittaṃ| paukkharañ‍ñau paukkharaṇiyau vā ic‍cādi|

vādhikārau anuttasamuc‍cayatthau, taina vidū yakkhāditaupi inī, paracittavidunī, saralauparassattāni, paracittavidunī paracittaviduniyau, yakkhinī yakkhiniyau, sīhinī sīhiniyau ic‍cādi|

īkārantaṃ|

ukārantau itthiliṅgau yāgusaddau|

tassa rattisaddassaiva rūpanayau| amādaisādiabhāvauva visaisau|

yāgu, yāgū yāguyau, hai yāgu, hai yāgū yāguyau, yāguṃ, yāgū yāguyau, yāguyā, yāgūhi yāgūbhi yāguhi yāgubhi, yāguyā, yāgūnaṃ yāgunaṃ, yāguyā, yāgūhi yāgūbhi yāguhi yāgubhi, yāguyā, yāgūnaṃ yāgunaṃ, yāguyaṃ yāguyā, yāgūsu yāgusu|

aivaṃ dhātu dhainu kāsu daddu kacchu kaṇḍu raj‍ju karaiṇu piyaṅgu sassuādīni|

mātusaddassa bhaidau| tassa pitusaddassaiva rūpanayau| ‘‘āratta’’miti bhāvaniddaisaina ārādaisābhāvai ‘‘patau yā’’ti yādaisauva visaisau|

mātā, mātarau, bhauti māta, bhauti mātā bhautiyau mātarau, mātaraṃ, mātarai mātarau, mātarā mātuyā matyā, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā ukāralaupau, rassattañ‍ca| mātaraihi mātaraibhi mātūhi mātūbhi mātuhi mātubhi, mātu mātussa mātuyā, mātarānaṃ mātānaṃ mātūnaṃ mātunaṃ, mātarā mātuyā, mātaraihi mātaraibhi mātūhi mātūbhi, mātu mātussa mātuyā, mātarānaṃ mātānaṃ mātūnaṃ mātunaṃ, mātari, mātaraisu mātūsu mātusu|

aivaṃ dhītā, dhītarau, duhitā, duhitarau ic‍cādi|

ukārantaṃ|

ūkārantau itthiliṅgau jambūsaddau|

jambū, jambū jambuyau, hai jambu, hai jambū jambuyau, jambuṃ, jambū jambuyau ic‍cādi itthīsaddasamaṃ|

aivaṃ vadhū ca sarabhū, sarabū sutanū camū|

vāmūrū nāganāsūrū, samāni khalu jambuyā||

ūkārantaṃ|

aukārantau itthiliṅgau gausaddau|

tassa pul‍liṅgagausaddassaiva rūpanayau|

kañ‍ñā ratti nadī itthī, mātulānī ca bhikkhunī|

daṇḍinī yāgu mātā ca, jambū gautitthisaṅgahau||

itthiliṅgaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

napuṃsakaliṅga

atha  napuṃsakaliṅgāni vuc‍cantai|

akārantau napuṃsakaliṅgau cittasaddau|

purai viya syādyuppatti, ‘‘citta si’’ itīdha –

‘‘napuṃsakaihi, atau nic‍ca’’nti ca vattatai|

195. siṃ|

si, aṃiti dvipadamidaṃ| akārantaihi napuṃsakaliṅgaihi parassa sivacanassa aṃ hauti nic‍caṃ| saralaupapakatibhāvādi, cittaṃ|

bahuvacanai ‘‘yaunaṃ ni napuṃsakaihī’’ti vattatai|

196. atau nic‍caṃ|

akārantaihi napuṃsakaliṅgaihi yaunaṃ nic‍caṃ ni hauti| ‘‘sabbayaunīnamāai’’ti nissa vā ākārau| añ‍ñattha ‘‘yausu kata’’ic‍cādinā dīghau| cittā cittāni|

yaunaṃ nibhāvai cāaittai, siddhaipi avisaisatau|

‘‘atau nic‍ca’’nti ārambhā, āaittaṃ k‍vacidaividha||

ālapanai galaupau| hai citta, hai cittā cittāni, dutiyāyaṃ nissa vikappainaikārau| cittaṃ, cittai cittāni| saisaṃ purisasaddaina samaṃ|

aivamañ‍ñānipi –

puñ‍ña pāpa phala rūpa sādhanaṃ,

sauta ghāna sukha dukkha kāraṇaṃ|

dāna sīla dhana jhāna laucanaṃ,

mūla kūla bala jālamaṅgalaṃ||

naḷina  liṅga mukha’ṅga jala’mbujaṃ,

pulina dhañ‍ña hirañ‍ña padā’mataṃ|

paduma vaṇṇa susāna vanā’yudhaṃ,

hadaya cīvara vattha kuli’ndriyaṃ||

nayana vadana yānau’dāna saupāna pānaṃ,

bhavana bhuvana lauhā’lāta tuṇḍa’ṇḍa pīṭhaṃ|

karaṇa maraṇa ñāṇā’rammaṇā’rañ‍ña tāṇaṃ,

caraṇa nagara tīracchatta chiddau’dakāni –

ic‍cādīni|

kammasaddassa tatiyaikavacanādīsu rūpabhaidau|

kammaṃ, kammā kammāni, hai kamma, hai kammā kammāni, kammaṃ, kammai kammāni|

‘‘vā, u, nāmhi, cā’’ti ca vattatai|

197. a kammantassa ca|

kammasaddantassa ukāra akārādaisā haunti vā nāmhi vibhattimhi| antaggahaṇaina thāmaddhādīnamantassapi uttaṃ| casaddaggahaṇaina yuva maghavānamantassa ā hauti k‍vaci nā suic‍caitaisu| kammunā kammanā kammaina vā, kammaihi kammaibhi|

sasmāsu ‘‘u nāmhi cā’’ti aittha casaddaina puma kammathāmantassa cukārau vā sasmāsūti uttaṃ| kammunau kammassa, kammānaṃ, kammunā kammā kammamhā kammasmā, kammaihi kammaibhi, kammunau kammassa, kammānaṃ|

smiṃvacanai ‘‘brahmatau tu smiṃnī’’ti aittha tusaddaina k‍vaci ni hauti| kammani kammai kammamhi kammasmiṃ, kammaisu|

aivaṃ  thāmunā thāmaina thāmasā vā, thāmunau thāmassa, thāmunā thāmā| addhunā, addhunau ic‍cādi purimasamaṃ|

guṇavantu si, ‘‘savibhattissa, ntussa, simhī’’ti ca vattatai|

198. aṃ napuṃsakai|

napuṃsakai vattamānassa liṅgassa sambandhinau ntuppac‍cayassa savibhattissa aṃ hauti simhi vibhattimhi| guṇavaṃ cittaṃ|

yaumhi ‘‘ntussantau yausu cā’’ti attaṃ, ikārau ca| guṇavanti, guṇavantāni, saisaṃ ñaiyyaṃ|

gacchanta si, ‘‘simhi gacchantādīnaṃ ntasaddau a’’miti aṃ| gacchaṃ gacchantaṃ, gacchantā gacchantāni|

akārantaṃ|

ākārantau napuṃsakaliṅgau assaddhāsaddau|

‘‘assaddhā’’iti ṭhitai –

‘‘samāsassā’’ti adhikic‍ca ‘‘sarau rassau napuṃsakai’’ti samāsantassa rassattaṃ, samāsattā nāmabyapadaisau, syādyuppatti| saisaṃ cittasamaṃ|

assaddhaṃ kulaṃ, assaddhā assaddhāni kulāni ic‍cādi|

tathā mukhanāsikāsaddau| tassa dvandaikattā sabbatthaikavacanamaiva| mukhanāsikaṃ, hai mukhanāsika, mukhanāsikaṃ, mukhanāsikaina ic‍cādi|

ākārantaṃ|

ikārantau  napuṃsakaliṅgau aṭṭhisaddau|

syādyuppatti, silaupau, aṭṭhi|

‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

199. yaunaṃ ni napuṃsakaihi|

napuṃsakaliṅgaihi paraisaṃ sabbaisaṃ yaunaṃ ni hauti vā|

aṭṭhīni, añ‍ñattha nic‍caṃ yaulaupau, dīghau ca, aṭṭhī, tathā hai aṭṭhi, hai aṭṭhī aṭṭhīni, aṭṭhiṃ, aṭṭhī aṭṭhīni, aṭṭhinā ic‍cādi aggisaddasamaṃ|

aivaṃ satthi dadhi vāri akkhi acchi ac‍ci ic‍cādīni|

ikārantaṃ|

īkārantau napuṃsakaliṅgau sukhakārīsaddau|

‘‘sukhabhārī si’’ itīdha anapuṃsakattābhāvā sismimpi ‘‘aghau rassa’’mic‍cādinā rassattaṃ, silaupau| sukhakāri dānaṃ, sukhakārī sukhakārīni, hai sukhakāri, hai sukhakārī hai sukhakārīni, sukhakārinaṃ sukhakāriṃ, sukhakārī sukhakārīni|

saisaṃ daṇḍīsaddasamaṃ| aivaṃ sīghayāyīādīni|

īkārantaṃ|

ukārantau napuṃsakaliṅgau āyusaddau| tassa aṭṭhisaddassaiva rūpanayau|

āyu, āyū āyūni, hai āyu, hai āyū hai āyūni, āyuṃ, āyū āyūni, āyunā āyusāti  manaugaṇādittā siddhaṃ| āyūhi āyūbhi, āyunau āyussa, āyūnamic‍cādi|

aivaṃ cakkhu vasu dhanu dāru tipu madhu hiṅgu siggu vatthu matthu jatu ambu assuādīni|

ukārantaṃ|

ūkārantau napuṃsakaliṅgau gautrabhūsaddau|

gautrabhū si, napuṃsakattā rassattaṃ, silaupau| gautrabhu cittaṃ, gautrabhū gautrabhūni, hai gautrabhu, hai gautrabhū hai gautrabhūni, gautrabhuṃ, gautrabhū gautrabhūni, gautrabhunā ic‍cādi pul‍liṅgai abhibhūsaddasamaṃ|

aivaṃ abhibhū sayambhū dhammañ‍ñūādīni|

ūkārantaṃ|

aukārantau napuṃsakaliṅgau cittagausaddau|

‘‘cittā gāvau assa kulassā’’ti atthai bahubbīhisamāsai katai ‘‘sarau rassau napuṃsakai’’ti aukārassa ṭhānappayatanāsan‍nattā rassattamukārau, syādyuppatti, silaupau| cittagu kulaṃ, cittagū cittagūni ic‍cādi āyusaddasamaṃ|

aukārantaṃ|

cittaṃ kammañ‍ca assaddha-mathaṭṭhi sukhakāri ca|

āyu gautrabhū dhammañ‍ñū, cittagūti napuṃsakai||

napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

sabbanāma

atha  sabbanāmāni vuc‍cantai|

sabba, katara, katama, ubhaya, itara, añ‍ña, añ‍ñatara, añ‍ñatama, pubba, para, apara, dakkhiṇa, uttara, adhara, ya, ta, aita, ima, amu, kiṃ, aika, ubha, dvi, ti, catu, tumha, amha iti sattavīsati sabbanāmāni, tāni sabbanāmattā tiliṅgāni|

tattha sabbasaddau niravasaisatthau, sau yadā pul‍liṅgavisiṭṭhatthābhidhāyī, tadā rūpanayau| purai viya syādyuppatti, ‘‘sau’’ti sissa aukārau, saralaupaparanayanāni| sabbau janau|

bahuvacanai ‘‘sabba yau’’ itīdha ‘‘parasamañ‍ñā payaugai’’ti sabbādīnaṃ sabbanāmasañ‍ñā|

‘‘yau’’ti vattatai|

200. sabbanāmakāratai paṭhamau|

sabbaisaṃ itthipumanapuṃsakānaṃ nāmāni sabbanāmāni, taisaṃ sabbaisaṃ sabbanāmasañ‍ñānaṃ liṅgānaṃ akāratau parau paṭhamau yau aittamāpaj‍jatai| sabbai purisā|

akāratauti kiṃ? sabbā amū|

hai sabba sabbā, hai sabbai, sabbaṃ, sabbai, sabbaina, sabbaihi sabbaibhi|

catutthaikavacanai āyādaisai sampattai –

‘‘atau, ā ai, smāsmiṃnaṃ, āya catutthaikavacanassā’’ti ca vattatai|

201. tayau  naiva ca sabbanāmaihi|

akārantaihi sabbanāmaihi paraisaṃ smā smiṃ ic‍caitaisaṃ, catutthaikavacanassa ca ā ai āyaic‍caitai ādaisā naiva hauntīti āyādaisābhāvau| caggahaṇaṃ katthaci paṭisaidhanivattanatthaṃ, taina pubbādīhi smā smiṃnaṃ ā ai ca haunti| sabbassa|

‘‘akārau, ai’’ti ca vattatai|

202. sabbanāmānaṃ naṃmhi ca|

sabbaisaṃ sabbanāmānaṃ akārau aittamāpaj‍jatai naṃmhi vibhattimhi|

‘‘sabbanāmatau’’ti ca vattatai|

203. sabbatau naṃ saṃsānaṃ|

sabbatau sabbanāmatau parassa naṃvacanassa saṃ sānaṃic‍caitai ādaisā haunti|

sabbaisaṃ sabbaisānaṃ, sabbasmā sabbamhā, sabbaihi sabbaibhi, sabbassa, sabbaisaṃ sabbaisānaṃ, sabbasmiṃ sabbamhi, sabbaisu|

itthiyaṃ ‘‘itthiyamatau āppac‍cayau’’ti āppac‍cayau| añ‍ñaṃ kañ‍ñāsaddasamaṃ añ‍ñatra sa naṃ smiṃvacanaihi| sabbā pajā, sabbā sabbāyau, hai sabbai, hai sabbā sabbāyau, sabbaṃ, sabbā sabbāyau, sabbāya, sabbāhi sabbābhi|

catutthaikavacanai ‘‘sabbanāmatau vā’’, sabbatau kauti itau ‘‘sabbatau’’ti ca vattatai|

204. ghapatau  smiṃsānaṃ saṃsā|

sabbatau sabbanāmatau ghapasañ‍ñatau smiṃ saic‍caitaisaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ saṃsāādaisā haunti vā|

‘‘saṃsāsvaikavacanaisu cā’’ti vattatai|

205. ghau rassaṃ|

ghasañ‍ñau ākārau rassamāpaj‍jatai saṃsāsvaikavacanaisu vibhattādaisaisu paraisu|

‘‘sāgamau’’ti vattatai|

206. saṃsāsvaikavacanaisu ca|

saṃ sāic‍caitaisu aikavacanaṭṭhānasambhūtaisu vibhattādaisaisu paraisu liṅgamhā sakārāgamau hauti|

sabbassā sabbāya, sabbāsaṃ sabbāsānaṃ, sabbāya, sabbāhi sabbābhi, sabbassā sabbāya, sabbāsaṃ sabbāsānaṃ|

smiṃmhi ‘‘sabbanāmatau, ghapatau’’ti ca vattatai|

207. naitāhi smimāyayā|

aitaihi sabbanāmaihi ghapasañ‍ñaihi parassa smiṃvacanassa naiva āya yādaisā hauntīti āyābhāvau| vādhikāratau k‍vaci hauti dakkhiṇāya uttarāyāti ādi|

saṃyamādaisā, sabbassaṃ sabbāyaṃ, sabbāsu|

napuṃsakai sabbaṃ cittaṃ, sabbāni, hai sabba, hai sabbāni, sabbaṃ, sabbāni| saisaṃ pul‍liṅgai viya ñaiyyaṃ|

aivaṃ  katarādīnaṃ añ‍ñatamasaddapariyantānaṃ tīsupi liṅgaisu rūpanayau|

tattha katarakatamasaddā pucchanatthā|

ubhayasaddau dviavayavasamudāyavacanau|

itarasaddau vuttappaṭiyaugavacanau|

añ‍ñasaddau adhikatāparavacanau|

añ‍ñatara añ‍ñatamasaddā aniyamatthā|

‘‘yau, sabbanāmakāratai paṭhamau’’ti ca vattatai|

208. dvandaṭṭhā vā|

dvandasamāsaṭṭhā sabbanāmakāratau parau paṭhamau yau aittamāpaj‍jatai vā| katarau ca katamau cāti katarakatamai, katarakatamā vā ic‍cādi|

pubbādayau disādivavatthānavacanā|

pubbau kālau| bahuvacanai ‘‘dhātuliṅgaihi parā pac‍cayā’’ti aittha parāti niddaisatau pubbādīhi yauvacanassa vikappainaikārau|

pubbai pubbā, hai pubba, hai pubbai hai pubbā, pubbaṃ, pubbai, pubbaina, pubbaihi pubbaibhi, pubbassa, pubbaisaṃ pubbaisānaṃ| ‘‘smāsmiṃnaṃ vā’’ti vikappainākāraikārā| pubbā pubbasmā pubbamhā, pubbaihi pubbaibhi, pubbassa, pubbaisaṃ pubbaisānaṃ, pubbai pubbasmiṃ pubbamhi, pubbaisu|

itthiyaṃ pubbā disā, pubbā pubbāyau ic‍cādi sabbāsaddasamaṃ|

napuṃsakai  pubbaṃ ṭhānaṃ, pubbāni, hai pubba, hai pubbāni, pubbaṃ, pubbāni, saisaṃ pul‍liṅgasamaṃ| aivaṃ parāparadakkhiṇuttarādharasaddā|

‘‘sabbanāmatau, dvandaṭṭhā’’ti ca vattatai|

209. nāñ‍ñaṃ sabbanāmikaṃ|

dvandaṭṭhā sabbanāmatau parassa yauvacanassa ṭhapaitvā aittaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sabbanāmikaṃ kāriyaṃ na hautīti saṃsānamādaisābhāvau| pubbāparānaṃ, pubbuttarānaṃ adharuttarānaṃ, ‘‘nāñ‍ñaṃ sabbanāmika’’nti vinādhikāraina yaugaina tatiyāsamāsaipi| māsapubbāya, māsapubbānaṃ|

‘‘nāñ‍ñaṃ sabbanāmika’’nti ca vattatai|

210. bahubbīhimhi ca|

bahubbīhimhi ca samāsai sabbanāmikavidhānaṃ nāñ‍ñaṃ hauti| piyapubbāya, piyapubbānaṃ, piyapubbai|

casaddaggahaṇaina disatthasabbanāmānaṃ bahubbīhimhi sabbanāmikavidhānaṃva hauti|

dakkhiṇassā ca pubbassā ca yadantarāḷanti atthai bahubbīhi, dakkhiṇapubbassaṃ, dakkhiṇapubbassā, aivaṃ uttarapubbassaṃ, uttarapubbassā ic‍cādi|

yataitasaddādīnamālapanai rūpaṃ na sambhavati| yasaddau aniyamatthau|

yau purisau, yai purisā, yaṃ, yai| yā kañ‍ñā, yā yāyau, yaṃ, yā yāyau| yaṃ cittaṃ, yāni, yaṃ, yāni| saisaṃ sabbattha sabbasaddasamaṃ|

ta  aita ima amu kiṃic‍caitai parammukha samīpa ac‍cantasamīpadūra pucchanatthavacanā|

tasaddassa bhaidau| ‘‘ta si’’ itīdha –

‘‘anapuṃsakassāyaṃ simhīti, sa’’miti ca vattatai|

211. aitataisaṃ tau|

aita taic‍caitaisaṃ anapuṃsakānaṃ takārau sakāramāpaj‍jatai simhi vibhattimhi| sau purisau|

sabbanāmaggahaṇañ‍ca, itau taggahaṇañ‍ca vattatai|

212. tassa vā nattaṃ sabbattha|

taic‍caitassa sabbanāmassa takārassa nattaṃ hauti vā sabbattha liṅgaisu| nai tai, naṃ taṃ, nai tai, naina taina, naihi naibhi taihi taibhi|

‘‘sabbassa, tassa vā sabbatthā’’ti ca vattatai|

213. sasmāsmiṃsaṃsāsvattaṃ|

taic‍caitassa sabbanāmassa sabbassaiva attaṃ hauti vā sa smā smiṃ saṃ sāic‍caitaisu vacanaisu sabbattha liṅgaisu| assa nassa tassa, naisaṃ taisaṃ naisānaṃ taisānaṃ|

‘‘smāhismiṃnaṃ mhābhimhi vā’’ti itau ‘‘smāsmiṃnaṃ, mhāmhī’’ti ca vattatai|

214. na timaihi katākāraihi|

ta imaic‍caitaihi katākāraihi paraisaṃ smāsmiṃnaṃ mhā mhiic‍caitai ādaisā na haunti|

asmā  nasmā tasmā namhā tamhā, naihi naibhi taihi taibhi, assa nassa tassa, naisaṃ taisaṃ naisānaṃ taisānaṃ, asmiṃ nasmiṃ tasmiṃ namhi tamhi, naisu taisu|

itthiyaṃ ‘‘tā si’’ itīdha sādaisasilaupā| sā kañ‍ñā, nattaṃ| nā tā nāyau tāyau, naṃ taṃ, nā tā nāyau tāyau, nāya tāya, nāhi tāhi nābhi tābhi|

‘‘aitimāsamī’’ti itau aitimāggahaṇañ‍ca ‘‘tassā vā’’ti itau taggahaṇañ‍ca pañ‍camiyantavasaina vattatai ‘‘vā’’ti ca|

215. tatau sassa ssāya|

tatau tā aitā imātau parassa sassa vibhattissa ssāyādaisau hauti vā|

‘‘saṃsāsvaikavacanaisu ca, i’’iti ca vattatai|

216. tassā vā|

tāic‍caitassa itthiyaṃ vattamānassa antassa ikārau hauti vā saṃ sāsvaikavacanaisu vibhattādaisaisu| tissāya tassāya assāya nassāya assā nassā tissā tassā nāya tāya, nāsaṃ tāsaṃ|

pañ‍camīchaṭṭhīsu tatiyācatutthīsamaṃ| sattamiyaṃ assaṃ nassaṃ tissaṃ tassaṃ nāyaṃ tāyaṃ, nāsu tāsu|

napuṃsakai simhisādaisābhāvā nattaṃ| naṃ taṃ, nāni tāni, naṃ taṃ, nāni tāni, naina taina ic‍cādi pul‍liṅgasamaṃ|

aita si, ‘‘aitataisaṃ tau’’ti sakārādaisau| aisau purisau, aitai, aitaṃ, aitai ic‍cādi sabbasaddasamaṃ|

sa smiṃsu pana ‘‘saṃsāsvaikavacanaisu cā’’ti vattatai|

217. aitimāsami|

antāpaikkhāyaṃ chaṭṭhī, aitā imāic‍caitaisamantau sarau ikārau hauti saṃsāsvaikavacanaisu vibhattādaisaisu| sādaisagatikattā ssāyādaisaipi| casaddādhikāratau añ‍ñaikāsaddādīnamantassa ca|

aitissāya aitissā aitāya, aitāsaṃ aitāsānaṃ, aitāya, aitāhi aitābhi, aitissāya aitissā aitāya, aitāsaṃ aitāsānaṃ, aitissaṃ aitāyaṃ, aitāsu|

casaddatau añ‍ñissā añ‍ñāya, añ‍ñissaṃ añ‍ñāyaṃ| aikissā aikāya, aikissaṃ aikāyaṃ| itarissā itarāya, itarissaṃ itarāyaṃ ic‍cādi|

napuṃsakai aitaṃ, aitāni, aitaṃ, aitāni, saisaṃ ñaiyyaṃ|

‘‘sabbassimassā’’ti vattatai|

218. anapuṃsakassāyaṃ simhi|

219. animi  nāmhi ca|

imasaddassa sabbassaiva ana imiādaisā haunti nāmhi vibhattimhi| anitthiliṅgassaivaitaṃ gahaṇaṃ| anaina iminā|

‘‘sunaṃhisū’’ti vattatai|

220. sabbassimassai vā|

sabbassa imasaddassa aikārau hauti vā su naṃ hiic‍caitaisu vacanaisu|

āppac‍cayantāniddaisā, sabbatthāti avuttatau|

anitthiliṅgassaivaittha, gahaṇañhi imassiti||

aihi aibhi imaihi imaibhi|

‘‘sabbassa, vā, sabbattha, sasmāsmiṃsaṃsāsvatta’’nti ca vattatai|

221. imasaddassa ca|

imasaddassa ca sabbassaiva attaṃ hauti vā sa smā smiṃsaṃ sāic‍caitaisu vacanaisu sabbattha liṅgaisu|

assa imassa, aisaṃ aisānaṃ imaisaṃ imaisānaṃ, asmā imasmā imamhā, aihi aibhi imaihi imaibhi, assa imassa, aisaṃ aisānaṃ imaisaṃ imaisānaṃ, asmiṃ imasmiṃ imamhi, aisu imaisu|

itthiyaṃ imā si, ayamādaisasilaupā|

ayaṃ kañ‍ñā, imā imāyau, imaṃ, imā imāyau, imāya, imāhi imābhi| catutthiyaṃ attaṃ, ikāra- ssāyādaisā  ca, assāya imissāya assā imissā imāya, imāsaṃ imāsānaṃ| sattamiyaṃ assaṃ imissaṃ imissā vā, ‘‘taisu vuddhilaupā’’dinā smiṃvacanassa vā sādaisau| imāyaṃ, imāsu| saisaṃ ñaiyyaṃ|

napuṃsakai ima si, ‘‘savibhattissa, vā’’ti ca vattatai|

222. imassidamaṃsisu na puṃsakai|

napuṃsakai vattamānassa sabbassaiva imasaddassa savibhattissa idaṃ hauti vā aṃ sisu paraisu|

idaṃ cittaṃ viraucati, imaṃ, imāni, idaṃ pupphaṃ passasi, imaṃ, imāni, anaina iminā, aihi aibhi imaihi imaibhi ic‍cādi pul‍liṅgai viya ñaiyyaṃ|

amusaddassa bhaidau| amu si –

‘‘vā, anapuṃsakassa, simhī’’ti ca vattatai|

223. amussa mau saṃ|

anapuṃsakassa amusaddassa makārau sakāramāpaj‍jatai vā simhi parai| asu rājā|

‘‘sabbanāmatau, vā’’ti ca vattatai|

224. sabbatau kau|

sabbatau sabbanāmatau parau kaic‍cayamāgamau hauti vā| puna sabbatauggahaṇaina hīnāditaupi kau| ‘‘amussa mau sa’’nti vinādhikāraina yaugaina kakāraipi sādaisau|

amū purisā, amuṃ, amū, amunā, amūhi amūbhi amuhi amubhi, amussa| ‘‘amussādu’’nti vinādhikāraina yaugaina aduṃādaisau, adussa, amūsaṃ amūsānaṃ amusaṃ amusānaṃ, amusmā amumhā, amūhi amūbhi amuhi amubhi, amussa adussa, amūsaṃ amūsānaṃ amusaṃ amusānaṃ, amusmiṃ amumhi, amūsu amusu|

itthiyaṃ simhi sādaisādi|

asu kañ‍ñā asukā amukā vā, amū amuyau, amuṃ, amū amuyau, amuyā, amūhi amūbhi, amussā amuyā, amūsaṃ amūsānaṃ, amuyā, amūhi amūbhi, amussā amuyā, amūsaṃ amūsānaṃ, amussaṃ amuyaṃ amuyā, amūsu|

napuṃsakai amusi| ‘‘savibhattissa’’, imassidamic‍cāditau ‘‘aṃsisu napuṃsakai’’ti ca vattatai|

225. amussāduṃ|

napuṃsakai vattamānassa sabbassaiva amusaddassa savibhattissa aduṃ hauti aṃsisu paraisu| aduṃ pupphaṃ, amū amūni, aduṃ, amū amūni, amunā ic‍cādi pul‍liṅgasamaṃ|

kiṃsaddassa bhaidau| ‘‘kiṃ si’’ itīdha –

‘‘kissa  ka vai cā’’ti itau ‘‘kissa, ka’’iti ca vattatai|

226. saisaisu ca|

kimic‍caitassa kasaddau ādaisau hauti vappac‍cayatau saisaisu vibhattibhaidaisu| aittha ca ‘‘kissa ka vai cā’’ti suttai casaddaina vappac‍cayāvasiṭṭha thamādippac‍cayānaṃ gahitattā saisaggahaṇaina vibhattiyauva gayhantai| caggahaṇaṃ katthaci nivattanatthaṃ, taina ‘‘kissa, kismi’’ntiādi ca sijjhati| ‘‘sau’’ti sissa au, saralaupādi|

kau aisau, kai, kaṃ, kai, kaina, kaihi kaibhi, kassa kissa, niggahītalaupādi, kaisaṃ kaisānaṃ, kasmā kamhā, kaihi kaibhi, kassa kissa, kaisaṃ kaisānaṃ, kasmiṃ kismiṃ kamhi kimhi, kaisu|

itthiyaṃ ‘‘kiṃ si’’itīdha ‘‘saisaisu cā’’ti vibhattiyaṃ parāyaṃ kādaisai katai ‘‘itthiyamatau āppac‍cayau’’ti majjhai āppac‍cayau, silaupau|

kā aisā kañ‍ñā, kā kāyau, kaṃ, kā kāyau ic‍cādi sabbāsaddasamaṃ|

napuṃsakai kiṃ si, laupavidhissa balavatarattā paṭhamaṃ silaupai katai puna vibhattiparattābhāvā, ‘‘tadanuparaudhainā’’ti paribhāsatau vā kādaisābhāvau| kiṃ aitaṃ, kāni|

dutiyaikavacanai ‘‘k‍vaci laupa’’nti niggahītalaupai katai ‘‘aṃmau niggahītaṃ jhalapaihī’’ti niggahītaṃ| kiṃ, kāni ic‍cādi pul‍liṅgasamaṃ|

aikasaddau  saṅkhyātulyāsahāyañ‍ñavacanau| yadā saṅkhyāvacanau, tadā sabbatthaikavacanantauva, añ‍ñattha bahuvacanantaupi| aikau, aikā, aikaṃ ic‍cādi sabbattha sabbasaddasamaṃ| saṃsāsvaiva visaisau|

ubhasaddau dvisaddapariyāyau, sadā bahuvacanantauva|

‘‘ubha yau’’ itīdha ‘‘tatau yaunamau tū’’ti aittha tuggahaṇaina k‍vaci yaunamaukārau| ubhau purisāubhai vā, ubhau purisai ubhai| su hisu ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā k‍vaci aikārassaukārau| ubhauhi ubhaubhi ubhaihi ubhaibhi|

227. ubhāditau namin‍naṃ|

ubhaic‍caivamāditau naṃvacanassa in‍naṃ hauti|

ubhin‍naṃ, ubhauhi ubhaubhi ubhaihi ubhaibhi, ubhin‍naṃ, ubhausu ubhaisu|

dviādayau saṅkhyāsaṅkhyaiyyavacanā, bahūnaṃ vācitattā sadā bahuvacanantāva|

‘‘dvi yau’’itīdha ‘‘savibhattissa, itthipumanapuṃsakasaṅkhya’’nti ca adhikārau|

228. yausu dvin‍naṃ dvai ca|

dviic‍caitassa saṅkhyāsaddassa itthipumanapuṃsakai vattamānassa savibhattissa dvaiic‍cādaisau hauti yausu paraisu| casaddaina duvai ca, k‍vaci duvi ca naṃmhi| bahuvacanuc‍cāraṇaṃ dvisaddatau  bahuvacanamaiva hautīti ñāpanatthaṃ| dvai dhammā, dvai itthiyau, dvai rūpāni, duvai vā, aivaṃ dutiyāyampi, dvīhi dvībhi|

naṃmhi dīghai sampattai –

229. nau ca dvāditau naṃmhi|

dviic‍caivamāditau saṅkhyātau nakārāgamau hauti naṃmhi vibhattimhi| casaddaggahaṇaina itthiyaṃ ti ca tusaddatau ssañ‍cāgamau naṃmhi vibhattimhi| dvin‍naṃ duvin‍naṃ vā, dvīhi dvībhi, dvin‍naṃ duvin‍naṃ, dvīsu|

tisaddassa bhaidau| ‘‘ti yau’’itīdha

yaulaupai sampattai

230. ti catun‍naṃ tissau catassau tayaucattārautīṇi cattāri|

ti catun‍naṃ saṅkhyānaṃ itthipumanapuṃsakai vattamānānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ tissau catassau tayau cattārautīṇi cattāriic‍caitai ādaisā haunti yausu paraisu| tayau purisā, tayau purisai passa, tīhi tībhi|

‘‘na’’miti vattatai|

231. iṇṇamiṇṇan‍naṃ tīhi saṅkhyāhi|

tiic‍caitasmā saṅkhyāsaddā parassa naṃvacanassa iṇṇaṃ iṇṇan‍naṃic‍caitai ādaisā haunti, saralaupādi| tiṇṇaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ, tīhi tībhi, tiṇṇaṃ tiṇṇan‍naṃ, tīsu|

itthiyaṃ  tissau itthiyau, tissau, tīhi tībhi, naṃmhi ssañ‍cāgamau, tissan‍naṃ, ssaṃbyavadhānatau iṇṇābhāvau, saisaṃ samaṃ|

napuṃsakai tīṇi, tīṇi| saisaṃ pul‍liṅgasamaṃ|

tathā catusaddassapi yausu ‘‘ticatun‍na’’ntiādinā yathāvuttādaisau, ‘‘tatau yaunamau tū’’ti aittha tusaddaina k‍vaci aukārau ca| cattārau caturau vā, cattārau caturau, catūhi catūbhi catubbhi, catun‍naṃ, nakārāgamau| catūhi catūbhi catubbhi, catun‍naṃ, catūsu|

itthiyaṃ catassau, catassau, naṃmhi ssañ‍cāgamau, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā caturukārassa akārau| catassan‍naṃ| saisaṃ samaṃ|

napuṃsakai cattāri, cattāri| saisaṃ pul‍liṅgasamaṃ|

tathā –

nīlādiguṇanāmañ‍ca, bahubbīhi ca taddhitaṃ|

sāmañ‍ñavutyatītādi-kitantaṃ vāc‍caliṅgikaṃ||

aitthaidaṃ vuc‍catai –

aisai’sau aitamiti ca,

pasiddhi atthaisu yaisu laukassa|

thīpun‍napuṃsakāniti,

vuc‍cantai tāni nāmāni||

tiliṅgaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

aliṅganāma

athāliṅgaisu  nāmaisu tumhamhasaddā vuc‍cantai|

taisaṃ panāliṅgattā tīsu liṅgaisu samānarūpaṃ| ‘‘tumhamha’’iti ṭhitai syādyuppatti|

‘‘savibhattīnaṃ, tumhamhāka’’nti adhikārau|

232. tvamahaṃ simhi ca|

sabbaisaṃ tumhaamhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ tvaṃahaṃic‍caitai ādaisā haunti simhi vibhattimhi| casaddaina tumhassa tuvañ‍ca hauti| tvaṃ pumā, tvaṃ itthī, tvaṃ napuṃsakaṃ, tuvaṃ satthā vā| ahaṃ pumā, ahaṃ itthī, ahaṃ napuṃsakaṃ|

bahuvacanai ‘‘sabbanāmakāratai paṭhamau’’ti aikārau| tumhai tiṭṭhatha, bhiyyau amhai mahaimasai|

‘‘amhassā’’ti vattatai|

233. mayaṃ yaumhi paṭhamai|

sabbassa amhasaddassa savibhattissa mayaṃādaisau hauti yaumhi paṭhamai| mayaṃ gacchāma|

aittha ca aikasmimpi gāravabahumānaina bahuttasamāraupā bahuvacanaṃ hauti|

234. tavaṃ mamañ‍ca navā|

sabbaisaṃ tumha amhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ tavaṃ mamaṃic‍caitai ādaisā haunti navā yathāk‍kamaṃ aṃmhi vibhattimhi| tavaṃ, mamaṃ passa|

235. taṃ  mamaṃmhi|

sabbaisaṃ tumha amhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ taṃ maṃic‍caitai ādaisā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ aṃmhi vibhattimhi| taṃ, maṃ|

236. tumhassa tuvaṃ tvamaṃmhi|

sabbassa tumhasaddassa savibhattissa tuvaṃtvaṃic‍caitai ādaisā haunti aṃmhi vibhattimhi| tuvaṃ tvaṃ|

237. vā yvappaṭhamau|

tumhamhaihi parau appaṭhamau yau ākaṃ hauti vā| tumhākaṃ passāmi, tumhai passāmi, amhākaṃ passasi, amhai passasi|

238. nāmhi tayā mayā|

sabbaisaṃ tumha amhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ tayā mayāic‍caitai ādaisā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ nāmhi vibhattimhi|

239. tayātayīnaṃ takārau tvattaṃ vā|

tayā tayiic‍caitaisaṃ takārau tvattamāpaj‍jatai vā| tvayā tayā, mayā, tumhaihi tumhaibhi, amhaihi amhaibhi|

240. sassaṃ|

tumhamhaihi sassa vibhattissa amādaisau hauti vā| tumhaṃ, amhaṃ dīyatai|

241. tava  mama sai|

sabbaisaṃ tumha amhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ tavamamaic‍caitai ādaisā haunti sai vibhattimhi, vikappaināyaṃ vijjhantarassa vij‍jamānattā|

‘‘sai’’ti vattatai|

242. tuyhaṃ mayhañ‍ca|

sabbaisaṃ tumha amhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ tuyhaṃmayhaṃic‍caitai ādaisā ca haunti sai vibhattimhi| tava, mama tuyhaṃ, mayhaṃ vā dīyatai|

243. amhassa mamaṃ savibhattissa sai|

sabbassaiva amhasaddassa savibhattissa mamaṃādaisau hauti sai vibhattimhi| mamaṃ dīyatai|

‘‘sassa’’nti itau sīhagatiyā ‘‘a’’miti vattatai|

244. tumhamhaihi namākaṃ|

tumhamhaihi parassa naṃvacanassa ākamic‍cādaisau hauti, añ‍ca| ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā amhassa k‍vaci asmādaisau| tumhaṃ tumhākaṃ, amhaṃ amhākaṃ asmākaṃ vā|

pañ‍camiyaṃ ‘‘amhatumhanturāja’’ic‍cādinā smāvacanassa nābhāvātidaisau| tayā, mayā apaiti, tumhaihi, amhaihi tumhaibhi amhaibhi, tumhaṃ amhaṃ tava mama, tuyhaṃ, mayhaṃ mamaṃ pariggahau, tumhaṃ tumhākaṃ, amhaṃ amhākaṃ asmākaṃ dhammatā|

‘‘smiṃmhī’’ti vattatai|

245. tumhamhākaṃ  tayi mayi|

sabbaisaṃ tumha amhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ tayi mayiic‍caitai ādaisā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ smiṃmhi vibhattimhi| takārassa tvattaṃ| tvayi tayi mayi, tumhaisu amhaisu|

taisaṃ aiva tumha amhasaddānaṃ padatau paraisaṃ k‍vaci ādaisantaravidhānai rūpabhaidau|

‘‘navā’’ti adhikārau|

246. padatau dutiyācatutthīchaṭṭhīsu vau nau|

sabbaisaṃ tumha amhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ padasmā paraisaṃ vau nauādaisā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ dutiyācatutthīchaṭṭhīsu bahuvacanaisu navā| aittha ca ‘‘aikavacanaisū’’ti vakkhamānattā ‘‘bahuvacanaisū’’ti laddhaṃ| pahāya vau gamissāmi, mā nau aj‍ja vikantiṃsu, dhammaṃ vau bhikkhavai daisissāmi, saṃvibhajaitha nau raj‍jaina, tuṭṭhausmi vau pakatiyā, satthā nau bhagavā anuppattau|

navāti kiṃ? bhayaṃ tumhāka nau siyā, aisau amhākaṃ satthā|

‘‘padatau, catutthīchaṭṭhīsū’’ti vattatai|

247. taimaikavacanaisu ca|

sabbaisaṃ tumha amhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ padasmā paraisaṃ tai maiādaisā haunti yathāk‍kamaṃ catutthīchaṭṭhīsu aikavacanaisu  navā| dadāmi tai gāmavarāni pañ‍ca, dadāhi mai gāmavaraṃ, idaṃ tai raṭṭhaṃ, ayaṃ mai puttau|

navāti kiṃ? idaṃ cīvaraṃ tuyhaṃ vikappanatthāya dammi, suṇātha vacanaṃ mama|

navādhikāratau caittha,

vau nau tai maiti yai imai|

pādādau ca ca vā aivā-

diyaugai ca na haunti tai||

yathā –

na saucāmi na raudāmi, tava sutvāna māṇava|

tuyhañ‍cāpi mahārāja, mayhañ‍ca raṭṭhavaḍḍhana||

aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañ‍caiva tumhākañ‍cāti|

padatauti kiṃ? tava ñāti, mama ñāti|

‘‘tai mai’’ti vattatai|

248. na aṃmhi|

sabbaisaṃ tumha amhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ padasmā paraisaṃ tai maiādaisā na haunti aṃmhi vibhattimhi| passaiyya taṃ vassasataṃ āraugyaṃ| sau mamabravīti|

‘‘taimaikavacanai’’ti vattatai|

249. vā tatiyai ca|

sabbaisaṃ tumha amhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ padasmā paraisaṃ tai mai ādaisā haunti vā yathāk‍kamaṃ tatiyaikavacanai parai| kataṃ tai pāpaṃ, kataṃ tayā pāpaṃ, kataṃ mai puñ‍ñaṃ, kataṃ mayā puñ‍ñaṃ|

‘‘vā, tatiyai’’ti ca vattatai|

250. bahuvacanaisu vau nau|

sabbaisaṃ tumha amhasaddānaṃ savibhattīnaṃ padasmā paraisaṃ vau nauādaisā haunti vā yathāk‍kamaṃ tatiyābahuvacanai parai| kataṃ vau kammaṃ, kataṃ nau kammaṃ|

bahuvacananiddaisaina k‍vaci yaumhi paṭhamai ca vau nau haunti| gāmaṃ vau gacchaiyyātha, gāmaṃ nau gacchaiyyāma|

tathā pañ‍cādīnamaṭṭhārasantānaṃ, katisaddassa cāliṅgattā tiliṅgaipi samānarūpaṃ, aliṅgattā aiva pañ‍cāditau itthippac‍cayābhāvau|

‘‘pañ‍ca yau’’itīdha –

‘‘yausu dvin‍naṃ dvai cā’’ti itau ‘‘yausū’’ti vattatai, ‘‘itthipumanapuṃsakasaṅkhya’’nti ca|

251. pañ‍cādīnamakārau|

antāpaikkhāyaṃ chaṭṭhī, pañ‍cādīnaṃ aṭṭhārasantānaṃ saṅkhyānaṃ itthipumanapuṃsakai vattamānānamantassa savibhattissa akārau hauti yausu paraisu| ā aiādaisāpavādauyaṃ, pañ‍cakkhandhā, pañ‍ca gatiyau, pañ‍ca indriyāni| aivaṃ dutiyāyañ‍ca|

‘‘sunaṃhisū’’ti vattatai|

252. pañ‍cādīnamattaṃ|

pañ‍cādīnamaṭṭhārasantānaṃ saṅkhyānamantau attamāpaj‍jatai su naṃ hiic‍caitaisu paraisu| aittadīghāpavādauyaṃ| pañ‍cahi pañ‍cabhi, pañ‍can‍naṃ, pañ‍cahi pañ‍cabhi, pañ‍can‍naṃ, pañ‍casu| aivaṃ cha sa tta aṭṭhanava dasasaddā|

‘‘aikañ‍ca dasa cā’’ti atthai dvandasamāsai, ‘‘aikaina adhikā dasā’’ti atthai tappurisai vā katai

‘‘saṅkhyānai’’ti vattatai|

253. dvaikaṭṭhānamākārau vā|

dviaikaaṭṭhaic‍caitaisamantau ākārau hauti vā saṅkhyānai uttarapadai parai| vavatthitavibhāsāyaṃ| aikādasa, dvādasa, aṭṭhārasa|

saṅkhyānaiti kimatthaṃ? aikadantau, dvidantau, aṭṭhatthambhau|

‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

254. aikāditau dassa ra saṅkhyānai|

aikāditau saṅkhyātau parassa dasassa ādissa dassa rakārau hauti vā saṅkhyānai| saisaṃ samaṃ| aikārasa, aikādasa|

dvai ca dasa ca, dvīhi vā adhikā dasāti dvidasa itīdha –

‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

255. vīsatidasaisu bā dvissa tu|

vīsati dasaic‍caitaisu paraisu dvisaddassa bā hauti vā| tusaddaina tiṃsāyampi| rakārau, āttañ‍ca| bārasa, dvādasa|

tayau ca dasa ca, tīhi vā adhikā dasāti tairasa| aittha ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā tisaddassa taiādaisau ānavutiyā|

cattārau  ca dasa ca, catūhi vā adhikā dasāti catuddasa ic‍catra –

‘‘gaṇanai, dasassā’’ti ca vattatai|

256 . catūpapadassa laupau tuttarapadādi cassa cu caupi navā|

gaṇanai dasassādimhi ṭhitassa catuic‍caitassa upapadassa tusaddau laupau hauti, uttarapadādimhi ṭhitassa catūpapadassa cakārassa cu cauādaisā haunti navā| cuddasa, cauddasa, catuddasa|

apiggahaṇaina anupapadassāpi gaṇanai padādicakārassa laupau, cu cau haunti navā| yathā – tālīsaṃ, cuttālīsaṃ, cauttālīsaṃ, cattālīsaṃ|

pañ‍ca ca dasa ca, pañ‍cahi vā adhikā dasāti atthai pañ‍cadasa| ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā pañ‍casaddassa dasa vīsatīsu k‍vaci pan‍napaṇṇaādaisā| pan‍narasa, paṇṇarasa|

cha ca dasa ca, chahi vā adhikā dasāti atthai chadasa itīdha –

‘‘chassā’’ti vattatai|

257. dasai sau nic‍cañ‍ca|

chaic‍caitassa saṅkhyāsaddassa nic‍caṃ sau hauti dasai parai|

‘‘saṅkhyānaṃ, vā’’ti ca vattatai|

258. la darānaṃ|

saṅkhyānaṃ dakārarakārānaṃ lakārādaisau hauti vā|

laḷānamavisaisau| vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau, taina ‘‘sauḷasa’’ iti nic‍caṃ, ‘‘taiḷasa, tairasa, cattālīsaṃ, cattārīsa’’miti vibhāsā, dasa pan‍narasādīsu na ca hauti|

satta ca dasa ca, sattahi vā adhikā dasāti atthai sattarasa, sattadasa|

aṭṭha ca dasa ca, aṭṭhahi vā adhikā dasāti atthai aṭṭhadasa itīdha āttai katai –

‘‘vā, dassa, ra, saṅkhyānai’’ti ca vattatai|

259. aṭṭhāditau ca|

aṭṭhāditau ca dasasaddassa dakārassa rakārau hauti vā saṅkhyānai| aṭṭhārasa, aṭṭhādasa|

aṭṭhāditauti kiṃ? catuddasa|

katisaddau bahuvacanantauva, ‘‘kati yau’’ itīdha

nic‍caṃ yaulaupādi, rassattaṃ, kati tiṭṭhanti, kati passasi, katihi katibhi, katinaṃ, katihi katibhi, katinaṃ, katisu|

aliṅganāmaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

vibhattippac‍cayavidhāna

atha vibhattippac‍cayantā vuc‍cantai|

taisaṃ panāliṅgattā, nipātattā ca tiliṅgai, vacanadvayai ca samānaṃ rūpaṃ|

purisasmā, purisaihi vāti atthai –

260. k‍vaci  tau pañ‍camyatthai|

sabbasmā suddhanāmatau, sabbanāmatau ca liṅgamhā k‍vaci taupac‍cayau hauti pañ‍camyatthai|

261. tvādayau vibhattisañ‍ñā|

tauādi yaisaṃ dānipariyantānaṃ pac‍cayānaṃ tai haunti pac‍cayā tvādayau, tai pac‍cayā vibhattisañ‍ñā haunti| taina tadantānampi vibhatyantapadattaṃ siddhaṃ hauti|

purisatau, aivaṃ rājatau vā, cauratau vā, aggitau vā, gahapatitau vā, hatthitau, haitutau, sabbañ‍ñutau, kañ‍ñatau, yuttitau, itthitau, bhikkhunitau, aittha ca ‘‘k‍vacādimajjhuttarānaṃ dīgharassāpac‍cayaisu cā’’ti taupac‍cayai rassattaṃ| yāgutau, jambutau, cittatau, āyutau ic‍cādi|

sabbanāmatau sabbasmā, sabbaihīti vā atthai sabbatau, aivaṃ yatau, tatau, kataratau, katamatau, itaratau, añ‍ñatau, aikatau, ubhayatau, pubbatau, paratau, aparatau, dakkhiṇatau, uttaratau, amutau|

‘‘kissa, ku’’iti ca vattatai|

262. tratauthaisu ca|

kimic‍caitassa ku hauti tratau thaic‍caitaisu ca paraisu| kasmā, kaihīti vā kutau|

‘‘tauthaisū’’ti vattatai|

263. sabbassaitassākārau  vā|

sabbassa aitasaddassa akārau hauti vā tauthaic‍caitaisu| aitasmā, aitaihīti vā atau|

‘‘sabbassaitassā’’ti ca vattatai|

264. ai tauthaisu ca|

sabbassa aitasaddassa aikārau hauti vā tauthaic‍caitaisu| dvittaṃ, aittau|

‘‘sabbassā’’ti vattatai|

265. imassi thaṃdānihataudhaisu ca|

imasaddassa sabbassaiva ikārau hauti thaṃdānihataudhaic‍caitaisu ca| imasmāti itau|

‘‘k‍vaci tau’’ti yaugavibhāgaina ādippabhutīhi tau sattamiyatthai| anic‍cādīhi tatiyatthai ca| yathā – ādimhībhi atthai āditau| aivaṃ majjhatau, aikatau, puratau, pacchatau, passatau, piṭṭhitau, pādatau, sīsatau, aggatau, mūlatau, paratau ic‍cādayau|

tatiyatthai anic‍caināti anic‍catau, anic‍catau sammasati| aivaṃ dukkhatau, raugatau, gaṇḍatau ic‍cādayau|

‘‘atthai, k‍vacī’’ti ca vattatai|

266. tratha sattamiyā sabbanāmaihi|

sabbanāmaihi parā tra thaic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti k‍vaci sattamyatthai| sabbasmiṃ, sabbaisu cāti sabbatra sabbattha| aivaṃ  yatra yattha, tatra tattha, itaratra itarattha, añ‍ñatra añ‍ñattha, ubhayatra ubhayattha, paratra parattha, kutra kuttha, ‘‘tratauthaisu cā’’ti kuttaṃ| ‘‘kissa ka vai cā’’ti suttai casaddaina kādaisau| kattha, amutra amuttha|

‘‘sabbassaitassākārau’’ti vattatai|

267. trai nic‍caṃ|

sabbassaiva aitasaddassa akārau hauti nic‍caṃ trai parai| atra| ‘‘sabbassaitassākārau vā’’ti attaṃ, ‘‘ai tauthaisu cā’’ti aikārau, attha, aittha|

‘‘k‍vaci, atthai, sattamiyā’’ti ca adhikārau, sabbasminti atthai –

268. sabbatau dhi|

sabbaic‍caitasmā dhippac‍cayau hauti k‍vaci sattamyatthai| sabbadhi|

269. kismā vau|

kimic‍caitasmā vappac‍cayau hauti k‍vaci sattamyatthai|

270. kissa ka vai ca|

kimic‍caitassa kasaddau ādaisau hauti vappac‍cayaiparai| caggahaṇaina thahamādiavappac‍cayaipi| ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā kakārai akārassa laupau ca vamhi| k‍va gatausi tvaṃ|

‘‘kismā’’ti vattatai|

271. hiṃhaṃhiñ‍canaṃ|

kimic‍caitasmā hiṃ haṃ hiñ‍canaṃic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti k‍vaci sattamyatthai|

‘‘kissā’’ti vattatai|

272. ku hiṃhaṃsu ca|

kimic‍caitassa ku hauti hiṃ haṃ ic‍caitaisu| caggahaṇaina hiñ‍canaṃdācanamic‍cādīsupi| kisminti kuhiṃ, kuhaṃ, kuhiñ‍canaṃ, kahaṃ kādaisau|

‘‘hiṃ ha’’nti vattatai|

273. tamhā ca|

taic‍caitasmā ca sabbanāmatau hiṃhaṃpac‍cayā haunti k‍vaci sattamyatthai| tasminti tahiṃ, tahaṃ|

274. imasmā hadhā ca|

imasaddatau hadhappac‍cayā haunti k‍vaci sattamyatthai| ‘‘imassi tha’’ntiādinā ikārau| imasminti iha, idha|

275. yatau hiṃ|

yaic‍caitasmā sabbanāmatau hiṃpac‍cayau hauti k‍vaci sattamyatthai| yasminti yahiṃ|

‘‘kālai’’ti adhikārauyaṃ|

kasmiṃ kālaiti atthai

276. kiṃsabbañ‍ñaikayakuhi  dā dācanaṃ|

kiṃ sabbaañ‍ñaaikayaic‍caitaihi sabbanāmaihi dāpac‍cayau hauti| kuic‍caitasmā dācanañ‍ca kālai k‍vaci sattamyatthai|

‘‘kissa ka vai cā’’ti suttai casaddaina kādaisau, kadā|

277. sabbassa sau dāmhi vā|

sabbaic‍caitassa saddassa sasaddādaisau hauti vā dāpac‍cayai parai| sabbasmiṃ kālaiti sadā, sabbadā|

aivaṃ añ‍ñadā, aikadā, yadā, kasmiṃ kālaiti kudācanaṃ, ‘‘ku hiṃhaṃsu cā’’ti suttai casaddaina kuttaṃ, ‘‘ku’’iti nipātanaina vā|

‘‘dā’’ti vattatai|

278. tamhā dāni ca|

taic‍caitasmā sabbanāmatau dānidāpac‍cayā haunti kālai k‍vaci sattamyatthai| tasmiṃ kālaiti tadāni, tadā|

279. imasmā rahidhunādāni ca|

imasaddatau rahi dhunā dāniic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti kālai k‍vaci sattamyatthai|

‘‘sabbassa, imassā’’ti ca vattatai|

280. aita  rahimhi|

sabbassa imasaddassa aitādaisau hauti rahimhi pac‍cayai parai| imasmiṃ kālaiti aitarahi|

281. a dhunāmhi ca|

sabbassaiva imasaddassa akārau hauti dhunāpac‍cayai parai| adhunā, imasaddassa ikārau| imasmiṃ kālaiti idāni|

vibhattippac‍cayavidhānaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

aupasaggikapada

athāliṅgasaṅkhyāvibhattibhaidā upasagganipātā vuc‍cantai|

pa parā ni nī u du saṃ vi ava anu pari adhi abhi pati su ā ati api apa upa iti pīsati upasaggā|

tattha pasaddau pakārā’dikamma padhāni’ssariya’ntaubhāvaviyauga tappara bhusattha sambhavatitti anāvila patthanādīsu| paiti ayamupasaggau aitaisu pakārādīsu atthaisu vattati, yathā – pakārai pañ‍ñā, ādikammai vippakataṃ, padhānai paṇītaṃ, padhānaṃ padhānattaṃ, issariyai pabhū ayaṃ daisassa, antaubhāvai pakkhittaṃ, viyaugai pavāsī, tapparai pācariyau, bhusatthai pavuddhakāyau, sambhavai himavatā gaṅgā pabhavati, tittiyaṃ pahūtaman‍naṃ, anāvilai pasan‍namudakaṃ, patthanai paṇihitaṃ|

parāiti  parihāni parājaya gativik‍kamā’masanādīsu| yathā – parihāniyaṃ parābhavau, parājayai parājitau, gatiyaṃ parāyanaṃ| vik‍kamai parak‍kamati, āmasanai aṅgassa parāmasanaṃ|

niiti nissaisa niggata nīharaṇa’ntaupavaisanā’bhāvanisaidha nikkhanta pātubhāvā’vadhāraṇa vibhajana upamū’padhāraṇā’vasānachaikādīsu| nissaisai nirutti, niravasaisaṃ daiti, niggatai nik‍kilaisau, niyyāti, nīharaṇai niddhāraṇaṃ, antaupavaisanai nikhātau, abhāvai nimmakkhikaṃ, nisaidhai nivāraiti, nikkhantai nibbānau, nibbānaṃ, pātubhāvai nimmitaṃ, avadhāraṇai nicchayau, vibhajanai niddaisau, upamāyaṃ nidassanaṃ, upadhāraṇai nisāmanaṃ, avasānai niṭṭhitaṃ, chaikai nipuṇau|

nīiti nīharaṇā’varaṇādīsu| nīharaṇai nīharati, āvaraṇai nīvaraṇaṃ|

uiti uggatu’ddhakamma padhāna viyauga sambhava atthalābhasatti sarūpakathanādīsu| uggatai uggacchati, uddhakammai āsanā uṭṭhitau, ukkhaipau, padhānai uttamau, laukuttarau, viyaugai ubbāsitau, sambhavai ubbhūtau, atthalābhai uppan‍naṃ ñāṇaṃ, sattiyaṃ ussahati gantuṃ, sarūpakathanai uddisati suttaṃ|

duiti asaubhanā’bhāvakucchitā’samiddhi kiccha virūpatādīsu| asaubhanai duggandhau, abhāvai dubbhikkhaṃ, kucchitai duk‍kaṭaṃ, asamiddhiyaṃ dussassaṃ, kicchai duk‍karaṃ, virūpatāyaṃ dubbaṇṇau, dummukhau|

saṃiti samaudhāna sammāsama samantabhāvasaṅgata saṅkhaipabhusattha sahattha appattha pabhavā’bhimukhabhāva saṅgaha pidhāna punappunakaraṇa samiddhādīsu| samaudhānai sandhi, sammāsamaisu samādhi, sampayuttau, samantabhāvai saṃkiṇṇā samul‍lapanā, saṅgatai saṅgamau, saṅkhaipai samāsau, bhusatthai sārattau, sahatthai saṃvāsau, appatthai samagghau| pabhavai sambhavau, abhimukhabhāvai sammukhaṃ, saṅgahai saṅgaṇhāti, pidhānai saṃvutaṃ, punappunakaraṇai sandhāvati, samiddhiyaṃ sampan‍nau|

viiti visaisa vividha viruddha vigata viyauga virūpatādīsu| visaisai vimutti visiṭṭhau, vividhai vimati vicitraṃ, viruddhai vivādau, vigatai vimalaṃ, viyaugai vippayuttau, virūpatāyaṃ virūpau|

avaiti adhaubhāga viyauga paribhava jānana suddhi nicchayadaisa thaiyyādīsu| adhaubhāgai avakkhittacakkhu, viyaugai aumuk‍kaupāhanau avakaukilaṃ vanaṃ, paribhavai avajānanaṃ avamañ‍ñati, jānanai avagacchati, suddhiyaṃ vaudānaṃ, nicchayai avadhāraṇaṃ, daisai avakāsau, thaiyyai avahārau|

anuiti anugatā’nupacchin‍na pacchattha bhusattha sādissa hīnatatiyattha lakkhaṇi’tthambhūtakkhāna bhāga vicchādīsu| anugatai anvaiti, anupacchin‍nai anusayau, pacchāsaddatthai anurathaṃ, bhusatthai anurattau, sādissai anurūpaṃ| hīnai anusāriputtaṃ pañ‍ñavantau, tatiyatthai nadimanvavasitā sainā, lakkhaṇai rukkhaṃ anu vij‍jautatai vij‍ju, itthambhūtakkhānai sādhu daivadattau mātaraṃ anu, bhāgai yadaittha maṃ anusiyā taṃ dīyatu, vicchāyaṃ rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ anu vij‍jautatai candau|

pariiti samantataubhāva paricchaida vaj‍janā’liṅgana nivāsanapūjābhaujanā’vajānana dausakkhāna lakkhaṇādīsu| samantataubhāvai parivutau, paricchaidai pariñ‍ñaiyyaṃ, vaj‍janai pariharati, āliṅganai parissajati, nivāsanai vatthaṃ paridhassati, pūjāyaṃ pāricariyā, bhaujanai bhikkhuṃ parivisati| avajānanai paribhavati, dausakkhānai paribhāsati, lakkhaṇādīsu rukkhaṃ pari vij‍jautatai vij‍juic‍cādi|

adhiiti adhiki’ssa’rūparibhāvā’dhibhavanajjhāyanā’dhiṭṭhānanicchayapāpuṇanādīsu| adhikai adhisīlaṃ, issarai adhipati, adhi brahmadattai pañ‍cālā, uparibhāvai adhirauhati, pathaviṃ adhisaissati, adhibhavanai adhibhavati, ajjhāyanai byākaraṇamadhītai, adhiṭṭhānai bhūmikampādiṃ adhiṭṭhāti, nicchayai adhimaukkhau, pāpuṇanai bhaugakkhandhaṃ adhigacchati|

abhiiti abhimukhabhāva visiṭṭhā’dhi ku’ddhakamma kula sāruppavandana lakkhaṇi’tthambhūtakkhāna vicchādīsu| abhimukhabhāvai abhimukhau abhik‍kamati, visiṭṭhai abhidhammau, adhikai abhivassati, uddhakammai abhiruhati, kulai abhijātau, sāruppai abhirūpau, vandanai abhivādaiti, lakkhaṇādīsu purimasamaṃ|

patiiti patigata paṭilaumapatinidhi patidāna nisaidhanivattana sādissa patikaraṇā’dāna patibaudha paṭic‍ca lakkhaṇi’tthambhūtakkhāna bhāgavicchādīsu| patigatai pac‍cakkhaṃ, paṭilaumai patisautaṃ, patinidhimhi ācariyatau pati sissau, patidānai tailatthikassa ghataṃ pati dadāti, nisaidhai paṭisaidhanaṃ, nivattanai paṭik‍kamati, sādissai patirūpakaṃ, patikaraṇai patikārau, ādānai patiggaṇhāti, patibaudhai paṭivaidhau, paṭic‍cai pac‍cayau, lakkhaṇādīsu purimasamaṃ|

suiti saubhana suṭṭhusammā samiddhi sukhatthādīsu| saubhanai sugandhau, suṭṭhusammādatthaisu suṭṭhu gatau sugatau, sammā gatautipi sugatau, samiddhiyaṃ subhikkhaṃ, sukhatthai sukarau|

āiti  abhimukhabhāvu’ddhakamma mariyādā’bhividhi patti’cchāparissajana ādikammaggahaṇa nivāsa samīpa’vhānādīsu| abhimukhabhāvai āgacchati, uddhakammai ārauhati, mariyādāyaṃ āpabbatā khaittaṃ, abhividhimhi ākumāraṃ yasau kac‍cāyanassa, pattiyaṃ āpattimāpan‍nau, icchāyaṃ ākaṅkhā, parissajanai āliṅganaṃ, ādikammai ārambhau, gahaṇai ādīyati ālambati, nivāsai āvasathau, samīpai āsan‍naṃ, avhānai āmantaisi|

atiiti abhik‍kamanā’tik‍kantā’tisaya bhusatthādīsu| atik‍kamanai atiraucati amhaihi, atītau, atik‍kantai ac‍cantaṃ, atisayai atikusalau, bhusatthai atik‍kaudhau ativuddhi|

apiiti sambhāvanā’paikkhā samuc‍caya garaha pañhādīsu| sambhāvanāyaṃ api dibbaisu kāmaisu, mairumpi vinivijjhitvā gacchaiyya, apaikkhāyaṃ ayampi dhammau aniyatau, samuc‍cayai itipi arahaṃ, antampi antaguṇampi ādāya, garahai api amhākaṃ paṇḍitaka, pañhai api bhantai bhikkhaṃ labhittha|

apaiti apagata garaha vaj‍jana pūjā padussanādīsu| apagatai apamānau apaitau, garahai apagabbhau, vaj‍janai apasālāya āyanti vāṇijā, pūjāyaṃ vuddhāpacāyī, padussanai aparajjhati|

upaiti upagamana samīpū’papatti sādissā’dhikū’paribhāvā’nasana dausakkhāna sañ‍ñā pubbakamma pūjā gayhākāra bhusatthādīsu| upagamanai nisin‍naṃ vā upanisīdaiyya, samīpai upanagaraṃ, upapattiyaṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jati, atha vā upapatti yutti, yathā – upapattitau ikkhatīti upaikkhā, sādissai upamānaṃ upamā, adhikai upa khāriyaṃ dauṇau, uparibhāvai upasampan‍nau, anasanai upavāsau, dausakkhānai paraṃ upapadati, sañ‍ñāyaṃ upadhā upasaggau, pubbakammai upak‍kamau upakārau, pūjāyaṃ buddhupaṭṭhākau, mātupaṭṭhānaṃ, gayhākārai saucaiyyapac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ, bhusatthai upādānaṃ, upāyāsau, upanissayauti| iti anaikatthā hi upasaggā|

vuttañ‍ca –

‘‘upasagga nipātā ca, pac‍cayā ca imai tayau|

naikainaikatthavisayā, iti nairuttikābravu’’nti||

tattha upasaggānaṃ nāmākhyātavisaisakattā liṅgasañ‍ñāyaṃ aniyamaina syādimhi sampattai taisaṃ saṅkhyākammādibhaidābhāvā taihi paṭhamaikavacanamaiva bhavati|

‘‘laupa’’nti vattamānai

282. sabbāsamāvusaupasagganipātādīhi ca|

āvusausaddatau, upasagganipātaihi ca sabbāsaṃ parāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ laupau hauti| ādisaddaina k‍vaci suttapadādīhi ca| aittha ca āvusautimassa visuṃ gahaṇaṃ sasaṅkhyattadīpanatthanti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

upaic‍catthaṃ saj‍jantīti, upasaggā hi pādayau|

cādī padādimajjhantai, nipātā nipatantiti||

paharaṇaṃ pahārau, aivaṃ parābhavau, nivāsau, nīhārau, uhārau, duhārau, saṃhārau, vihārau, avahārau, anuhārau, parihārau, adhihārau, abhihārau, patihārau, suhārau, āhārau, atihārau, apihārau, apahārau, upahārau| paharati, parābhavati, nivasati, nīharati, uddharati ic‍cādi yaujaitabbaṃ|

dhātvatthaṃ bādhatai kauci, kauci tamanuvattatai|

tamaivañ‍ñau visaisaiti, upasaggagatī tidhā||

aupasaggikapadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

naipātikapada

samuc‍cayavikappanapaṭisaidhapūraṇādiatthaṃ asatvavācakaṃ naipātikaṃ padaṃ|

tatra caiti samuc‍cayā’nvācayai’tarītarayaugasamāhārā’vadhāraṇādīsu|

vāiti vikappanū’pamāna samuc‍caya vavatthitavibhāsāsu|

na nau mā a alaṃ halaṃ ic‍caitai paṭisaidhanatthai|

alaṃ pariyatti bhūsanaisu ca|

pūraṇatthaṃ duvidhaṃ atthapūraṇaṃ padapūraṇañ‍ca|

tattha atha khalu vata vatha athau assu yagghai hi carahi naṃ taṃ vā ca tuva vau pana havai kīva ha tatau yathā sudaṃ khau vai haṃ ainaṃ saiyyathidaṃ ic‍caivamādīni padapūraṇāni|

tattha – athaiti pañhā’nantariyā’dhikārādīsu ca|

khaluiti paṭisaidhā’vadhāraṇa pasiddhīsu ca|

vataiti aikaṃsa khaidā’nukampasaṅkappaisu ca|

athauiti anvādaisai ca|

hiiti  haitu avadhāraṇaisu ca|

tuiti visaisa haitu nivattanādīsu ca|

panaiti visaisaipi|

havai, vaiic‍caitai aikaṃsatthaipi|

haṃiti visāda sambhamaisupi|

saiyyathidanti taṃ katamanti atthaipi|

atthapūraṇaṃ duvidhaṃ vibhattiyuttaṃ, avibhattiyuttañ‍ca|

atthi sak‍kā labbhā ic‍caitai paṭhamāyaṃ|

āvusau ambhau hambhau rai arai harai jaiic‍caitai āmantanai|

divā bhiyyau namau ic‍caitai paṭhamāyaṃ, dutiyāyañ‍ca|

sayaṃ sāmaṃ saṃ sammā kinti ic‍caitai tatiyatthai, sautau dhāpac‍cayantā ca| suttasau padasau anic‍catau dukkhatau aikadhā dvidhā ic‍cādi|

tavai tuṃpac‍cayantā catutthiyā, kātavai dātavai kātuṃ kāraituṃ dātuṃ dāpaituṃ ic‍cādi|

sau taupac‍cayantā pañ‍camiyatthai, dīghasau aurasau rājatau vā cauratau vā ic‍cādi|

tau sattamyatthaipi, trathādipac‍cayantā ca| aikatau puratau pacchatau passatau piṭṭhitau pādatau sīsatau aggatau mūlatau yatra yattha yahiṃ tatra tattha tahiṃ tahaṃ ic‍cādi|

samantā sāmantā paritau abhitau samantatau aikajjhaṃ aikamantaṃ haiṭṭhā upari uddhaṃ adhau tiriyaṃ sammukhā  parammukhā āvi rahau tirau uc‍caṃ nīcaṃ antau antarā ajjhattaṃ bahiddhā bāhirā bāhiraṃ bahi auraṃ pāraṃ ārā ārakā pacchā purai huraṃ paic‍ca ic‍caitai sattamiyā|

sampati āyati aj‍ja aparaj‍ju paraj‍ja suvai svai parasuvai hiyyau parai saj‍ju sāyaṃ pātau kālaṃ kal‍laṃ divā nattaṃ nic‍caṃ satataṃ abhiṇhaṃ abhikkhaṇaṃ muhuṃ muhuttaṃ bhūtapubbaṃ purā yadā tadā kadā ic‍cādayau kālasattamiyā| iti vibhattiyuttāni|

avibhattiyuttaisu ca appaiva appaivanāma nu ic‍caitai saṃsayatthai|

addhā añ‍ñadatthu taggha jātu kāmaṃ sasak‍kaṃic‍caitai aikaṃsatthai|

aivaiti avadhāraṇai|

kac‍cinu’kin‍nunanukathaṃ kiṃsu kiṃic‍caitai pucchanatthai|

aivaṃ iti itthaṃ ic‍caitai nidassanai|

iti haitu vākyaparisamattīsu ca|

yāva tāva yāvatā tāvatā kittāvatā aittāvatā kīva ic‍caitai paricchaidanatthai|

aivaṃ sāhu lahu aupāyikaṃ patirūpaṃ āma sādhu iti sampaṭicchanatthai|

yathā tathā yathaiva tathaiva aivaṃ aivamaiva aivamaivaṃ aivampi yathāpi saiyyathāpi saiyyathāpināma viya iva yathariva tathariva yathānāma tathānāma yathāhi tathāhi yathācatathāca ic‍caitai paṭibhāgatthai|

yathāiti  yauggatā vicchā padatthānativattanidassanaisu ca|

aivaṃiti upadaisa pañhādīsu ca|

kiñ‍cāpiiti anuggahatthai|

ahauiti garaha pasaṃsana patthanaisu ca|

nāmaiti garaha pasaṃsana sañ‍ñā pañhaisu ca|

sādhuiti pasaṃsana yācanaisu ca|

iṅgha handa ic‍caitai caudanatthai|

sādhu suṭṭhu aivamaitanti anumaudanai|

kiraiti anussavaṇa assaddhaiyyaisu|

nūnaiti anumānā’nussaraṇa parivitak‍kanaisu|

kasmāiti kāraṇapucchanai|

yasmā tasmā tathāhi taina ic‍caitai kāraṇacchaidanatthai|

saha saddhiṃ samaṃ amāiti samakriyāyaṃ|

vinā ritaiiti vippayaugai|

nānā puthu bahuppakārai|

puthu visuṃ asaṅghātai ca|

duṭṭhu ku jigucchāyaṃ|

puna appaṭhamai|

kathañ‍ci kicchatthai ca|

dhā kkhattuṃ sakiñ‍ca saṅkhyāvibhāgai|

īsakaṃ  appatthai|

saṇikaṃ mandatthai|

khippaṃ araṃ lahu āsuṃ tuṇṇaṃ aciraṃ sīghatthai|

ciraṃ cirassaṃ dīghakālai|

cai yadi saṅkāvaṭṭhānai|

dhuvaṃ thirāvadhāraṇaisu|

hā visādai|

tuṇhī abhāsanai|

sacchi pac‍cakkhai|

musā micchā alikaṃ asac‍cai|

suvatthi āsīsatthai ic‍cādi|

tuna tvāna tvāpac‍cayantā ussuk‍kanatthai bhavanti|

yathā – passituna passiya passitvāna passitvā disvā disvānadassaitvā dātuna datvāna datvā upādāya dāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā vicaiyya vinaiyya nihac‍ca samaic‍ca apaic‍ca upaic‍ca ārabbha āgamma ic‍cādi|

aivaṃ nāmākhyātaupasaggavinimuttaṃ yadabyayalakkhaṇaṃ, taṃ sabbaṃ nipātapadanti vaiditabbaṃ|

vuttañ‍ca –

‘‘muttaṃ padattayā yasmā, tasmā nipatatyantarā|

naipātikanti taṃ vuttaṃ, yaṃ abyaya salakkhaṇa’’nti||

naipātikapadaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

pul‍liṅgaṃ itthiliṅgañ‍ca, napuṃsakamathāparaṃ|

tiliṅgañ‍ca aliṅgañ‍ca, nāmikaṃ pañ‍cadhā ṭhitaṃ||

iti padarūpasiddhiyaṃ nāmakaṇḍau dutiyau|

3. kārakakaṇḍa

atha  vibhattīnamatthabhaidā vuc‍cantai|

tattha aikampi atthaṃ kammādivasaina, aikattādivasaina ca vibhajantīti vibhattiyau, syādayau| tā pana paṭhamādibhaidaina sattavidhā|

tattha kasmiṃ atthai paṭhamā?

283. liṅgatthai paṭhamā|

liṅgatthābhidhānamattai paṭhamāvibhatti hauti|

liṅgassa atthau liṅgatthau| aittha ca līnaṃ aṅganti liṅgaṃ, apākaṭau avayavau, purisautiādīnañhi pakatippac‍cayādivibhāgakappanāya nipphāditānaṃ saddappatirūpakānaṃ nāmikapadānaṃ paṭhamaṃ ṭhapaitabbaṃ pakatirūpaṃ apākaṭattā, avayavattā ca liṅganti vuc‍cati| atha vā visadāvisadaubhayarahitākāravauhārasaṅkhātaina tividhaliṅgaina sahitatthassa, tabbinimuttassupasaggādīnamatthassa ca līnassa gamanatau, liṅganatau vā liṅganti anvatthanāmavasaina vā ‘‘dhātuppac‍cayavibhattivaj‍jitamatthavaṃ liṅga’’nti vacanatau parasamañ‍ñāvasaina vā liṅganti idha pāṭipadikāparanāmadhaiyyaṃ syādivibhatyantapadapakatirūpamaiva vuc‍catīti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

liṅgassatthau nāma pabandhavisaisākāraina pavattamānai rūpādayau upādāya pañ‍ñāpīyamānau tadañ‍ñānañ‍ñabhāvaina anibbacanīyau samūhasantānādibhaidau upādāpañ‍ñattisaṅkhātau ghaṭapaṭādivauhāratthau ca pathavīdhātuphassādīnaṃ sabhāvadhammānaṃ  kāladaisādibhaidabhin‍nānaṃ vijātiyavinivattau sajātiyasādhāraṇau yathāsaṅkaitamāraupasiddhau taj‍jāpañ‍ñattisaṅkhātau kakkhaḷattaphusanādisāmañ‍ñākārau ca|

sau pana kammādisaṃsaṭṭhau, suddhau cāti duvidhau| tattha kammādīsu dutiyādīnaṃ vidhīyamānattā kammādisaṃsaggarahitau liṅgasaṅkhyāparimāṇayuttau, tabbinimuttupasaggādipadatthabhūtau ca suddhau saddatthau idha liṅgatthau nāma|

yau pana ākhyātakitakataddhitasamāsaihi vuttau kammādisaṃsaṭṭhau atthau, saupi dutiyādīnaṃ puna attanā vattabbassa atthavisaisassābhāvaina avisayattā, liṅgatthamattassa sambhavatau ca paṭhamāyaiva visayau|

hauti caittha –

paṭhamāvupasaggatthai, kaisañ‍catthai nipātasaddānaṃ|

liṅgādikai ca suddhai-bhihitai kammādiatthaipi||

saliṅgai tāva – aisau purisau, aitai purisā, aisā kañ‍ñā, aitā kañ‍ñāyau, aitaṃ cittaṃ, aitāni cittāni|

sasaṅkhyai – aikau dvai|

saparimāṇai – dauṇau khārī āḷhakaṃ|

liṅgādivinimuttai sattāmattai – ca vā ha ahaṃ atthi sak‍kālabbhā ic‍cādi|

‘‘liṅgatthai paṭhamā’’ti adhikic‍ca ‘‘ālapanai cā’’ti ālapanatthai ca paṭhamā, abhimukhaṃ katvā lapanaṃ ālapanaṃ, āmantanaṃ avhānanti atthau|

aittha ca āmantanaṃ nāma pagaiva laddhasarūpassa saddaina abhimukhīkaraṇaṃ, katābhimukhau pana ‘‘gacchā’’tiādinā nayaina kriyāya  yaujīyati, tasmā āmantanasamayai kriyāyaugābhāvatau idaṃ kārakavauhāraṃ na labhati|

vuttañ‍ca

‘‘saddainābhimukhīkārau, vij‍jamānassa vatthunau|

āmantanaṃ vidhātabbai, natthi ‘rājā bhavai’tida’’nti||

bhau purisa aihi, bhau purisā vā, bhavantau purisā aitha|

kasmiṃ atthai dutiyā?

284. kammatthai dutiyā|

kammatthai liṅgamhā dutiyāvibhatti hauti|

anabhihitai aivāyaṃ, ‘‘kammani dutiyāyaṃ ttau’’ti vacanañ‍caittha ñāpakaṃ|

kiṃ kammaṃ?

‘‘yaina vā kayiratai taṃ karaṇa’’nti itau ‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

285. yaṃ karauti taṃ kammaṃ|

yaṃ vā karauti, yaṃ vā vikarauti, yaṃ vā pāpuṇāti, taṃ kārakaṃ kammasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

idha liṅgakālavacanamatantaṃ| karīyatīti kammaṃ| tattha kārakaṃ, sādhakaṃ kriyānipphattiyā kāraṇamuc‍catai, taṃ pana kārakaṃ chabbidhaṃ kammaṃ kattā karaṇaṃ sampadānamapādānamaukāsau cāti| tattha sabhāvatau, parikappatau vā kammādimhi satiyaiva kriyābhāvatau kammādīnaṃ chan‍nampi kārakavauhārau siddhauva hauti|

taṃ  pana kammaṃ tividhaṃ nibbattanīyaṃ vikaraṇīyaṃ pāpaṇīyañ‍cāti| yathā – mātā puttaṃ vijāyati, āhārau sukhaṃ janayati| ghaṭaṃ karauti daivadattau, kaṭṭhamaṅgāraṃ karauti, suvaṇṇaṃ kaiyūraṃ, kaṭakaṃ vā karauti, vīhayau lunāti| daivadattau nivaisanaṃ pavisati, ādic‍caṃ passati, dhammaṃ suṇāti, paṇḍitai payirupāsati|

vuttañ‍ca

‘‘nibbattivikatippatti-bhaidaina tividhaṃ mataṃ|

kattu kriyābhigammaṃ taṃ, sukhaṅgāraṃ nivaisana’’nti||

aittha ca icchitānicchitakathitākathitādibhaidamanapaikkhitvā sabbasaṅgāhakavasaina ‘‘yaṃ karauti taṃ kamma’’nti vuttattā, atthantaravikappanavādhikāratau ca sabbattha imināva kammasañ‍ñā hauti|

tattha anicchitakammaṃ yathā – kaṇṭakaṃ maddati, visaṃ gilati, gāmaṃ gacchantau rukkhamūlaṃ upagacchati|

akathitakammaṃ yathā – yañ‍ñadattaṃ kambalaṃ yācatai brāhmaṇau| aittha hi ‘‘kambala’’miti kathitakammaṃ dvikammikāya yācanakriyāya pattumicchitatarattā| ‘‘yañ‍ñadatta’’miti appadhānattā akathitakammaṃ| tathā samiddhaṃ dhanaṃ bhikkhatai, ajaṃ gāmaṃ nayati, parābhavantaṃ purisaṃ, mayaṃ pucchāma gautamaṃ, bhagavā bhikkhū aitadavauca ic‍cādi|

abhihitakammai pana na hauti, yathā – kaṭau karīyatai daivadattaina, sugataina daisitau dhammau, yañ‍ñadattau kambalaṃ yācīyatai brāhmaṇaina ic‍cādi|

‘‘dutiyā’’ti adhikārau|

286. gati  buddhi bhuja paṭha hara kara sayādīnaṃ kāritai vā|

gamu sappa gatimhi, budha baudhanai, budha avagamanai vā, bhuja pālanabyavaharaṇaisu, paṭha byattiyaṃ vācāyaṃ, hara haraṇai, kara karaṇai, si sayai ic‍caivamādīnaṃ dhātūnaṃ payaugai kāritai sati payauj‍jakakattubhūtai kammani liṅgamhā dutiyāvibhatti hauti vā| nic‍casampattai vikappatthauyaṃ, taina tassa pakkhai tatiyā hauti|

yau kauci purisau gāmaṃ gacchati, tamañ‍ñau payaujayati| purisau purisaṃ gāmaṃ gamayati, purisaina vā gāmaṃ gamayati| aivaṃ sissaṃ dhammaṃ baudhaiti ācariyau, mātā puttaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhaujayati, sissaṃ dhammaṃ pāṭhaiti ācariyau, purisau purisaṃ bhāraṃ hāraiti, tathā purisau purisaṃ kammaṃ kārayati, purisaina vā kammaṃ kārāpayati, purisau purisaṃ sayāpayati| aivaṃ sabbattha kāritai kattukammani dutiyā|

kāritaiti kiṃ? purisau gāmaṃ gacchati|

abhihitai na bhavati, purisaina purisau gāmaṃ gamīyatai, sissau dhammaṃ baudhīyatai ic‍cādi|

287. kāladdhānamac‍cantasaṃyaugai|

ac‍cantaṃ nirantaraṃ saṃyaugauac‍cantasaṃyaugau| kāladdhānaṃ dabbaguṇakriyāhi ac‍cantasaṃyaugai taihi kāladdhānavācīhi liṅgaihi dutiyāvibhatti hauti|

kālai tāva – sattāhaṃ gavapānaṃ, māsaṃ maṃsaudanaṃ, saradaṃ ramaṇīyā nadī, sabbakālaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ nandanaṃ, māsaṃ sajjhāyati, tayau māsai abhidhammaṃ daisaisi|

addhānai  – yaujanaṃ vanarāji, yaujanaṃ dīghau pabbatau, kausaṃ sajjhāyati|

ac‍cantasaṃyaugaiti kiṃ? māsai māsai bhuñ‍jati, yaujanai yaujanai vihāraṃ patiṭṭhāpaisi|

288. kammappavacanīyayuttai|

kammappavacanīyaihi nipātaupasaggaihi yuttai yaugai sati liṅgamhā dutiyāvibhatti hauti|

kammaṃ pavacanīyaṃ yaisaṃ tai kammappavacanīyā, parasamañ‍ñāvasaina vā anvādayau kammappavacanīyā|

tattha anusaddassa lakkhaṇai, sahatthai, hīnai ca kammappavacanīyasañ‍ñā vuttā| yathā – pabbajitamanu pabbajiṃsu, nadimanvavasitā bārāṇasī, nadiyā saha avabaddhāti atthau, anu sāriputtaṃ pañ‍ñavā|

lakkhaṇādīsu ‘‘lakkhaṇi’tthambhūtakkhānabhāga vicchāsu pati pari anavau’’ti pati pari anūnaṃ kammappavacanīyasañ‍ñā vuttā|

lakkhaṇai sūriyuggamanaṃ pati dibbā bhakkhā pātubhavaiyyuṃ, rukkhaṃ pati vij‍jautatai candau, rukkhaṃ pari, rukkhaṃ anu|

itthambhūtakkhānai sādhu daivadattau mātaraṃ pati, mātaraṃ pari, mātaraṃ anu|

bhāgai yadaittha maṃ pati siyā, maṃ pari, maṃ anu, taṃ dīyatu|

vicchāyaugai  atthamatthaṃ pati saddau nivisati, rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ pati vij‍jautatai candau, rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ pari, rukkhaṃ rukkhaṃ anu|

‘‘abhirabhāgai’’ti abhissa bhāgavaj‍jitaisu lakkhaṇādīsu kammappavacanīyasañ‍ñā vuttā| taṃ khau pana bhavantaṃ gautamaṃ aivaṃ kalyāṇau kittisaddau abbhuggatau, sādhu daivadattau mātaraṃ abhi|

nipātai dhi brāhmaṇassa hantāra mic‍caivamādi|

289. k‍vaci dutiyā chaṭṭhīnamatthai|

chaṭṭhīnaṃ atthai k‍vaci dutiyāvibhatti hauti| antarāabhitau paritau pati paṭibhātiyaugai ayaṃ| antarā ca rājagahaṃ antarā ca nāḷandaṃ addhānamaggappaṭipan‍nau, rājagahassa ca nāḷandāya ca majjhaiti atthau| abhitau gāmaṃ vasati, paritau gāmaṃ vasati, nadiṃ nairañ‍jaraṃ pati, nairañ‍jarāya nadiyā samīpaiti atthau| paṭibhantu taṃ cunda baujjhaṅgā, upamā maṃ paṭibhāti, upamā mayhaṃ upaṭṭhahatīti atthau|

‘‘k‍vaci dutiyā, atthai’’ti ca vattatai|

290. tatiyāsattamīnañ‍ca|

tatiyāsattamīnamatthai ca k‍vaci liṅgamhā dutiyāvibhatti hauti|

tatiyatthai sacai maṃ nālapissati, tvañ‍ca maṃ nābhibhāsasi, vinā saddhammaṃ kutau sukhaṃ, upāyamantaraina na atthasiddhi|

sattamiyatthai – kālai, upānvajjhāvasassa payaugai, adhisiṭṭhāvasānaṃ payaugai, tappānacārai ca dutiyā|

kālai tāva – pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā, aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā| imaṃ rattiṃ cattārau mahārājānau|

upādipubbassa vasadhātussa payaugai – gāmaṃ upavasati, gāmaṃ anuvasati, vihāraṃ adhivasati, gāmaṃ āvasati, agāraṃ ajjhāvasati| tathā pathaviṃ adhisaissati, gāmaṃ adhitiṭṭhati, gāmaṃ ajjhāvasati|

tappānacāraisu – nadiṃ pivati, gāmaṃ carati ic‍cādi|

kasmiṃ atthai tatiyā?

291. karaṇai tatiyā|

karaṇakārakai tatiyāvibhatti hauti|

kiṃ karaṇaṃ?

292. yaina vā kayiratai taṃ karaṇaṃ|

yaina vā kattā upakaraṇabhūtaina vatthunā kriyaṃ abyavadhānaina karauti, yaina vā vikarauti, yaina vā pāpuṇāti, taṃ kārakaṃ karaṇasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

karīyatai anaināti karaṇaṃ, aittha ca satipi sabbakārakānaṃ kriyāsādhakattai ‘‘yaina vā kayiratai’’ti visaisaitvā vacanaṃ kattūpakaraṇabhūtaisu kārakaisu sādhakatamassaiva gahaṇatthaṃ|

vuttañ‍ca
