‘‘yassa sabbavisaisaina, kriyāsaṃsiddhihaitutā|

sambhāvīyati taṃ vuttaṃ, karaṇaṃ nāma kāraka’’nti||

taṃ pana duvidhaṃ ajjhattika bāhiravasaina|

yathā – hatthaina kammaṃ karauti, cakkhunā rūpaṃ passati, manasā dhammaṃ viñ‍ñāya| dattaina vīhayau lunāti, agginā kuṭiṃ jhāpaiti|

‘‘tatiyā’’ti adhikārau|

293. kattari ca|

kattari ca kārakai liṅgamhā tatiyāvibhatti hauti| caggahaṇaina itthambhūtalakkhaṇai, kriyāpavaggai, pubbasadisasamūnattha kalaha nipuṇa missaka sakhilatthādiyaugai, kāladdhānaisu, pac‍cattakammatthapañ‍camiyatthādīsu ca tatiyā|

kau ca kattā?

294. yau karauti sa kattā|

yau kriyaṃ attappadhānau hutvā karauti, sau kattusañ‍ñau hauti|

sau tividhau suddhakattā haitukattā kammakattāti| tattha yau sayamaiva kriyaṃ karauti, sau suddhakattā| yau añ‍ñaṃ kātuṃ samatthaṃ akarauntaṃ kammaṃ niyaujaiti, sau haitukattā, yathā – gantuṃ samatthau daivadattau, tamañ‍ñau payaujaiti ‘‘gamayati daivadatta’’nti|

yaṃ pana tattha tattha gacchati daivadattau, tamañ‍ñau payaujayati ‘‘gamayati daivadatta’’nti haitvatthanidassanaṃ, tampi sāmatthiyadassanavasaina  vuttanti gahaitabbaṃ| añ‍ñathā yadi sayamaiva gacchati, kiṃ tattha payaujakabyāpāraina akarauntaṃ balaina kārayati, pāsāṇaṃ uṭṭhāpayatītiādikañ‍ca na sijjhaiyya|

aittha pana

‘‘kattā’’ti vattatai|

293. yau kāraiti sa haitu|

yau kattāraṃ kāraiti, sau haitusañ‍ñau hauti, kattā cāti haitukattusañ‍ñā|

yau pana parassa kriyaṃ paṭic‍ca kammabhūtaupi sukarattā sayamaiva sijjhantau viya hauti, sau kammakattā nāma, yathā – sayaṃ karīyatai kaṭau, sayamaiva pac‍catai audanauti|

vuttañ‍ca

‘‘attappadhānau kiriyaṃ, yau nibbattaiti kārakau|

appayuttau payuttau vā, sa kattāti pavuc‍cati||

haitukattāti kathitau,

kattunau yau payaujakau|

kammakattāti sukarau,

kammabhūtau kathīyatai’’ti||

nanu ca ‘‘saṃyaugau jāyatai’’tiādīsu kathaṃ purai asatau jananakriyāya kattubhāvausiyāti? vuc‍catai – laukasaṅkaitasiddhau hi saddappayaugau, avij‍jamānampi hi laukau saddābhidhaiyyatāya vij‍jamānaṃ viya gahaitvā vauharati, vikappabuddhigahitākārauyaiva hi saddainābhidhīyatai, na tu vatthusabhāvau, añ‍ñathā sutamayañāṇainapi pac‍cakkhaina viya vatthusabhāvasacchikaraṇappasaṅgau ca musāvāda kudiṭṭhivādādīnamabhāvappasaṅgau ca siyā, tasmā buddhiparikappitapañ‍ñattivasainapi saddappavatti hautīti asatau saṃyaugādissapi hautaiva jananakriyāya kattukārakatāti|

yathāha

‘‘vauhāravisayau saddau, naikantaparamatthikau|

buddhisaṅkappitau atthau, tassatthauti pavuc‍cati||

buddhiyā gahitattā hi, saṃyaugau jāyatai iti|

saṃyaugau vij‍jamānauva, kattā bhavati jātiyā’’ti||

tatra tatiyā jinaina daisitau dhammau, buddhaina jitau mārau, ahinā daṭṭhau narau, buddhaina baudhitau laukau, saddhaihi kāritau vihārau|

abhihitai na bhavati| kaṭaṃ karauti daivadattau, kāraiti vā|

itthambhūtassa lakkhaṇai – sā bhin‍naina sīsaina paggharantaina lauhitaina paṭivissakānaṃ ujjhāpaisi, ūnapañ‍cabandhanaina pattaina añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaiyya, tidaṇḍakaina paribbājakamaddakkhi|

apavaggai – aikāhainaiva bārāṇasiṃ pāyāsi, navahi māsaihi vihāraṃ niṭṭhāpaisi, yaujanaina adhītaṃ byākaraṇaṃ, kriyāpavaggauti kriyāya āsuṃ pariniṭṭhāpanaṃ|

pubbādiyaugai – māsaina pubbau, pitarā sadisau, mātarā samau, kahāpaṇaina ūnau, dhanaina vikalau, asinā kalahau, ācāraina nipuṇau, vācāya nipuṇau, guḷaina missakaṃ, tilaina missakaṃ, vācāya sakhilau, maṇinā atthau, dhanaina atthau, pitarā tulyau|

kāladdhānaisu – māsaina bhuñ‍jati, yaujanaina gacchati|

pac‍cattai – attanāva attānaṃ samman‍nati|

kammatthai – tilaihi khaittai vapati|

pañ‍camiyatthai – sumuttā mayaṃ taina mahāsamaṇaina|

296. sahādiyaugai ca|

saha saddhiṃ samaṃ nānā vinā alaṃ kimic‍caivamādīhi yaugai liṅgamhā tatiyāvibhatti hauti, casaddaina sahatthaipi|

tattha sahasaddaina yaugau kriyā guṇa dabba samavāyai sambhavati| yathā – vitak‍kaina saha vattati, puttaina saha thūlau, antaivāsikasaddhivihārikaihi saha ācariyupajjhāyānaṃ lābhau, nisīdi bhagavā saddhiṃ bhikkhusaṅghaina, sahassaina samaṃ mitā, sabbaihi mai piyaihi manāpaihi nānābhāvau vinābhāvau, saṅghau vināpi gaggaina upausathaṃ karaiyya, alaṃ tai idha vāsaina, kiṃ mai aikaina tiṇṇaina, purisaina thāmadassinā, kiṃ tai jaṭāhi dummaidha, kiṃ tai ajinasāṭiyā|

sahatthai – daivadattau rājagahaṃ pāvisi kaukālikaina pacchāsamaṇaina, dukkhau bālaihi saṃvāsau|

297. haitvatthai ca|

yaugaggahaṇamihānuvattatai, haitvatthai, haitvatthappayaugai ca liṅgamhā tatiyāvibhatti hauti|

kismiñ‍ci phalai diṭṭhasāmatthiyaṃ kāraṇaṃ haitu, sauyaiva atthau, tasmiṃ haitvatthai, an‍naina vasati, dhammaina vasati, vij‍jāya vasati|

na jac‍cāvasalau hauti,

na jac‍cā hauti brāhmaṇau|

kammunā vasalau hauti,

kammunā hauti brāhmaṇau||

dānaina bhaugavā, ācāraina kulī|

kaina pāṇi kāmadadau, kaina pāṇi madhussavau|

kaina tai brahmacariyaina, puñ‍ñaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhati||

haitvatthappayaugai – kaina nimittaina, kaina payaujanaina, kainaṭṭhaina, kaina haitunā vasati|

298. sattamyatthai ca|

sattamyatthai ca liṅgamhā tatiyāvibhatti hauti|

kāladdhānadisādaisādīsu cāyaṃ| taina samayaina, taina kālaina, kālaina dhammassavaṇaṃ, sau vau mamac‍cayaina satthā, māsaina bhuñ‍jati, yaujanaina dhāvati| puratthimaina dhataraṭṭhau, dakkhiṇaina virūḷhakau, pacchimaina virūpakkhau, uttaraina  kasivantau janaughamaparaina ca, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami ic‍cādi|

299. yainaṅgavikārau|

yaina byādhimatā aṅgaina aṅginau vikārau lakkhīyatai, tattha tatiyāvibhatti hauti| aittha ca aṅgamassa atthīti aṅgaṃ, sarīraṃ| akkhinā kāṇau, hatthaina kuṇī, pādaina khañ‍jau, piṭṭhiyā khuj‍jau|

300. visaisanai ca|

visaisīyati visaisitabbaṃ anaināti visaisanaṃ, gauttādi| tasmiṃ gauttanāmajātisippavayauguṇasaṅkhātai visaisanatthai tatiyāvibhatti hauti, casaddaina pakatiādīhi ca| gauttaina gautamau nāthau|

sāriputtauti nāmaina, vissutau pañ‍ñavā ca sau|

jātiyā khattiyau buddhau, laukai appaṭipuggalau||

tadahu pabbajitau santau, jātiyā sattavassikau|

saupi maṃ anusāsaiyya, sampaṭicchāmi matthakai||

sippaina naḷakārau sau, aikūnatiṃsau vayasā, vij‍jāya sādhu, pañ‍ñāya sādhu, tapasā uttamau, suvaṇṇaina abhirūpau|

pakatiādīsu – pakatiyā abhirūpau, yaibhuyyaina mattikā, samaina dhāvati, visamaina dhāvati, dvidauṇaina dhañ‍ñaṃ kiṇāti, sahassaina assakai kiṇāti ic‍cādi|

kasmiṃ atthai catutthī?

301. sampadānai  catutthī|

sampadānakārakai liṅgamhā catutthīvibhatti hauti|

kiñ‍ca sampadānaṃ?

302. yassa dātukāmau raucatai dhārayatai vā taṃ sampadānaṃ|

yassa vā dātukāmau, yassa vā raucatai, yassa vā dhārayatai, taṃ kārakaṃ sampadānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti| sammā padīyatai assāti sampadānaṃ, paṭiggāhakau|

taṃ pana tividhaṃ diyyamānassānivāraṇajjhaisanānumativasaina| yathā – buddhassa pupphaṃ yajati, baudhirukkhassa jalaṃ dadāti| ajjhaisanai – yācakānaṃ dhanaṃ dadāti| anumatiyaṃ – bhikkhūnaṃ dānaṃ daiti|

yathāha

‘‘anirākaraṇārādha-nābbhanuñ‍ñavasaina hi|

sampadānaṃ tidhā vuttaṃ, rukkha yācaka bhikkhavau’’ti||

dātukāmauti kiṃ? rañ‍ñau daṇḍaṃ dadāti|

raucanādīsu pana – samaṇassa raucatai sac‍caṃ, māyasmantānampi saṅghabhaidau ruc‍cittha, yassāyasmatau khamati, daivadattassa suvaṇṇacchattaṃ dhārayatai yañ‍ñadattau|

‘‘sampadānaṃ, vā’’ti ca vattatai|

303. silāgha hanu ṭhā sapa dhāra piha kudha duhi ssāsūya rādhikkha pac‍cāsuṇa anupatigiṇapubbakattāraucanatthatadattha tumatthālamattha mañ‍ñānādarappāṇini gatyatthakammani āsisattha sammuti bhiyyasattamyatthaisu ca|

catuppadamidaṃ| silāgha katthanai, hanu apanayanai, ṭhā gatinivattimhi, sapa ak‍kausai, dhara dhāraṇai, piha icchāyaṃ ic‍caitaisaṃ dhātūnaṃ payaugai, kudha kaupai, duha jighaṃsāyaṃ, issa issāyaṃ, usūya dausāvikaraṇai ic‍caitaisaṃ tadatthavācīnañ‍ca dhātūnaṃ payaugai ca rādha hiṃsāsaṃrādhaisu, ikkha dassanaṅkaisūti imaisaṃ payaugai ca pati āpubbassa su savaṇaiti imassa ca anupatipubbassa gaisaddaiti imassa ca pubbakattā ca āraucanatthappayaugai, tadatthai, tumatthai, alamatthappayaugai ca mañ‍ñatippayaugai anādarai appāṇini ca gatyatthānaṃ kammani ca āsisatthappayaugai ca sammuti bhiyyappayaugaisu ca sattamyatthai cāti taṃ kammādikārakaṃ sampadānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti, casaddaggahaṇaina pahiṇatikappati pahauti upamāñ‍jalikaraṇa phāsu atthasaiyyappabhutiyaugai ca purai viya catutthī|

silāghādippayaugai tāva – buddhassa silāghatai| upajjhāyassa silāghatai, thaumaitīti atthau|

hanutai mayhamaiva, hanutai tuyhamaiva, apalapatīti atthau|

upatiṭṭhaiyya sakyaputtānaṃ vaḍḍhakī, aittha ca upaṭṭhānaṃ nāma upagamanaṃ| bhikkhussa bhuñ‍jamānassa pānīyaina vā vidhūpanaina vā upatiṭṭhaiyya|

tuyhaṃ sapatai, mayhaṃ sapatai, aittha ca sapanaṃ nāma sac‍cakaraṇaṃ|

dhārayatippayaugai dhanikauyaiva sampadānaṃ, suvaṇṇaṃ tai dhārayatai, iṇaṃ dhārayatīti atthau| tassa rañ‍ñau mayaṃ nāgaṃ dhārayāma|

pihappayaugai  icchitauyaiva, daivāpi tassa pihayanti tādinau, daivāpi taisaṃ pihayanti, sambuddhānaṃ satīmataṃ, patthaintīti atthau|

kaudhādiatthānaṃ payaugai yaṃ pati kaupau, tassa kujjha mahāvīra, yadihaṃ tassa kuppaiyyaṃ|

duhayati disānaṃ maighau, yau mittānaṃ na dubbhati|

titthiyā issanti samaṇānaṃ|

duj‍janā guṇavantānaṃ usūyanti, kā usūyā vijānataṃ|

rādhikkhappayaugai yassa vipucchanaṃ kammavikhyāpanatthaṃ, vādhikāratau dutiyā ca| ārādhau mai rañ‍ñau, rañ‍ñau aparajjhati, rājānaṃ vā aparajjhati, kyāhaṃ ayyānaṃ aparajjhāmi, kyāhaṃ ayyai aparajjhāmi vā|

āyasmatau upālitthairassa upasampadāpaikkhau upatissau, āyasmantaṃ vā|

pac‍cāsuṇa anupatigiṇānaṃ pubbakattā ca suṇautissa dhātussa pac‍cāyaugai, giṇassa ca anupatiyaugai pubbassa kammunau yau kattā, sau sampadānasañ‍ñau hauti| yathā – bhagavā bhikkhū aitadavauca, aittha ‘‘bhikkhū’’ti akathitakammaṃ, ‘‘aita’’nti kathitakammaṃ, pubbassa vacanakammassa kattā bhagavā| bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ, āsuṇanti buddhassa bhikkhū, tathā bhikkhu janaṃ dhammaṃ sāvaiti, tassa bhikkhunau janau anugiṇāti, tassa bhikkhunau janau patigiṇāti, sādhukāradānādinā taṃ ussāhayatīti atthau|

yau vadaiti sa kattāti, vuttaṃ kammanti vuc‍cati|

yau paṭiggāhakau tassa, sampadānaṃ vijāniyāti||

āraucanatthappayaugai  yassa āraucaiti, taṃ sampadānaṃ| āraucayāmi vau bhikkhavai, paṭivaidayāmi vau bhikkhavai, āmantayāmi tai mahārāja, āmanta khau taṃ gacchāmāti vā| aittha ca āraucanasaddassa kathanappakāratthattā daisanatthādippayaugaipi catutthī| dhammaṃ vau daisaissāmi, daisaitu bhantai bhagavā dhammaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ, yathā nau bhagavā byākaraiyya, niruttiṃ tai pavakkhāmi ic‍cādi|

tadatthai sampadānasañ‍ñā, catutthī ca|

‘‘atau, vā’’ti ca vattatai|

304. āya catutthaikavacanassa tu|

akārantatau liṅgamhā parassa catutthaikavacanassa āyādaisau hauti vā, saralaupādi|

buddhassatthāya dhammassatthāya saṅghassatthāya jīvitaṃ paric‍cajāmi, piṇḍapātaṃ paṭisaivāmi naiva davāya na madāya na maṇḍanāya na vibhūsanāya, ūnassa pāripūriyā, atthāya hitāya sukhāya saṃvattati|

tumatthai – laukānukampāya, laukamanukampitunti atthau| tathā phāsuvihārāya|

alaṃsaddassa atthā arahapaṭikkhaipā| arahatthai – alaṃ mai raj‍jaṃ, alaṃ bhikkhu pattassa, akkhadhuttau purisapuggalau nālaṃ dārabharaṇāya, alaṃ mal‍lau mal‍lassa, arahati mal‍lau mal‍lassa|

paṭikkhaipai – alaṃ tai idha vāsaina, alaṃ mai hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaina, kiṃ mai aikaina tiṇṇaina, kiṃ tai jaṭāhi dummaidha, kiṃ taittha catumaṭṭhassa|

mañ‍ñatippayaugai anādarai appāṇini kammaniyaiva – kaṭṭhassa tuvaṃ mañ‍ñai, kaḷiṅgarassa tuvaṃ mañ‍ñai, jīvitaṃ tiṇāyapi na mañ‍ñamānau|

anādaraiti kiṃ? suvaṇṇaṃ taṃ mañ‍ñai| appāṇinīti kiṃ? gadrabhaṃ tuvaṃ mañ‍ñai|

gatyatthakammani vādhikāratau dutiyā ca| appau saggāya gacchati, appau saggaṃ gacchati, nibbānāya vajantiyā, mūlāya paṭikassaiyya, mūlaṃ paṭikassaiyya|

āsīsanatthai āyubhaddakusalādiyaugaiyaiva, āyasmatau dīghāyu hautu, ‘‘tautitā sasmiṃnāsū’’ti ntussa savibhattissa tau ādaisau| bhaddaṃ bhavatau hautu, kusalaṃ bhavatau hautu, anāmayaṃ bhavatau hautu, sukhaṃ bhavatau hautu, atthaṃ bhavatau hautu, hitaṃ bhavatau hautu, svāgataṃ bhavatau hautu, sautthi hautu sabbasattānaṃ|

sammutippayaugai – sādhu sammuti mai tassa bhagavatau dassanāya|

bhiyyappayaugai bhiyyausau mattāya|

sattamiyatthai āvikaraṇa pātubhavanādiyaugai – tuyhañ‍cassa āvikaraumi, tassa mai sak‍kau pāturahausi|

casaddaggahaṇaina  pahiṇādikriyāyaugai, phāsuādināmapayaugai ca – tassa pahiṇaiyya, bhikkhūnaṃ dūtaṃ pāhaisi, kappati samaṇānaṃ āyaugau, aikassa din‍naṃ dvin‍naṃ tiṇṇaṃ pahauti, upamaṃ tai karissāmi, añ‍jaliṃ tai paggaṇhāmi| tathā tassa phāsu hauti, laukassatthau, maṇinā mai atthau, saiyyau mai atthau ic‍cādi|

‘‘catutthī’’ti vattatai|

305. namauyaugādīsvapi ca|

namausaddayaugai, sautthisvāgatādīhi ca yaugai liṅgamhā catutthīvibhatti hauti| namau tai buddha vīratthu, namau karauhi nāgassa, namatthu buddhānaṃ, namatthu baudhiyā, sautthi pajānaṃ, svāgataṃ tai mahārāja, athau tai adurāgataṃ|

‘‘kālai, bhavissatī’’ti ca vattatai|

306. bhāvavācimhi catutthī|

bhāvavācimhi catutthīvibhatti hauti bhavissatikālai| bhavanaṃ bhāvau| pac‍cissatai, pacanaṃ vā pākau, pākāya vajati, pacituṃ gacchatīti atthau| aivaṃ bhaugāya vajati ic‍cādi|

kasmiṃ atthai pañ‍camī?

307. apādānai pañ‍camī|

kimapādānaṃ?

308. yasmādapaiti  bhayamādattai vā tadapādānaṃ|

yasmā vā avadhitau apaiti, yasmā vā bhayahaitutau bhayaṃ bhavati, yasmā vā akkhātārā vij‍jaṃ ādadāti, taṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti| apanaitvā itau ādadātīti apādānaṃ|

taṃ pana tividhaṃ visayabhaidaina niddiṭṭhavisayaṃ, upāttavisayaṃ, anumaiyyavisayañ‍cāti|

apādānasañ‍ñāvisayassa kriyāvisaisassa niddiṭṭhattā niddiṭṭhavisayaṃ| yathā – gāmā apainti munayau, nagarā niggatau rājā|

aittha ca ‘‘pāpā cittaṃ nivārayai, pāpā nivāraintī’’tiādīsu yadipi kāyasaṃyaugapubbakāpagamanaṃ natthi, tathāpi cittasaṃyaugapubbakassa apagamanassa sambhavatau iminā ca apādānasañ‍ñā|

yattha pana apagamanakriyaṃ upāttaṃ ajjhāhaṭaṃ visayaṃ katvā pavattati, taṃ upāttavisayaṃ| yathā – valāhakā vij‍jautatai vij‍ju, kusūlatau pacatīti| aittha ca ‘‘valāhakā nikkhamma, kusūlatau apanaitvā’’ti ca pubbakriyā ajjhāharīyati|

anumaiyyavisayaṃ yathā – māthurā pāṭaliputtakaihi abhirūpā| aittha hi kainaci guṇaina uk‍kaṃsīyantīti anumaiyyauva kriyāvisaisau| idha pana dūrantikādisuttai vibhattaggahaṇaina apādānasañ‍ñā|

vuttañ‍ca

‘‘niddiṭṭhavisayaṃ kiñ‍ci, upāttavisayaṃ tathā|

anumaiyyavisayañ‍cāti, apādānaṃ tidhā mata’’nti||

tadaiva  calācalavasaina duvidhampi hauti|

calaṃ yathā – dhāvatā hatthimhā patitau aṅkusadhārī|

acalaṃ yathā – pabbatā autaranti vanacarā|

bhayahaitumhi – caurā bhayaṃ jāyati, taṇhāya jāyatī bhayaṃ, pāpatau uttasati, akkhātari – upajjhāyā sikkhaṃ gaṇhāti, ācariyamhā adhītai, ācariyatau suṇāti|

‘‘apādāna’’nti adhikārau|

309. dhātunāmānamupasaggayaugādīsvapi ca|

dhātavau ca nāmāni ca dhātunāmāni, taisaṃ avihitalakkhaṇānaṃ dhātunāmānaṃ payaugai, upasaggayaugai ca ādisaddaina nipātayaugai ca taṃyuttaṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

dhātuppayaugai tāva – parājiyaugai yau asayhau, pabhūyaugai pabhavau, janiyaugai jāyamānassa pakati ca| yathā – buddhasmā parājainti añ‍ñatitthiyā| himavatā pabhavanti pañ‍ca mahānadiyau, anavatattamhā mahāsarā pabhavanti, aciravatiyā pabhavanti kun‍nadiyau| kāmatau jāyatī saukau, yasmā sau jāyatai gini, urasmā jātau puttau, kammatau jātaṃ indriyaṃ|

nāmappayaugai añ‍ñatthitarādīhi yuttai – nāñ‍ñatra dukkhā sambhauti, nāñ‍ñaṃ dukkhā nirujjhati, tatau añ‍ñaina kammaina, tatau itaraṃ, ubhatau sujātau puttau ic‍cādi|

upasaggayuttaisu  apaparīhi vaj‍janatthaihi yaugai, mariyādābhividhiatthai āyaugai patinā patinidhipatidānatthaina yaugai ca| yathā – apasālāya āyanti vāṇijā, sālaṃ vaj‍jaitvāti atthau| tathā paripabbatā daivau vassati, pabbataṃ vaj‍jaitvāti atthau| mariyādāyaṃ – āpabbatā khaittaṃ| abhividhimhi – ābrahmalaukā saddau abbhuggacchati, brahmalaukaṃ abhibyāpaitvāti atthau| patinidhimhi – buddhasmā pati sāriputtau dhammadaisanāya ālapati taimāsaṃ| patidānai – ghatamassa tailasmā pati dadāti, kanakamassa hirañ‍ñasmā pati dadāti|

nipātayuttaisu ritai nānā vinādīhi yaugai – ritai saddhammā kutau sukhaṃ labhati| tai bhikkhū nānākulā pabbajitā| vinā saddhammā natthañ‍ñau kauci nāthau laukai vij‍jati| ariyaihi puthagaivāyaṃ janau, yāva brahmalaukā saddau abbhuggacchati|

apiggahaṇaina kammāpādānakārakamajjhaipi pañ‍camī kāladdhānaihi, pakkhasmā vijjhati migaṃ luddakau, itau pakkhasmā migaṃ vijjhatīti vuttaṃ hauti| aivaṃ māsasmā bhuñ‍jati bhaujanaṃ, kausā vijjhati kuñ‍jaraṃ|

casaddaggahaṇaina pabhutyādiatthai, tadatthappayaugai ca – yatauhaṃ bhagini ariyāya jātiyā jātau, yatau sarāmi attānaṃ, yatau pattausmi viñ‍ñutaṃ, yatvādhikaraṇamainaṃ, yatau pabhuti, yatau paṭṭhāya, tatau paṭṭhāya ic‍cādi|

310. rakkhaṇatthānamicchitaṃ|

rakkhaṇatthānaṃ dhātūnaṃ payaugai yaṃ icchitaṃ, taṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti, cakārādhikāratau anicchitañ‍ca| rakkhaṇañ‍caittha nivāraṇaṃ, tāyanañ‍ca| kākai rakkhanti taṇḍulā, yavā paṭisaidhainti gāvau|

anicchitaṃ yathā – pāpā cittaṃ nivārayai, pāpānivārainti, rājatau vā cauratau vā aggitau vā udakatau vā nānābhayatau vā nānāraugatau vā nānāupaddavatau vā ārakkhaṃ gaṇhantu|

‘‘icchita’’miti vattatai|

311. yaina vā’dassanaṃ|

yaina adassanamicchitaṃ antaradhāyantaina, taṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti vā, antaradhānaivāyaṃ| upajjhāyā antaradhāyati sissau, nilīyatīti atthau| mātāpitūhi antaradhāyati puttau|

vāti kiṃ? jaitavanai antarahitau| yaināti kiṃ? yakkhau tatthaiva antaradhāyati|

312. dūrantikaddhakālanimmānatvālaupadisāyaugavibhattārappayauga suddhappamaucana haitu vivittappamāṇa pubbayauga bandhana guṇavacana pañha kathanathaukākattūsu ca|

dūratthai, antikatthai, addhanimmānai, kālanimmānai, tvālaupai, disāyaugai, vibhattai, āratippayaugai, suddhatthappayaugai, pamaucanatthappayaugai, haitvatthai, vivittatthappayaugai, pamāṇatthai, pubbayaugai, bandhanatthappayaugai, guṇavacanai, pañhai, kathanai, thaukatthai, akattari ca yadavadhibhūtaṃ, haitukammādibhūtañ‍ca, taṃ kārakaṃ apādānasañ‍ñaṃ hauti, casaddaina yathāyaugaṃ dutiyā, tatiyā, chaṭṭhī ca|

aittha ca dūrantikañ‍ca dūrantikatthañ‍cāti dūrantikanti sarūpaikasaisaṃ katvā vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ, taina dūrantikatthappayaugai, tadatthai ca apādānasañ‍ñau hauti|

dūratthappayaugai tāva – kīvadūrau itau naḷakāragāmau, tatau havai dūrataraṃ vadanti, gāmatau nātidūrai| ārakā tai maughapurisā imasmā dhammavinayā, ārakā taihi bhagavā| dūratthai – dūratauva namassanti, addasa dūratauva āgacchantaṃ|

antikatthappayaugai – antikaṃ gāmā, āsan‍naṃ gāmā, samīpaṃ gāmā, gāmassa samīpanti atthau|

dutiyā, tatiyā ca, dūraṃ gāmaṃ āgatau, dūraina gāmaina āgatau, dūratau gāmā āgatauti atthau| dūraṃ gāmaina vā| antikaṃ gāmaṃ āgatau, antikaṃ gāmaina vā, āsan‍naṃ gāmaṃ, āsan‍naṃ gāmaina vā ic‍cādi|

addhakālanimmānai nimmānaṃ nāma parimāṇaṃ, tasmiṃ gamyamānai – itau mathurāya catūsu yaujanaisu saṅkassaṃ, rājagahatau pañ‍cacattālīsayaujanamatthakai sāvatthi|

kālanimmānai – itau aikanavutikappamatthakai vipassī bhagavā laukai udapādi, itau vassasahassac‍cayaina buddhau laukai uppaj‍jissati|

tvāpac‍cayantassa laupau nāma tadatthasambhavaipi avij‍jamānatā, tasmiṃ tvālaupai kammādhikaraṇaisu – pāsādā saṅkamaiyya, pāsādaṃ abhiruhitvā saṅkamaiyyāti atthau| tathā hatthikkhandhā saṅkamaiyya, abhidhammā pucchanti, abhidhammaṃ sutvā vā, abhidhammā kathayanti, abhidhammaṃ paṭhitvā vā, āsanā vuṭṭhahaiyya, āsanai nisīditvā vā|

disatthavācīhi yaugai, disatthai ca – itau sā purimā disā, itau sā dakkhiṇā disā, itau sā pacchimā disā, itau sā uttarā disā, avīcitau uparibhavaggā, uddhaṃ pādatalā, adhau kaisamatthakā ic‍cādi| disatthai – puratthimatau dakkhiṇatautiādi| aittha pana sattamiyatthai taupac‍cayaupi bhavissati|

vibhattaṃ nāma sayaṃ vibhattassaiva tadañ‍ñatau guṇaina vibhajanaṃ, tasmiṃ vibhattai – yatau paṇītatarau vā visiṭṭhatarau vā natthi, attadantau tatau varaṃ| kiñ‍cāpi dānatau sīlamaiva varaṃ, tatau mayā sutā assutamaiva bahutaraṃ, sīlamaiva sutā saiyyau| chaṭṭhī ca, chan‍navutīnaṃ pāsaṇḍānaṃ pavaraṃ yadidaṃ sugatavinayau|

āratippayaugau nāma viramaṇatthasaddappayaugau| tattha – asaddhammā ārati, virati pāpā, pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā paṭiviratau, appaṭiviratau musāvādā|

suddhatthappayaugai – laubhanīyaihi dhammaihi suddhau asaṃsaṭṭhau, mātitau ca pititau ca suddhau anupak‍kuṭṭhau agarahitau|

pamaucanatthappayaugai – parimuttau dukkhasmāti vadāmi, muttau mārabandhanā, na tai muc‍canti mac‍cunā, muttauhaṃ sabbapāsaihi|

haitvatthai, sarūpaikasaisassa gahitattā haitvatthappayaugai ca sabbanāmatau – kasmā nu tumhaṃ daharā na mīyarai, kasmā idhaiva maraṇaṃ bhavissati, kasmā haitunā, yasmā ca kammāni karaunti, yasmā tiha bhikkhavai, tasmā tiha bhikkhavai aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ, tasmā buddhausmi brāhmaṇa, yasmā kāraṇā, tasmā kāraṇā, kiṃ kāraṇā| dutiyā, chaṭṭhī ca, kiṃ kāraṇaṃ, taṃ kissa haitu, kissa tumhai kilamatha|

kaina haitunā, kaina kāraṇaina, yaina midhaikac‍cai sattā, taina nimittaina, taina vuttamic‍cādīsu ‘‘haitvatthai cā’’ti tatiyā|

vivittaṃ nāma vivaicanaṃ, tadatthappayaugai – vivittau pāpakā dhammā, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi, vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi|

pamāṇatthai tatiyā ca, āyāmatau ca vitthāratau ca yaujanaṃ, gambhīratau ca puthulatau ca yaujanaṃ candabhāgāya parimāṇaṃ, parikkhaipatau navayaujanasataparimāṇau majjhimapadaisau|

dīghasau nava vidatthiyau sugatavidatthiyā pamāṇikā kāraitabbā|

aittha ca ‘‘smāhismiṃna’’mic‍cāditau ‘‘smā’’ti ca ‘‘sau, vā’’ti ca vattamānai

313. dīghauraihi|

dīgha auraic‍caitaiti smāvacanassa sauādaisau hauti vā|

dīghasau, dīghamhā vā, [aurasau, auramhā vā] tatiyā ca, yaujanaṃ āyāmaina, yaujanaṃ vitthāraina, yaujanaṃ ubbaidhaina sāsaparāsi|

paṭhamatthavācakaina pubbasaddaina yaugau pubbayaugau, aittha ca pubbaggahaṇaṃ adisatthavuttinau pubbādiggahaṇassupalakkhaṇanti daṭṭhabbaṃ, taina parādiyaugaipi| yathā – pubbaiva mai bhikkhavai sambaudhā, itau pubbaināhausi, tatau paraṃ pac‍cantimā janapadā, dhātuliṅgaihi parā pac‍cayā, tatau aparaina samayaina, tatau uttarimpi ic‍cādi|

bandhanatthappayaugai bandhanahaitumhi iṇai pañ‍camī, tatiyā ca hauti, satasmā baddhau narau rañ‍ñā, sataina vā baddhau narau|

phalasādhanahaitubhūtassa guṇassa vacanaṃ guṇavacanaṃ, tasmiṃ guṇavacanai pañ‍camī, tatiyā ca, issariyā janaṃ rakkhati rājā, issariyaina vā, sīlatau naṃ pasaṃsanti, sīlaina vā, pañ‍ñāya vimuttimanau ic‍cādi|

pañhakathanaisu – kutausi tvaṃ, kutau bhavaṃ, pāṭaliputtatau| aittha ca kathanaṃ nāma vissaj‍janaṃ|

thaukatthai asatvavacanai karaṇai tatiyā ca, thaukā muc‍cati, thaukaina muc‍cati vā, appamattakā muc‍cati, appamattakaina vā, kicchā muc‍cati, kicchaina vā|

akattari  akārakai ñāpakahaitumhi – kammassa katattā upacitattā ussan‍nattā vipulattā uppan‍naṃ hauti cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇa, na tāvidaṃ nāmarūpaṃ ahaitukaṃ sabbattha sabbadā sabbaisañ‍ca aikasadisabhāvāpattitau|

hutvā abhāvatau nic‍cā, udayabbayapīḷanā|

dukkhā avasavattittā, anattāti tilakkhaṇaṃ||

‘‘pañ‍camī’’ti vattatai|

314. kāraṇatthai ca|

karauti attanau phalanti kāraṇaṃ, kārakahaitu [janakahaitu], tasmiṃ kāraṇatthai ca pañ‍camīvibhatti hauti, vikappaināyaṃ, haitvatthai tatiyāya ca vihitattā, ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ adassanā aivamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ, avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā, saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau, saṅkhāraniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā ic‍cādi|

kasmiṃ atthai chaṭṭhī?

315. sāmismiṃ chaṭṭhī|

kau ca sāmī?

316. yassa vā pariggahau taṃ sāmī|

pariggayhatīti pariggahau, yau yassa pariggahau āyattau sambandhī, taṃ pati sau atthau sāmisañ‍ñau hauti| vāggahaṇaina sāmitabba rujādiyaugaipi|

aittha  ca kriyābhisambandhābhāvā na kārakatā sambhavati| sāmibhāvau hi kriyākārakabhāvassa phalabhāvaina gahitau, tathā hi ‘‘rañ‍ñau purisau’’ti vuttai yasmā rājā dadāti, purisau ca patiggaṇhāti, tasmā ‘‘rājapurisau’’ti viñ‍ñāyati| aivaṃ yau yassa āyattau saivakādibhāvaina vā bhaṇḍabhāvaina vā samīpa samūhāvayavavikāra kāriyaavatthā jāti guṇa kriyādivasaina vā, tassa sabbassāpi sau sambandhādhārabhūtau visaisanaṭṭhānī āgamīvasaina tividhaupi atthau sāmī nāmāti gahaitabbau|

vuttañ‍ca

‘‘kriyākārakasañ‍jātau,

assaidaṃ bhāvahaitukau|

sambandhau nāma sau atthau,

tattha chaṭṭhī vidhīyatai||

pāratantyañhi sambandhau,

tattha chaṭṭhī bhavaititau|

upādhiṭṭhānā gamitau,

na visaisyāditau titau’’ti||

visaisanatau tāva – rañ‍ñau purisauti, aittha ca rājā purisaṃ añ‍ñasāmitau visaisaiti nivattaitīti visaisanaṃ, purisau taina visaisīyatīti visaisitabbau, aivaṃ sabbattha visaisitabbayaugai visaisanatauva chaṭṭhī|

bhaṇḍaina sambandhai – pahūtaṃ mai dhanaṃ sak‍ka, aitassa paṭivīsau, bhikkhussa pattacīvaraṃ|

samīpasambandhai – ambavanassa avidūrai, nibbānassaiva santikai|

samūhasambandhai – suvaṇṇassa rāsi, bhikkhūnaṃ samūhau|

avayavasambandhai – manussassaiva tai sīsaṃ, rukkhassa sākhā|

vikārasambandhai – suvaṇṇassa vikati, bhaṭṭhadhañ‍ñānaṃ sattu|

kāriyasambandhai – yavassa aṅkurau, maighassa saddau, puttāpi tassa bahavau, kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau|

avatthāsambandhai – khandhānaṃ pātubhāvau, khandhānaṃ jarā, khandhānaṃ bhaidau|

jātisambandhai – manussassa bhāvau, manussānaṃ jāti|

guṇasambandhai – suvaṇṇassa vaṇṇau, vaṇṇau na khīyaitha tathāgatassa, buddhassa guṇaghausau, pupphānaṃ gandhau, phalānaṃ rasau, cittassa phusanā, sippikānaṃ sataṃ natthi, tilānaṃ muṭṭhi, taisaṃ samāyaugau, sandhinau vimaukkhau, tathāgatassa pañ‍ñāpāramiṃ ārabbha, pubbacariyaṃ vā, sukhaṃ tai, dukkhaṃ tai, caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi, pañ‍ñāya paṭubhāvau, rūpassa lahutā, rūpassa mudutā, rūpassa upacayau|

kriyāsambandhai – pādassa ukkhipanaṃ, pādassa avakkhaipanaṃ vā, hatthassa samiñ‍janaṃ, pādānaṃ pasāraṇaṃ, dhātūnaṃ gamanaṃ, dhātūnaṃyaiva ṭhānaṃ, nisaj‍jā, sayanaṃ vā| tathā tassa nāmagauttādi, tassa kāraṇaṃ, tassa mātāpitarau, tassa puratau pāturahausi, tassa pacchatau, nagarassa dakkhiṇatau, vassānaṃ tatiyai māsai, na tassa upamā, kuvairassa bali ic‍cādi|

ṭhānitau – yamaidantassādaisau, au avassa|

āgamitau – puthassāgamau ic‍cādi|

sāmiyaugai – daivānamindau, migānaṃ rājā|

tabba rujādiyaugai – mahāsaināpatīnaṃ ujjhāpaitabbaṃ vikanditabbaṃ viravitabbaṃ, daivadattassa rujati, tassa raugau uppaj‍jati, rajakassa vatthaṃ dadāti, musāvādassa auttappaṃ ic‍cādi|

‘‘k‍vaci, tatiyāsattamīna’’nti ca vattatai|

317. chaṭṭhī ca|

tatiyāsattamīnamatthai k‍vaci chaṭṭhīvibhatti hauti|

yajassa karaṇai – pupphassa buddhaṃ yajati, pupphaina vā, ghatassa aggiṃ juhauti|

suhitatthayaugai – pattaṃ audanassa pūraitvā, audanaināti atthau| imamaiva kāyaṃ pūraṃ nānappakārassa asucinau pac‍cavaikkhati, pūraṃ hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇassa, pūrati bālau pāpassa|

tulyatthakimalamādiyaugai – pitussa tulyau, pitarā vā tulyau, mātu sadisau, mātarā sadisau vā, kiṃ tassa ca tuṭṭhassa, kiṃ taina tuṭṭhaināti atthau| alaṃ tassa ca tuṭṭhassa|

kattari kitappac‍cayayaugai – saubhanā kac‍cāyanassa kati, kac‍cāyanaina vā, rañ‍ñau sammatau, rañ‍ñā vā, aivaṃ rañ‍ñau  pūjitau, rañ‍ñau sak‍katau, rañ‍ñau apacitau, rañ‍ñau mānitau, amataṃ taisaṃ bhikkhavai aparibhuttaṃ, yaisaṃ kāyagatāsati aparibhuttā ic‍cādi|

sattamiyatthai kusalādiyaugai – kusalā nac‍cagītassa sikkhitā cāturitthiyau, kusalau tvaṃ rathassa aṅgapac‍caṅgānaṃ, kusalau maggassa, kusalau amaggassa, santi hi bhantai uḷārā yakkhā bhagavatau pasan‍nā, divasassa tikkhattuṃ, divasai tikkhattuṃ vā, māsassa dvikkhattuṃ ic‍cādi|

‘‘k‍vaci, chaṭṭhī’’ti ca vattatai|

318. dutiyāpañ‍camīnañ‍ca|

dutiyāpañ‍camīnamatthai ca k‍vaci chaṭṭhīvibhatti hauti|

dutiyatthai kammani kitakayaugai – tassa bhavanti vattārau, sahasā kammassa kattārau, amatassa dātā, bhin‍nānaṃ sandhātā, sahitānaṃ anuppadātā, baudhaitā pajāya, kammassa kārakau natthi, vipākassa ca vaidakau, avisaṃvādakau laukassa, pāpānaṃ akaraṇaṃ sukhaṃ, catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya pasādau, acchariyau arajakaina vatthānaṃ rāgau, acchariyau agaupālakaina gāvīnaṃ dauhau|

tathā saricchādīnaṃ kammani – mātu sarati, mātaraṃ sarati, na taisaṃ kauci sarati, sattānaṃ kammappac‍cayā, puttassa icchati, puttamicchati|

karautissa  patiyatanai ca – patiyatanaṃ abhisaṅkhārau, udakassa patikurutai, udakaṃ patikurutai, kaṇḍassa patikurutai, kaṇḍaṃ patikurutai|

pañ‍camiyatthai parihānibhayatthayaugai – assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, kiṃ nu khau ahaṃ tassa sukhassa bhāyāmi, sabbai tasanti daṇḍassa, sabbai bhāyanti mac‍cunau, bhītau catun‍naṃ āsivisānaṃ ic‍cādi|

k‍vacīti kiṃ? gambhīrañ‍ca kathaṃ kattā, kālaina dhammiṃ kathaṃ bhāsitā hauti, paraisaṃ puñ‍ñāni anumauditā, bujjhitā sac‍cāni, kaṭaṃ kārakau, pasavau ghātakau|

tathā na niṭṭhādīsu ca – sukhakāmī vihāraṃ katau, rathaṃ katavantau, rathaṃ katāvī, kaṭaṃ katvā, kaṭaṃ karauntau, kaṭaṃ karānau, kaṭaṃ kurumānau ic‍cādi|

kasmiṃ atthai sattamī?

319. aukāsai sattamī|

aukāsakārakai sattamīvibhatti hauti|

kau ca aukāsau?

320. yaudhārau tamaukāsaṃ|

ādhārīyati asminti ādhārau, adhikaraṇaṃ| kattukammasamavaitānaṃ nisaj‍japacanādikriyānaṃ patiṭṭhānaṭṭhaina yau ādhārau, taṃ kārakaṃ aukāsasañ‍ñaṃ hauti|

kaṭai  nisīdati daivadattau, thāliyaṃ audanaṃ pacati| aittha hi daivadattataṇḍulānaṃ kattukammānaṃ dhāraṇatau taṃsamavaitaṃ āsanapacanasaṅkhātaṃ kriyaṃ dhāraiti nāma|

sau panāyamaukāsau catubbidhau byāpikau aupasilaisikau sāmīpikau vaisayikauti|

tattha byāpikau nāma yattha sakalaupi ādhārabhūtau atthau ādhaiyyaina patthaṭau hauti, yasmiñ‍ca ādhaiyyabhūtaṃ kiñ‍ci byāpaitvā tiṭṭhati, taṃ yathā – tilaisu tailaṃ atthi, khīlaisu jalaṃ, dadhimhi sappīti|

aupasilaisikau nāma pac‍caikasiddhānaṃ bhāvānaṃ yattha upasilaisaina upagamau hauti, yasmiñ‍ca ādhaiyyau upasilissati al‍līyitvā tiṭṭhati, taṃ yathā – āsanai nisin‍nau saṅghau, thāliyaṃ audanaṃ pacati, ghaṭaisu udakaṃ atthi, dūrai ṭhitau, samīpai ṭhitauti|

sāmīpikau nāma yattha samīpai samīpivauhāraṃ katvā tadāyattavuttitādīpanatthaṃ ādhārabhāvau vikappīyati, taṃ yathā – gaṅgāyaṃ ghausau vasati, gaṅgāya samīpai vajau vasatīti atthau| bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai, sāvatthiyā samīpaiti atthau|

vaisayikau nāma yattha añ‍ñatthābhāvavasaina, daisantarāvacchaidavasaina vā ādhārabhāvau parikappau, taṃ yathā – ākāsai sakuṇā pakkhanti, bhūmīsu manussā caranti, jalaisu macchā, pādaisu patitau, pāpasmiṃ ramatī manau, pasan‍nau buddhasāsanai, pañ‍ñāya sādhu, vinayai nipuṇau, mātari sādhu, pitari nipuṇau ic‍cādi|

sabbaupi  cāyamādhārau padhānavasaina vā parikappitavasaina vā kriyāya patiṭṭhā bhavatīti aukāsautvaiva vuttauti vaiditabbau|

vuttañ‍caitaṃ

‘‘kiriyā kattukammānaṃ,

yattha hauti patiṭṭhitā|

‘aukāsau’ti pavuttau sau,

catudhā byāpikāditau||

byāpikau tilakhīrādi,

kaṭau aupasilaisikau|

sāmīpikau tu gaṅgādi,

ākāsau visayau matau’’ti||

‘‘chaṭṭhī, sattamī’’ti ca adhikārau|

321. sāmissarādhipatidāyādasakkhīpatibhūpasūtakusalaihi ca|

sāmī issara adhipati dāyāda sakkhipatibhū pasūta kusalaic‍caitaihi yaugai chaṭṭhīvibhatti hauti, sattamī ca| ubhayatthaṃ vacanaṃ|

gavaṃ sāmi, gausu sāmi, gavaṃ issarau, gausu issarau, gavaṃ adhipati, gausu adhipati, gavaṃ dāyādau, gausu dāyādau, gavaṃ sakkhi, gausu sakkhi, gavaṃ patibhū, gausu patibhū, gavaṃ pasūtau, gausu pasūtau, gavaṃ kusalau, gausu kusalau|

322. niddhāraṇai ca|

nīharitvā dhāraṇaṃ niddhāraṇaṃ, jāti guṇa kriyā nāmaihi samudāyatau aikadaisassa puthak‍karaṇaṃ, tasmiṃ niddhāraṇatthai gamyamānai  tatau samudāyavāciliṅgamhā chaṭṭhīvibhatti hauti, sattamī ca|

manussānaṃ khattiyau sūratamau, manussaisu khattiyau sūratamau, kaṇhā gāvīnaṃ sampan‍nakhīratamā, kaṇhā gāvīsu sampan‍nakhīratamā, addhikānaṃ dhāvantau sīghatamau, addhikaisu dhāvantau sīghatamau, āyasmā ānandau arahataṃ añ‍ñatarau ahausi, arahantaisu vā ic‍cādi|

323. anādarai ca|

anādarai gamyamānai bhāvavatā liṅgamhā chaṭṭhīvibhatti hauti, sattamī ca| akāmakānaṃ mātāpitūnaṃ rudantānaṃ pabbaji, mātāpitūsu rudantaisu pabbaji|

ākauṭayantau sau naiti,

sivirājassa paikkhatau|

mac‍cu gacchati ādāya,

paikkhamānai mahājanai||

324. kammakaraṇanimittatthaisu sattamī|

kammakaraṇanimittaic‍caitaisvatthaisu liṅgamhā sattamīvibhatti hauti|

kammatthai – bhikkhūsu abhivādainti, muddhani cumbitvā, purisassa bāhāsu gahaitvā|

karaṇatthai – hatthaisu piṇḍāya caranti, pattaisu piṇḍāya caranti, pathaisu gacchanti, saupi maṃ anusāsaiyya, sampaṭicchāmi matthakai|

nimittatthai – dīpi cammaisu hañ‍ñatai, kuñ‍jarau dantaisu hañ‍ñatai, aṇumattaisu vaj‍jaisu bhayadassāvī, sampajānamusāvādai pācittiyaṃ, musāvādanimittaṃ musāvādappac‍cayāti atthau|

‘‘sattamī’’ti adhikārau|

325. sampadānai ca|

sampadānatthai ca liṅgamhā sattamīvibhatti hauti| saṅghai din‍naṃ mahapphalaṃ, saṅghai gautami daihi, saṅghai tai din‍nai ahañ‍caiva pūjitau bhavissāmi|

yā palālamayaṃ mālaṃ, nārī datvāna caitiyai|

alattha kañ‍canamayaṃ, mālaṃ baujjhaṅgikañ‍ca sā||

326. pañ‍camyatthai ca|

pañ‍camyatthai ca liṅgamhā sattamīvibhatti hauti| kadalīsu gajai rakkhanti|

327. kālabhāvaisu ca|

kālau nāma nimaisa khaṇa laya muhutta pubbaṇhādikau, bhāvau nāma kriyā, sā caittha kriyantarūpalakkhaṇāva adhippaitā, tasmiṃ kālatthai ca bhāvalakkhaṇai bhāvatthai ca liṅgamhā sattamīvibhatti hauti|

kālai – pubbaṇhasamayai gatau, sāyanhasamayai āgatau, akālai vassati tassa, kālai tassa na vassati, phussamāsamhā tīsu māsaisu vaisākhamāsau, itau satasahassamhi, kappai uppaj‍ji cakkhumā|

bhāvainabhāvalakkhaṇai – bhikkhusaṅghaisu bhaujīyamānaisu gatau, bhuttaisu āgatau, gausu duyhamānāsu gatau, duddhāsu āgatau, jāyamānai khau sāriputta baudhisattai ayaṃ dasasahassilaukadhātu saṃkampi sampakampi sampavaidhi|

pāsāṇā sakkharā caiva, kaṭhalā khāṇukaṇṭakā|

sabbai maggā vivaj‍jainti, gacchantai laukanāyakai||

imasmiṃ sati idaṃ hauti ic‍cādi|

328. upājhādhikissaravacanai|

dvipadamidaṃ| adhikatthai, issaratthai ca vattamānaihi upaadhiic‍caitaihi yaugai adhikissaravacanai gamyamānai liṅgamhā sattamīvibhatti hauti|

adhikavacanai – upa khāriyaṃ dauṇau, khāriyā dauṇau adhikauti atthau| tathā upa nikkhai kahāpaṇaṃ, adhi daivaisu buddhau, sammutiupapattivisuddhidaivasaṅkhātaihi tividhaihipi daivaihi sabbañ‍ñū buddhauva adhikauti atthau|

issaravacanai – adhi brahmadattai pañ‍cālā, brahmadattissarā pañ‍cālāti atthau|

329. maṇḍitussuk‍kaisu tatiyā ca|

maṇḍitaussuk‍kaic‍caitaisvatthaisu gamyamānaisu liṅgamhā tatiyāvibhatti hauti, sattamī ca| maṇḍitasaddau panaittha pasan‍natthavācakau, ussuk‍kasaddau saīhatthau| ñāṇaina pasan‍nau, ñāṇasmiṃ pasan‍nau, ñāṇaina ussuk‍kau, ñāṇasmiṃ ussuk‍kau sappurisau|

kārakaṃ  chabbidhaṃ sañ‍ñā-vasā chabbīsatīvidhaṃ|

pabhaidā sattadhā kammaṃ, kattā pañ‍cavidhau bhavai||

karaṇaṃ duvidhaṃ hauti, sampadānaṃ tidhā mataṃ|

apādānaṃ pañ‍cavidhaṃ, ādhārau tu catubbidhau||

vibhattiyau pana pac‍cattavacanādivasaina aṭṭhavidhā bhavanti| yathāha

‘‘pac‍cattamupayaugañ‍ca, karaṇaṃ sampadāniyaṃ|

nissak‍kaṃ sāmivacanaṃ, bhummālapanamaṭṭhama’’nti||

iti padarūpasiddhiyaṃ kārakakaṇḍau

tatiyau|

4. samāsakaṇḍa

atha nāmamaiva añ‍ñamañ‍ñasambandhīnaṃ samāsauti nāmanissitattā, sayañ‍ca nāmikattā nāmānantaraṃ samāsau vuc‍catai|

sau ca sañ‍ñāvasaina chabbidhau abyayībhāvau kammadhārayau digu tappurisau bahubbīhi dvandau cāti|

abyayībhāvasamāsa

tatra paṭhamaṃ abyayībhāvasamāsau vuc‍catai|

sau ca nic‍casamāsauti assapadaviggahau||

‘‘upanagaraṃ’’itīdha – upasaddatau paṭhamaikavacanaṃ si, tassa upasaggaparattā ‘‘sabbāsamāvusaupasagganipātādīhi cā’’ti laupau, nagarasaddatau chaṭṭhaikavacanaṃ sa, nagarassa samīpanti añ‍ñapadaina viggahai –

‘‘nāmānaṃ samāsau yuttatthau’’ti samāsavidhānai sabbattha vattatai|

330. upasagganipātapubbakau abyayībhāvau|

upasaggapubbakau, nipātapubbakau ca nāmikau yuttatthau taihaiva attapubbakaihi upasagganipātaihi saha nic‍caṃ samasīyatai, sau ca samāsau abyayībhāvasañ‍ñau hauti| idha abyayībhāvādisañ‍ñāvidhāyakasuttānaiva vā sañ‍ñāvidhānamukhaina samāsavidhāyakānīti daṭṭhabbāni|

tattha abyayamiti upasagganipātānaṃ sañ‍ñā, liṅgavacanabhaidaipi byayarahitattā, abyayānaṃ atthaṃ vibhāvayatīti abyayībhāvau abyayatthapubbaṅgamattā, anabyayaṃ abyayaṃ bhavatīti vā  abyayībhāvau| pubbapadatthappadhānau hi abyayībhāvau, aittha ca ‘‘upasagganipātapubbakau’’ti vuttattā upasagganipātānamaiva pubbanipātau|

331. nāmānaṃ samāsau yuttatthau|

taisaṃ nāmānaṃ payuj‍jamānapadatthānaṃ yau yuttatthau, sau samāsasañ‍ñau hauti, tadañ‍ñaṃ vākyamiti ruḷhaṃ|

nāmāni syādivibhatyantāni, samassataiti samāsau, saṅkhipiyatīti atthau|

vuttañhi

‘‘samāsau padasaṅkhaipau, padappac‍cayasaṃhitaṃ|

taddhitaṃ nāma hautaivaṃ, viñ‍ñaiyyaṃ taisamantara’’nti||

duvidhañ‍cassa samasanaṃ saddasamasanamatthasamasanañ‍ca, tadubhayampi luttasamāsai paripuṇṇamaiva labbhati| aluttasamāsai pana atthasamasanamaiva vibhattilaupābhāvatau, tatthāpi vā aikapadattūpagamanatau duvidhampi labbhataiva| dvai hi samāsassa payaujanāni aikapadattamaikavibhattittañ‍cāti|

yuttau atthau yuttattau, atha vā yuttau saṅgatau, sambandhau vā atthau yassa sauyaṃ yuttatthau, aitaina saṅgatatthaina yuttatthavacanaina bhin‍natthānaṃ aikatthībhāvau samāsalakkhaṇanti vuttaṃ hauti| aittha ca ‘‘nāmāna’’nti vacanaina ‘‘daivadattau pacatī’’tiādīsu ākhyātaina samāsau na hautīti dassaiti| sambandhatthaina pana yuttatthaggahaṇaina ‘‘bhaṭau rañ‍ñau puttau daivadattassā’’tiādīsu añ‍ñamañ‍ñānapaikkhaisu, ‘‘daivadattassa kaṇhā dantā’’tiādīsu ca añ‍ñasāpaikkhaisu  ayuttatthatāya samāsau na hautīti dīpaiti|

‘‘atthavasā vibhattivipariṇāmau’’ti vipariṇāmaina ‘‘yuttatthāna’’nti vattatai|

332. taisaṃ vibhattiyau laupā ca|

idha padantaraina vā taddhitappac‍cayaihi vā āyādippac‍cayaihi vā aikatthībhūtā yuttatthā nāma, taina ‘‘taisaṃ yuttatthānaṃ samāsānaṃ, taddhitāyādippac‍cayantānañ‍ca vibhattiyau laupanīyā hauntī’’ti atthau| samāsaggahaṇādhikārai pana sati taisaṃgahaṇaina vā taddhitāyādippac‍cayanta vibhattilaupau| caggahaṇaṃ ‘‘pabhaṅkarau’’tiādīsu laupanivattanatthaṃ|

vipariṇāmaina ‘‘luttāsu, vibhattīsū’’ti vattatai, yuttatthaggahaṇañ‍ca|

333. pakati cassa sarantassa|

luttāsu vibhattīsu sarantassa assa yuttatthabhūtassa tividhassapi liṅgassa pakatibhāvau hauti| casaddaina kiṃsamudaya idappac‍cayatādīsu niggahītantassapi| nimittābhāvai naimittakābhāvassa idha anicchitattā ayamatidaisau|

sakatthavirahainidha samāsassa ca liṅgabhāvābhāvā vibhattuppattiyamasampattāyaṃ nāmabyapadaisātidaisamāha|

334. taddhitasamāsakitakā nāmaṃvā’tavaitunādīsu ca|

taddhitantā, kitantā, samāsā ca nāmamiva daṭṭhabbā tavaituna tvāna tvādippac‍cayantai vaj‍jaitvā| caggahaṇaṃ kic‍cappac‍cayaāīinīitthippac‍cayantādissapi nāmabyapadaisatthaṃ| idha samāsaggahaṇaṃ atthavataṃ samudāyānaṃ nāmabyapadaisau samāsassaivāti niyamatthanti aparai|

‘‘abyayībhāvau’’ti vattatai|

335. sau napuṃsakaliṅgau|

sau abyayībhāvasamāsau napuṃsakaliṅgauva daṭṭhabbauti napuṃsakaliṅgattaṃ| aittha hi satipi liṅgātidaisai ‘‘adhipañ‍ña’’ntiādīsu ‘‘adhiñāṇaṃ’’ntiādi rūpappasaṅgau na hauti saddantarattā, ‘‘tipañ‍ña’’ntiādīsu viyāti daṭṭhabbaṃ, na cāyaṃ atidaisau, suttai atidaisaliṅgassa ivasaddassa adassanatau| purai viya syādyuppatti|

‘‘k‍vacī’’ti vattatai|

336. aṃ vibhattīnamakārantā abyayībhāvā|

tasmā akārantā abyayībhāvā parāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ k‍vaci aṃ hauti, saisaṃ naiyyaṃ|

taṃ upanagaraṃ, nagarassa samīpaṃ tiṭṭhatīti atthau| tāni upanagaraṃ, ālapanaipaivaṃ, taṃ upanagaraṃ passa, tāni upanagaraṃ|

na pañ‍camyāyamambhāvau, k‍vacīti adhikāratau|

tatiyāsattamīchaṭṭhī-nantu hauti vikappatau||

taina upanagaraṃ kataṃ, upanagaraina vā, taihi upanagaraṃ, upanagaraihi vā, tassa upanagaraṃ daihi, taisaṃ upanagaraṃ, upanagarā ānaya, upanagaramhā upanagarasmā, upanagaraihi, upanagaraṃ santakaṃ, upanagarassa vā, taisaṃ upanagaraṃ, upanagarānaṃ vā, upanagaraṃ nidhaihi, upanagaramhi upanagarasmiṃ, upanagaraṃ upanagaraisu vā| aivaṃ upakumbhaṃ|

abhāvai – darathānaṃ abhāvau niddarathaṃ, nimmasakaṃ|

pacchāatthai – rathassa pacchā anurathaṃ, anuvātaṃ|

yauggatāyaṃ – yathāsarūpaṃ, anurūpaṃ, rūpayaugganti atthau|

vicchāyaṃ – attānamattānaṃ pati pac‍cattaṃ, addhamāsaṃ addhamāsaṃ anu anvaddhamāsaṃ|

anupubbiyaṃ – jaiṭṭhānaṃ anupubbau anujaiṭṭhaṃ|

paṭilaumai – sautassa paṭilaumaṃ paṭisautaṃ, paṭipathaṃ, pativātaṃ, attānaṃ adhikic‍ca pavattā ajjhattaṃ|

pariyādābhividhīsu āpāṇakauṭiyā āpāṇakauṭikaṃ, ‘‘k‍vaci samāsantagatānamakārantau’’ti kappac‍cayau, ākumāraihi yasau kac‍cāyanassa ākumāraṃ|

samiddhiyaṃ – bhikkhāya samiddhīti atthai samāsaiva napuṃsakaliṅgattai ca katai –

‘‘samāsassa, antau’’ti ca vattatai|

337. sarau rassau napuṃsakai|

napuṃsakai vattamānassa samāsassa antau sarau rassau hauti| aittha ca abyayībhāvaggahaṇaṃ nānuvattaitabbaṃ, taina digudvandabahubbīhīsupi napuṃsakai vattamānassa samāsantassarassa rassattaṃ siddhaṃ hauti| ‘‘aṃ vibhattīna’’mic‍cādinā amādaisau, subhikkhaṃ| gaṅgāya samīpai vattatīti upagaṅgaṃ, maṇikāya samīpaṃ upamaṇikaṃ|

itthīsu  amikic‍cāti atthai samāsanapuṃsakarassattādīsu kataisu –

‘‘abyayībhāvā, vibhattīna’’nti ca vattatai|

338. añ‍ñasmā laupau ca|

akārantatau añ‍ñasmā abyayībhāvasamāsā parāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ laupau ca hauti| adhitti, itthīsu adhikic‍ca kathā pavattatīti atthau| adhitthi passa, adhitthi kataṃ ic‍cādi, aivaṃ adhikumāri, vadhuyā samīpaṃ upavadhu, gun‍naṃ samīpaṃ upagu, aukārassa rassattaṃ ukārau| aivaṃ upasaggapubbakau|

nipātapubbakau yathā – vuḍḍhānaṃ paṭipāṭi, yai yai vuḍḍhā vā yathāvuḍḍhaṃ, padatthānatik‍kamai – yathāk‍kamaṃ, yathāsatti, yathābalaṃ karauti, balamanatik‍kamitvā karautīti atthau| jīvassa yattakau paricchaidau yāvajīvaṃ, yāvatāyukaṃ, kappac‍cayau| yattakaina atthau yāvadatthaṃ, pabbatassa parabhāgau tiraupabbataṃ, tiraupākāraṃ, tiraukuṭṭaṃ, pāsādassa antau antaupāsādaṃ, antaunagaraṃ, antauvassaṃ, nagarassa bahi bahinagaraṃ, pāsādassa upari uparipāsādaṃ, uparimañ‍caṃ, mañ‍cassa haiṭṭhā haiṭṭhāmañ‍caṃ, haiṭṭhāpāsādaṃ, bhattassa purai puraibhattaṃ, aivaṃ pacchābhattaṃ|

sākal‍latthai – saha makkhikāya samakkhikaṃ bhuñ‍jati, na kiñ‍ci parivaj‍jaitīti atthau| ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā sahasaddassa sādaisau| gaṅgāya auraṃ auragaṅgamic‍cādi|

abyayībhāvasamāsau niṭṭhitau|

kammadhārayasamāsa

atha  kammadhārayasamāsau vuc‍catai|

sau ca navavidhau visaisanapubbapadau visaisanuttarapadau visaisanaubhayapadau upamānuttarapadau sambhāvanāpubbapadau avadhāraṇapubbapadau nanipātapubbapadau kupubbapadau pādipubbapadau cāti|

tattha visaisanapubbapadau tāva – ‘‘mahanta purisa’’itīdha ubhayattha paṭhamaikavacanaṃ si, tulyādhikaraṇabhāvappasiddhatthaṃ casadda tasaddappayaugau, mahantau ca sau purisau cāti viggahai –

itau paraṃ ‘‘vibhāsā rukkhatiṇa’’ic‍cāditau ‘‘vibhāsā’’ti samāsavidhānai sabbattha vattatai|

339. dvipadai tulyādhikaraṇai kammadhārayau|

dvai padāni nāmikāni tulyādhikaraṇāni añ‍ñamañ‍ñaina saha vibhāsā samasyantai, tasmiṃ dvipadai tulyādhikaraṇai sati sau samāsau kammadhārayasañ‍ñau ca hauti|

dvai padāni dvipadaṃ, tulyaṃ samānaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ atthau yassa padadvayassa taṃ tulyādhikaraṇaṃ, tasmiṃ dvipadai tulyādhikaraṇai| bhin‍nappavattinimittānaṃ dvin‍naṃ padānaṃ visaisanavisaisitabbabhāvaina aikasmiṃ atthai pavatti tulyādhikaraṇatā| kammamiva dvayaṃ dhārayatīti kammadhārayau| yathā hi kammaṃ kriyañ‍ca payaujanañ‍ca dvayaṃ dhārayati, kammai sati kriyāya, payaujanassa ca sambhavatau, tathā ayaṃ samāsau aikassa atthassa dvai nāmāni dhārayati, tasmiṃ samāsai sati aikatthajautakassa nāmadvayassa sambhavatau|

purai  viya samāsasañ‍ñāvibhattilaupapakatibhāvā, samāsainaiva tulyādhikaraṇabhāvassa vuttattā ‘‘vuttatthānamappayaugau’’ti casadda tasaddānamappayaugau|

340. mahataṃ mahā tulyādhikaraṇai padai|

mahanta saddassa mahā hauti tulyādhikaraṇai uttarapadai parai| mahatanti bahuvacanaggahaṇaina k‍vaci mahaādaisau ca, aittha ca visaisanassa pubbanipātau visaisanabhūtassa pubbapadassa mahādaisavidhānatauva viñ‍ñāyati|

‘‘kammadhārayau, digū’’ti ca vattatai|

341. ubhai tappurisā|

ubhai kammadhārayadigusamāsā tappurisasañ‍ñā haunti|

tassa purisau tappurisau, tappurisasadisattā ayampi samāsau anvatthasañ‍ñāya tappurisauti vuttau| yathā hi tappurisasaddau guṇamativattau, tathā ayaṃ samāsaupi| uttarapadatthappadhānau hi tappurisauti| tatau nāmabyapadaisau syādyuppatti| ayaṃ pana tappurisau abhidhaiyyavacanau, paraliṅgau ca|

mahāpurisau, mahāpurisā ic‍cādi purisasaddasamaṃ, aivaṃ mahāvīrau, mahāmuni, mahantañ‍ca taṃ balañ‍cāti mahābalaṃ, mahabbhayaṃ, mahaādaisau| santau ca sau purisau cāti sappurisau, ‘‘santasaddassa sau bhai bau cantai’’ti aittha casaddaina santasaddassa samāsai abhakāraipi sādaisau, tathā pubbapurisau, parapurisau, paṭhamapurisau, majjhimapurisau, uttamapurisau, dantapurisau, paramapurisau, vīrapurisau, saitahatthī, kaṇhasappau, nīluppalaṃ, lauhitacandanaṃ|

k‍vaci  vibhāsādhikāratau na bhavati, yathā – puṇṇau mantāniputtau, cittau gahapati, sak‍kau daivarājāti|

pumā ca sau kaukilau cāti atthai samāsai katai –

‘‘laupa’’nti vattatai|

342. pumassa liṅgādīsu samāsaisu|

pumaic‍caitassa antau akārau laupamāpaj‍jatai liṅgādīsu parapadaisu samāsaisu, ‘‘aṃmau niggahītaṃ jhalapaihī’’ti makārassa niggahītaṃ| puṅkaukilau| aivaṃ pun‍nāgau|

khattiyā ca sā kañ‍ñā cāti viggayha samāsai katai –

‘‘tulyādhikaraṇai, padai, itthiyaṃ bhāsitapumitthī pumāva cai’’ti ca vattatai|

343. kammadhārayasañ‍ñai ca|

kammadhārayasañ‍ñai ca samāsai itthiyaṃ vattamānai tulyādhikaraṇai uttarapadai parai pubbabhūtau itthivācakau saddau pubbai bhāsitapumā cai, sau pumā iva daṭṭhabbauti pubbapadai itthippac‍cayassa nivatti hauti|

khattiyakañ‍ñā, khattiyakañ‍ñāyau ic‍cādi| aivaṃ rattalatā, dutiyabhikkhā, brāhmaṇī ca sā dārikā cāti brāhmaṇadārikā, nāgamāṇavikā|

pubbapadassaivāyaṃ pumbhāvātidaisau, taina ‘‘khattiyakumārī kumārasamaṇī taruṇabrāhmaṇī’’tiādīsu uttarapadaisu itthippac‍cayassa na nivatti hauti|

itthiyamic‍caiva  kiṃ? kumārīratanaṃ, samaṇīpadumaṃ|

bhāsitapumāti kiṃ? gaṅgānadī, taṇhānadī, pathavīdhātu| ‘‘nandāpaukkharaṇī, nandādaivī’’tiādīsu pana sañ‍ñāsaddattā na hauti|

tathā puratthimau ca sau kāyau cāti puratthimakāyau, aittha ca kāyaikadaisau kāyasaddau| aivaṃ pacchimakāyau, haiṭṭhimakāyau, uparimakāyau, sabbakāyau, purāṇavihārau, navāvāsau, kataranikāyau, katamanikāyau, haituppac‍cayau, abahulaṃ bahulaṃ katanti bahulīkataṃ, jīvitappadhānaṃ navakaṃ jīvitanavakaṃ ic‍cādi|

visaisanuttarapadai jinavacanānuparaudhatau thairācariyapaṇḍitādi visaisanaṃ parañ‍ca bhavati| yathā – sāriputtau ca sau thairau cāti sāriputtatthairau| aivaṃ mahāmauggal‍lānatthairau, mahākassapatthairau, buddhaghausācariyau, dhammapālācariyau, ācariyaguttilauti vā, mahausadhau ca sau paṇḍitau cāti mahausadhapaṇḍitau| aivaṃ vidhurapaṇḍitau, vatthuvisaisau|

visaisanaubhayapadau yathā – sītañ‍ca taṃ uṇhañ‍cāti sītuṇhaṃ, siniddhau ca sau uṇhau cāti siniddhuṇhau, māsau| khañ‍jau ca sau khuj‍jau cāti khañ‍jakhuj‍jau| aivaṃ andhabadhirau, katākataṃ, chiddāvachiddaṃ, uc‍cāvacaṃ, chin‍nabhin‍naṃ, sittasammaṭṭhaṃ, gatapac‍cāgataṃ|

upamānuttarapadai abhidhānānuraudhatau upamānabhūtaṃ visaisanaṃ paraṃ bhavati| yathā – sīhau viya sīhau, muni ca sau sīhau cāti munisīhau| aivaṃ munivasabhau, munipuṅgavau, buddhanāgau, buddhādic‍cau, raṃsi viya raṃsi, saddhammau ca  sau raṃsi cāti saddhammaraṃsi| aivaṃ vinayasāgarau, puṇḍarīkamiva puṇḍarīkau, samaṇau ca sau puṇḍarīkau cāti samaṇapuṇḍarīkau, samaṇapadumau| candau viya candau, mukhañ‍ca taṃ candau cāti mukhacandau| aivaṃ mukhapadumaṃ ic‍cādi|

sambhāvanāpubbapadau yathā – dhammau iti buddhi dhammabuddhi| aivaṃ dhammasañ‍ñā, dhammasaṅkhātau, dhammasammatau, pāṇasañ‍ñitā, asubhasañ‍ñā, anic‍casañ‍ñā, anattasañ‍ñā, dhātusañ‍ñā, dhītusañ‍ñā, attasañ‍ñā, atthisañ‍ñā, attadiṭṭhi ic‍cādi|

avadhāraṇapubbapadau yathā – guṇau aiva dhanaṃ guṇadhanaṃ| aivaṃ saddhādhanaṃ, sīladhanaṃ, pañ‍ñādhanaṃ, cakkhu aiva indriyaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ| aivaṃ cakkhāyatanaṃ, cakkhudhātu, cakkhudvāraṃ, rūpārammaṇamic‍cādi|

nanipātapubbapadau yathā – na brāhmaṇauti atthai kammadhārayasamāsai, vibhattilaupādimhi ca katai –

‘‘ubhai tappurisā’’ti tappurisasañ‍ñā|

344. attaṃ nassa tappurisai|

nassa nipātapadassa tappurisai uttarapadai parai sabbassaiva attaṃ hauti| tappurisaikadaisattā tappurisau, abrāhmaṇau|

na nisaidhau satau yuttau,

daisādiniyamaṃ vinā|

asatau cāphalau tasmā,

kathamabrāhmaṇauti cai?

nisaidhatthānuvādaina, paṭisaidhavidhi k‍vaci|

parassa micchāñāṇattā-khyāpanāyaupapaj‍jatai||

duvidhau cassatthau pasaj‍jappaṭisaidhapariyudāsavasaina|

tattha  yau ‘‘asūriyapassā rājadārā’’tiādīsu viya uttarapadatthassa sabbathā abhāvaṃ dīpaiti, sau pasaj‍jappaṭisaidhavācī nāma| yau pana ‘‘abrāhmaṇa amanussā’’tiādīsu viya uttarapadatthaṃ pariyudāsitvā taṃsadisai vatthumhi kāriyaṃ paṭipādayati, sau pariyudāsavācī nāma|

vuttañ‍ca

‘‘pasaj‍jappaṭisaidhassa, lakkhaṇaṃ vatthunatthitā|

vatthutau añ‍ñatra vutti, pariyudāsalakkhaṇa’’nti||

nanvaivaṃ santaipi ‘‘abrāhmaṇau’’tiādīsu kathamuttarapadatthappadhānatā siyāti?

vuc‍catai – brāhmaṇādisaddānaṃ brāhmaṇādiatthassaiva taṃsadisādiatthassāpi vācakattā, brāhmaṇādisaddā hi kaivalābrāhmaṇādiatthaisvaiva pākaṭā, bhūsaddau viyasattāyaṃ, yadā tai pana añ‍ñaina sadisādivācakaina naiti nipātaina yuj‍janti, tadā taṃsadisatadañ‍ñatabbiruddhatadabhāvaisupi vattanti, bhūsaddau viya anvabhiyādiyaugai anubhavanaabhibhavanādīsu, tasmā uttarapadatthajautakauyaivaittha naiti nipātauti na dausau, taina abrāhmaṇauti brāhmaṇasadisauti vuttaṃ hauti| aivaṃ amanussau, assamaṇau|

añ‍ñatthai – na byākatā abyākatā, asaṃkiliṭṭhā, apariyāpan‍nā|

viruddhatthai – na kusalā akusalā, kusalapaṭipakkhāti atthau| aivaṃ alaubhau, amittau|

pasaj‍jappaṭisaidhai – na katvā akatvā, akātuna puñ‍ñaṃ akarauntau|

‘‘nassa, tappurisai’’ti ca vattatai|

345. sarai  ana|

naic‍caitassa padassa tappurisai uttarapadai ana hauti sarai parai|

na assau anassau, na ariyau anariyau| aivaṃ anissarau, aniṭṭhau, anupavādau, na ādāya anādāya, anaulaukaitvā ic‍cādi|

kupubbapadau yathā – kucchitaman‍nanti nic‍casamāsattā añ‍ñapadaina viggahau, kammadhārayasamāsai katai –

‘‘tappurisai, sarai’’ti ca vattatai|

346. kadada kussa|

kuic‍caitassa nipātassa tappurisai uttarapadai kada hauti sarai parai| kadan‍naṃ| aivaṃ kadasanaṃ|

saraiti kiṃ? kudārā, kuputtā, kudāsā, kudiṭṭhi|

‘‘kussā’’ti vattatai|

347. kāppatthaisu ca|

kuic‍caitassa appatthai vattamānassa kā hauti tappurisai uttarapadai parai| bahuvacanuc‍cāraṇatau kucchitatthai ca k‍vaci tappurisai| appakaṃ lavaṇaṃ kālavaṇaṃ| aivaṃ kāpupphaṃ, kucchitau purisau kāpurisau, kupurisau vā|

pādipubbapadau ca nic‍casamāsauva, padhānaṃ vacanaṃ pāvacanaṃ, bhusaṃ vaddhaṃ pavaddhaṃ, sarīraṃ, samaṃ, sammā vā ādhānaṃ samādhānaṃ, vividhā mati vimati, vividhau kappau vikappau, visiṭṭhau vā kappau  vikappau, adhikau daivau atidaivau| aivaṃ adhidaivau, adhisīlaṃ, sundarau gandhau sugandhau, kucchitau gandhau duggandhau, saubhanaṃ kataṃ sukataṃ, asaubhanaṃ kataṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ ic‍cādi|

yai idha avihitalakkhaṇā nāmanipātaupasaggā, taisaṃ ‘‘nāmānaṃ samāsau’’ti yaugavibhāgaina samāsau daṭṭhabbau| yathā – apunagaiyyā gāthā, acandamul‍laukikāni mukhāni, assaddhabhaujī, alavaṇabhaujītiādīsu ayuttatthattā nāñ‍ñaina samāsau|

tathā diṭṭhau pubbanti diṭṭhapubbau tathāgataṃ| aivaṃ sutapubbau dhammaṃ, gatapubbau maggaṃ, kammani diṭṭhā pubbanti diṭṭhapubbā daivā taina| aivaṃ sutapubbā dhammā, gatapubbā disā, pahārau, parābhavau, vihārau, āhārau, upahārau ic‍cādi|

kammadhārayasamāsau|

digusamāsa

atha digusamāsau vuc‍catai|

tayau laukā samāhaṭā cittaina sampiṇḍitā, tiṇṇaṃ laukānaṃ samāhārauti vā atthai –

‘‘nāmānaṃ samāsau yuttatthau’’ti vattamānai ‘‘dvipadai’’tiādinā kammadhārayasamāsau, tatau samāsasañ‍ñāyaṃ, vibhattilaupai, pakatibhāvai ca katai –

‘‘kammadhārayau’’ti vattatai|

348. saṅkhyāpubbau  digu|

saṅkhyāpubbau kammadhārayasamāsau digusañ‍ñau hauti|

dvai gāvau digu, digusadisattā ayampi samāsau digūti vuttau| atha vā saṅkhyāpubbattanapuṃsakaikattasaṅkhātaihi dvīhi lakkhaṇaihi gatau avagatauti digūti vuc‍cati, dvīhi vā lakkhaṇaihi gacchati pavattatīti digu| aittha ca ‘‘saṅkhyāpubbau’’ti vuttattā saṅkhyāsaddassaiva pubbanipātau, ‘‘ubhai tappurisā’’ti tappurisasañ‍ñā|

‘‘napuṃsakaliṅgau’’ti vattatai|

349. digussaikattaṃ|

digussa samāsassa aikattaṃ hauti, napuṃsakaliṅgattañ‍ca|

samāhāradigussaitaṃ gahaṇaṃ, tattha sabbatthaikavacanamaiva hauti, añ‍ñatra pana bahuvacanampi, nāmabyapadaisasyādyuppatti amādaisādi|

tilaukaṃ, hai tilauka, tilaukaṃ, tilaukaina, tilaukassa, tilaukā tilaukasmā tilaukamhā, tilaukassa, tilaukai tilaukamhi tilaukasmiṃ|

aivaṃ tayau daṇḍā tidaṇḍaṃ, tīṇi malāni samāhaṭāni, tiṇṇaṃ malānaṃ samāhārauti vā timalaṃ, tilakkhaṇaṃ, catussac‍caṃ, catassau disā catuddisaṃ, ‘‘sarau rassau napuṃsakai’’ti rassattaṃ, pañ‍casikkhāpadaṃ, saḷāyatanaṃ, sattāhaṃ, aṭṭhasīlaṃ, navalaukuttaraṃ, dasasīlaṃ, satayaujanaṃ|

tathā – dvai rattiyau dvirattaṃ, tissau rattiyau tirattaṃ, dvai aṅguliyau dvaṅgulaṃ, satta gaudāvariyau, tāsaṃ samāhārauti vā sattagaudāvaraṃ|

aittha ca ratti aṅguli gaudāvarīnamantassa –

350. k‍vaci samāsantagatānamakārantau|

rājādigaṇassaitaṃ gahaṇaṃ, taina samāsantagatānaṃ rājādīnaṃ nāmānaṃ antau k‍vaci akārau hautīti atthau| kāraggahaṇaina bahubbīhādimhi samāsantai k‍vaci kappac‍cayau hauti, surabhi su du pūtīhi gandhantassikārau ca|

atha vā a ca kau ca akā, rakārau padasandhikarau, taina k‍vaci samāsantagatānamantau hutvā a ka ic‍caitai pac‍cayā hauntīti atthau| taina pañ‍ca gāvau samāhaṭāti atthai samāsādiṃ katvā samāsantai appac‍cayai, ‘‘au sarai cā’’ti avādaisai ca katai ‘‘pañ‍cagava’’ntiādi ca sijjhati| ‘‘dviratta’’ntiādīsu pana appac‍cayai katai pubbasarassa ‘‘saralaupau’’tiādinā laupau|

asamāhāradigu yathā – aikau ca sau puggalau cāti aikapuggalau| aivaṃ aikadhammau, aikaputtau, tayau bhavā tibhavā, catassau disā catuddisā, dasasahassacak‍kavāḷāni ic‍cādi|

digusamāsau|

tappurisasamāsa

atha tappurisasamāsau vuc‍catai|

sau pana dutiyādīsu chasu vibhattīsu bhāvatau chabbidhau| tattha dutiyātappurisau gatanissitātītātik‍kantappattāpan‍nādīhi bhavati|

saraṇaṃ gatauti viggahai –

‘‘tappurisau’’ti vattatai|

351. amādayau parapadaibhi|

amādivibhatyantāni yuttatthāni pubbapadāni nāmaihi parapadaibhi saha vibhāsā samasyantai, sau samāsau tappurisasañ‍ñau hauti| ayañ‍ca tappurisau abhidhaiyyavacanaliṅgau|

gatādisaddā kitantattā tiliṅgā, vibhattilaupādi sabbaṃ pubbasamaṃ| sau saraṇagatau, tai saraṇagatā| sā saraṇagatā, tā saraṇagatāyau| taṃ kulaṃ saraṇagataṃ, tāni kulāni saraṇagatāni ic‍cādi|

aivaṃ arañ‍ñagatau, bhūmigatau, dhammaṃ nissitau dhammanissitau, atthanissitau, bhavaṃ atītau bhavātītau, kālātītau, pamāṇaṃ atik‍kantaṃ pamāṇātik‍kantaṃ| laukātik‍kantaṃ, sukhaṃ pattau sukhappattau, dukkhappattau, sautaṃ āpan‍nau sautāpan‍nau, niraudhasamāpan‍nau, rathaṃ āruḷhau rathāruḷhau, sabbarattiṃ saubhanau sabbarattisaubhanau, muhuttasukhaṃ|

upapadasamāsai pana vuttiyaiva tassa nic‍cattā| yathā – kammaṃ karautīti kammakārau, kumbhakārau, atthaṃ kāmaitīti atthakāmau, dhammakāmau, dhammaṃ dhāraitīti dhammadharau, vinayadharau, sac‍caṃ vadituṃ sīlamassāti sac‍cavādī ic‍cādi|

tavantumānantādikitantaihi vākyamaiva vavatthitavibhāsādhikāratau| yathā – audanaṃ bhuttavā, dhammaṃ suṇamānau, dhammaṃ suṇantau, kaṭaṃ karānau, anabhidhānatau vā, abhidhānalakkhaṇā hi taddhitasamāsakitakāti|

dutiyātappurisau|

tatiyā  kitaka pubba sadisa samūnattha kalaha nipuṇa missasakhilādīhi|

buddhaina bhāsitau buddhabhāsitau, dhammau| aivaṃ jinadaisitau, satthārā vaṇṇitau satthuvaṇṇitau, viñ‍ñūhi garahitau viñ‍ñugarahitau, viñ‍ñuppasatthau, issarakataṃ, sayaṃkataṃ, sukaihi āhaṭaṃ sukāhataṃ, rañ‍ñā hatau rājahatau, raugapīḷitau, aggidaḍḍhau, sappadaṭṭhau, sal‍laina viddhau sal‍laviddhau, icchāya apakatau icchāpakatau, sīlaina sampan‍nau sīlasampan‍nau| aivaṃ sukhasahagataṃ, ñāṇasampayuttaṃ, mittasaṃsaggau, piyavippayaugau, jātitthaddhau, guṇahīnau, guṇavuḍḍhau, catuvaggakaraṇīyaṃ, catuvaggādikattabbaṃ, kākaihi paiyyā kākapaiyyā, nadī|

k‍vaci vuttiyaiva, uraina gacchatīti uragau, pādaina pivatīti pādapau| k‍vaci vākyamaiva, parasunā chin‍navā, kākaihi pātabbā, dassanaina pahātabbā|

pubbādiyaugai – māsaina pubbau māsapubbau| aivaṃ mātusadisau, pitusamau, aikūnavīsati, sīlavikalau, asikalahau, vācānipuṇau, yāvakālikasaṃmissaṃ, vācāsakhilau, satthārā sadisau satthukappau, puñ‍ñaina atthikau puñ‍ñatthikau, guṇādhikau, guḷaina saṃsaṭṭhau audanau guḷaudanau, khīraudanau, assaina yuttau rathau assarathau, maggacittaṃ, jambuyā pañ‍ñātau lakkhitau dīpau jambudīpau, aikaina adhikā dasa aikādasa, jātiyā andhau jac‍candhau, pakatiyā maidhāvī pakatimaidhāvī ic‍cādi|

tatiyātappurisau|

catutthī  tadatthaatthahitadaiyyādīhi|

tadatthai – kathinassa dussaṃ kathinadussaṃ, kathinacīvaratthāyāti atthau| aivaṃ cīvaradussaṃ, cīvaramūlyaṃ, yāguyā atthāya taṇḍulā yāgutaṇḍulā, bhattataṇḍulā, saṅghassatthāya bhattaṃ saṅghabhattaṃ, āgantukānamatthāya bhattaṃ āgantukabhattaṃ| aivaṃ gamikabhattaṃ, pāsādāya dabbaṃ pāsādadabbaṃ|

atthai bhikkhusaṅghassatthāya vihārau bhikkhusaṅghatthau vihārau, bhikkhusaṅghatthā yāgu, bhikkhusaṅghatthaṃ cīvaraṃ| yassatthāya yadatthau, yadatthā, yadatthaṃ| aivaṃ tadatthau, tadatthā| tadatthaṃ| aitadatthau vāyāmau, aitadatthā kathā, aitadatthaṃ sautāvadhānaṃ| kimatthaṃ, attatthaṃ, paratthaṃ, vinayau saṃvaratthāya, sukhaṃ samādhatthāya, nibbidā virāgatthāya, virāgau vimuttatthāya| tathā laukassa hitau laukahitau, buddhassa daiyyaṃ buddhadaiyyaṃ, pupphaṃ| saṅghadaiyyaṃ, cīvaraṃ| idha na bhavati, saṅghassa dātabbaṃ, saṅghassa dātuṃ ic‍cādi|

catutthītappurisau|

pañ‍camī apagamana bhaya virati maucanatthādīhi|

maithunasmā apaitau maithunāpaitau| aivaṃ palāpāpagatau, nagaraniggatau, piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau| gāmatau nikkhantaṃ gāmanikkhantaṃ, rukkhaggā patitau rukkhaggapatitau, sāsanacutau, āpattivuṭṭhānaṃ, dharaṇitaluggatau, sabbabhavaihi nissaṭau sabbabhavanissaṭau|

bhayatthādiyaugai yathā – rājatau bhayaṃ rājabhayaṃ, cauraihi bhayaṃ caurabhayaṃ, amanussaihi bhayaṃ amanussabhayaṃ, aggitau bhayaṃ aggibhayaṃ| pāpatau bhītau pāpabhītau, pāpabhīrukau, akattabbatau virati akattabbavirati| aivaṃ kāyaduc‍caritavirati, vacīduc‍caritavirati, bandhanā muttau bandhanamuttau, vanamuttau, bandhanamaukkhau, kammatau samuṭṭhitaṃ kammasamuṭṭhitaṃ, uk‍kaṭṭhuk‍kaṭṭhaṃ, aumakaumakaṃ|

k‍vaci vuttiyaiva, kammatau jātaṃ kammajaṃ| aivaṃ cittajaṃ, utujaṃ, āhārajaṃ| idha na bhavati, pāsādā patitau|

pañ‍camītappurisau|

chaṭṭhī rañ‍ñau puttau rājaputtau| aivaṃ rājapurisau, ācariyapūjakau, buddhasāvakau, buddharūpaṃ, jinavacanaṃ, samuddaghausau, dhañ‍ñānaṃ rāsi dhañ‍ñarāsi, pupphagandhau, phalarasau, kāyassa lahutā kāyalahutā, maraṇassati, rukkhamūlaṃ, ayassa pattau ayaupattau, aivaṃ suvaṇṇakaṭāhaṃ, pānīyathālakaṃ, sappikumbhau|

‘‘daivānaṃ rājā’’ti atthai samāsādimhi katai ‘‘k‍vaci samāsantagatānamakārantau’’ti akārau, tatau ‘‘syā cā’’ti āttaṃ na bhavati| daivarājau, daivarājā, daivarājaṃ, daivarājai ic‍cādi purisasaddasamaṃ| attābhāvai sau daivarājā, tai daivarājānau ic‍cādi rājasaddasamaṃ| tathā daivānaṃ sakhā daivasakhau, daivasakhā, sau daivasakhā, tai daivasakhānau ic‍cādi|

pumassa liṅgaṃ pul‍liṅgaṃ| aivaṃ pumbhāvau, pumantalaupādi|

hatthipadaṃ, itthirūpaṃ, bhikkhunisaṅghau, jambusākhā, aittha ca ‘‘k‍vacādimajjhuttarāna’’ntiādinā majjhai īkārūkārānaṃ rassattaṃ|

vibhāsādhikāratau  k‍vaci vākyamaiva, sahasā kammassa kattārau, bhin‍nānaṃ sandhātā, kappassa tatiyau bhāgau, yā ca pakkhassa aṭṭhamī, manussānaṃ khattiyau sūratamau|

yuttatthau ic‍caiva? ‘‘bhaṭau rañ‍ñau purisau daivadattassā’’ti aittha ‘‘bhaṭasambandhai chaṭṭhī’’ti añ‍ñamañ‍ñānapaikkhatāya ayuttatthabhāvatau samāsau na bhavati, ‘‘kausalassa rañ‍ñau puttau’’tiādīsu pana sāpaikkhatāya asamatthattā na bhavati, sambandhīsaddānaṃ pana nic‍caṃ sāpaikkhattaipi gamakattā samāsau, yathā – daivadattassa gurukulaṃ, bhagavatau sāvakasaṅghautiādi|

chaṭṭhītappurisau|

sattamī rūpai sañ‍ñā rūpasañ‍ñā, aivaṃ rūpasañ‍caitanā, saṃsāradukkhaṃ, cakkhumhi san‍nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, dhammai ratau dhammaratau, dhammābhirati, dhammaruci, dhammagāravau, dhammaisu nirutti dhammanirutti, dānādhimutti, bhavantarakataṃ, dassanai assādau dassanassādau, arañ‍ñai vāsau arañ‍ñavāsau, vikālai bhaujanaṃ vikālabhaujanaṃ, kālai vassaṃ kālavassaṃ, vanai pupphaṃ vanapupphaṃ| aivaṃ vanamahisau, gāmasūkarau, samuddamacchau, āvāṭakacchapau, āvāṭamaṇḍūkau, kūpamaṇḍūkau, titthanāvā, itthīsu dhuttau itthidhuttau chāyāya sukkhau chāyāsukkhau, aṅgārapak‍kaṃ, cārakabaddhau|

idha vuttiyaiva, yathā – vanai caratīti vanacarau, kucchimhi sayatīti kucchisayau, thalai tiṭṭhatīti thalaṭṭhau| aivaṃ jalaṭṭhau, pabbataṭṭhau, maggaṭṭhau, paṅkai jātaṃ paṅkajaṃ, sirai ruhatīti sirauruhaṃ ic‍cādi|

idha  na bhavati, bhaujanai mattañ‍ñutā, indriyaisu guttadvāratā, āsanai nisin‍nau, āsanai nisīditabbaṃ|

sattamītappurisau|

‘‘tadanuparaudhainā’’ti vuttattā yathābhidhānaṃ tappurisai k‍vaci ac‍cantādīsu amādivibhatyantaṃ pubbapadaṃ paraṃ sambhavati|

yathā – antaṃ atik‍kantaṃ ac‍cantaṃ, ac‍cantāni, vailaṃ atik‍kantau ativailau, rassattaṃ| aivaṃ mālaṃ atītau atimālau, pattajīvikau, āpan‍najīvikau, akkhaṃ patigataṃ nissitanti pac‍cakkhaṃ dassanaṃ, pac‍cakkhau attabhāvau, pac‍cakkhā buddhi, atthaṃ anugataṃ anvatthaṃ, kaukilāya avakuṭṭhaṃ avakaukilaṃ vanaṃ, paric‍cattanti atthau| avamayūraṃ, ajjhayanāya parigilānau pariyajjhayanau, kammassa alaṃ samatthauti alaṃkammau, vacanāya alanti alaṃvacanau, vānatau nikkhantaṃ nibbānaṃ, kilaisaihi nikkhantau nik‍kilaisau, niraṅgaṇau, kausambiyā nikkhantau nik‍kausambī, vanatau niyyātau nibbanau, ācariyatau parau pācariyau| aivaṃ payyakau, parahiyyau, gaṅgāya upari uparigaṅgaṃ| aivaṃ haiṭṭhānadī, antausamāpatti, haṃsānaṃ rājā rājahaṃsau, haṃsarājā vā, māsassa addhaṃ addhamāsaṃ, māsaddhaṃ vā, āmalakassa addhaṃ addhāmalakaṃ, āmalakaddhaṃ vā, kahāpaṇassa aḍḍhaṃ aḍḍhakahāpaṇaṃ, aḍḍhamāsakaṃ, rattiyā aḍḍhaṃ aḍḍharattaṃ, rattiyā pubbaṃ pubbarattaṃ, rattiyā pacchā pacchārattaṃ| aittha ca ‘‘k‍vaci samāsantagatānamakārantau’’ti rattisaddantassa attaṃ, ahassa pubbaṃ pubbanhaṃ| aivaṃ sāyanhaṃ, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā ahassa anhādaisau|

amādiparatappurisau|

k‍vaci  tappurisai ‘‘pabhaṅkarā’’dīsu vibhattilaupau na bhavati|

yathā – pabhaṃ karautīti atthai ‘‘amādayau parapadaibhī’’ti samāsau, ‘‘nāmānaṃ samāsau yuttatthau’’ti samāsasañ‍ñā, tatau ‘‘taisaṃ vibhattiyau laupā cā’’ti vibhattilaupai sampattai tatthaiva caggahaṇaina pubbapadai vibhattilaupābhāvau| saisaṃ samaṃ| pabhaṅkarau, amataṃ dadātīti amatandadau, raṇaṃ jahātīti raṇañ‍jahau, jutiṃ dhāraitīti jutindharau, tathā sahasākataṃ, parassapadaṃ| attanaupadaṃ, bhayatau upaṭṭhānaṃ bhayatūpaṭṭhānaṃ, parataughausau, gavaṃpatitthairau, manasikārau, pubbainivāsau, pubbainivāsānussati, majjhaikalyāṇaṃ, antaivāsī, antaivāsikau, janaisutau, urasilaumau, kaṇṭhaikāḷau, sarasijamic‍cādi|

alaupatappurisau|

tappurisasamāsau niṭṭhitau|

bahubbīhisamāsa

atha bahubbīhisamāsau vuc‍catai|

sau ca navavidhau dvipadau tulyādhikaraṇau, dvipadau bhin‍nādhikaraṇau, tipadau nanipātapubbapadau, sahapubbapadau upamānapubbapadau saṅkhyaubhayapadau disantarāḷatthau byatihāralakkhaṇau cāti|

tattha dvipadau tulyādhikaraṇau bahubbīhi kammādīsu chasu vibhatyatthaisu bhavati|

tattha  dutiyatthai tāva – ‘‘āgatā samaṇā imaṃ saṅghārāma’’nti viggahai –

352. añ‍ñapadatthaisu bahubbīhi|

samasyamānapadatau añ‍ñaisaṃ paṭhamadutiyādivibhatyantānaṃ padānamatthaisu yuttatthāni nāmāni vibhāsā samasyantai, sau samāsau bahubbīhisañ‍ñau ca hauti|

bahavau vīhayau yassa sau bahubbīhi, bahubbīhisadisattā ayampisamāsau anvatthasañ‍ñāvasaina bahubbīhīti vuttau, añ‍ñapadatthappadhānau hi bahubbīhi|

duvidhau cāyaṃ bahubbīhi tagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇātagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇavasaina, taisu yattha visaisanabhūtau atthau añ‍ñapadatthaggahaṇaina gayhati, sau tagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇau, yathā – lambakaṇṇamānayāti|

yattha pana na gayhati, sau atagguṇasaṃviñ‍ñāṇau, yathā – bahudhanamānayāti|

idha bahubbīhisaddai viya visaisanassa pubbanipātau, saisaṃ pubbasamaṃ|

āgatasamaṇau saṅghārāmau| aittha ca āgatasaddau, samaṇasaddau ca attanau atthai aṭṭhatvā dutiyāvibhatyatthabhūtai saṅghārāmasaṅkhātai añ‍ñapadatthai vattanti, tadatthajautanatthamaiva tadanantaraṃ ‘‘saṅghārāmau’’ti padantaraṃ payuj‍jati, tatau samāsainaiva kammatthassa abhihitattā puna dutiyā na hauti| idaṃsaddassa ca appayaugau, aivaṃ sabbattha| bahubbīhi cāyaṃ abhidhaiyyaliṅgavacanau|

tathā āgatasamaṇā sāvatthi, āgatasamaṇaṃ jaitavanaṃ, paṭipan‍nā addhikā yaṃ pathaṃ sauyaṃ paṭipan‍naddhikau pathau, abhiruḷhā vāṇijā yaṃ nāvaṃ sā abhiruḷhavāṇijā nāvā| aivaṃ kammatthai bahubbīhi|

tatiyatthai bahubbīhi yathā – jitāni indriyāni yaina samaṇaina sauyaṃ jitindriyau samaṇau| aivaṃ diṭṭhadhammau, pattadhammau, katakic‍cau, jitā mārā anaināti jitamārau bhagavā, paṭividdhasabbadhammau|

catutthiyatthai bahubbīhi yathā – din‍nau suṅkau yassa rañ‍ñau sauyaṃ din‍nasuṅkau rājā, upanītaṃ bhaujanaṃ assa samaṇassāti upanītabhaujanau samaṇau, upahaṭau bali assāti upahaṭabali yakkhau|

pañ‍camiyatthai bahubbīhi yathā – niggatā janā asmā gāmā sauyaṃ niggatajanau gāmau, niggatau ayau asmāti nirayau, niggatā kilaisā aitasmāti nik‍kilaisau, apaitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ asmāti apaitaviñ‍ñāṇau matakāyau, apagataṃ bhayabhairavaṃ asmāti apagatabhayabhairavau arahā|

chaṭṭhiyatthai bahubbīhi yathā – chin‍nā hatthā yassa purisassa sauyaṃ chin‍nahatthau purisau| aivaṃ paripuṇṇasaṅkappau, khīṇāsavau, vītau rāgau assāti vītarāgau, dvai padāni assāti dvipadau, dvihatthau paṭau, taivij‍jau, catuppadau, pañ‍ca cakkhūni assāti pañ‍cacakkhu bhagavā, chaḷabhiñ‍ñau, rassattaṃ, navaṅgaṃ satthusāsanaṃ, dasabalau, anantañāṇau, tīṇi dasa parimāṇamaitaisanti tidasā daivā, samāsantassa attaṃ, idha parimāṇasaddassa san‍nidhānatau  dasasaddau saṅkhyānai vattatai, ayaṃ pac‍cayau aitaisanti idappac‍cayā, kau pabhavau assāti kiṃpabhavau ayaṃ kāyau, vigataṃ malamassāti vimalau, sundarau gandhau assāti sugandhaṃ candanaṃ| aivaṃ susīlau, sumukhau, kucchitau gandhau assāti duggandhaṃ kuṇapaṃ, duṭṭhu manau assāti dummanau| aivaṃ dussīlau, dummukhau, tapau aiva dhanaṃ assāti tapaudhanau, khantisaṅkhātaṃ balaṃ assāti khantibalau, indauti nāmaṃ aitassāti indanāmau|

chandajātādīsu visaisanavisaisitabbānaṃ yathicchitattā ubhayaṃ pubbaṃ nipatati, yathā – chandau jātau assāti chandajātau, jātau chandau assātipi jātachandau| aivaṃ sañ‍jātapītisaumanassau, pītisaumanassasañ‍jātau, māsajātau, jātamāsau, chin‍nahatthau, hatthacchin‍nau|

‘‘dīghā jaṅghāyassā’’ti viggayha samāsādimhi katai –

‘‘tulyādhikaraṇai, padai’’ti ca vattatai||

353. itthiyaṃ bhāsitapumitthī pumāva cai|

itthiyaṃ vattamānai tulyādhikaraṇai padai parai pubbai bhāsitapumā itthivācakau saddau atthi cai, sau pumā iva daṭṭhabbauti pubbapadai itthippac‍cayābhāvau, bahubbīhivisayauyaṃ, upari ‘‘kammadhārayasañ‍ñai cā’’ti vakkhamānattā|

354. k‍vacādimajjhuttarānaṃ dīgharassāpac‍cayaisu ca|

k‍vaci taddhitasamāsanāmaupasaggādīsu padaisu ādimajjhuttarabhūtānaṃ sarānaṃ jinavacanānuparaudhaina dīgharassā haunti pac‍cayaisu, apac‍cayaisu paraisu, aparabhūtaisu ca|

tattha 

dīghattaṃ pākaṭānūpa- ghātādau madhuvādisu|

rassattaṃ aj‍javai itthi- rūpādau ca ka, tādisūti||

bahubbīhisamāsai sati pul‍liṅgai uttarapadantassa rassattaṃ| dīghajaṅghau purisau, tathā pahūtā jivhā assāti pahūtajivhau bhagavā| mahatī pañ‍ñā assāti mahāpañ‍ñau| ‘‘mahataṃ mahā tulyādhikaraṇai padai’’ti mahādaisau|

itthiyamiti kiṃ? khamādhanau| bhāsitapumāti kiṃ? saddhādhurau, saddhāpakatikau, pañ‍ñāpakatikau, pañ‍ñāvisuddhikau, aittha ca ‘‘k‍vaci samāsantagatānamakārantau’’ti kappac‍cayau| tulyādhikaraṇai ic‍caiva? samaṇibhattikau, kumāribhattikau, kumāribhatti|

pubbapadassaivāyaṃ pumbhāvātidaisau, taina idha na bhavati| bahudāsikau purisau| bahukumārikaṃ kulaṃ|

‘‘gāṇṭhivau dhanu assā’’ti viggayha samāsādimhi katai –

355. dhanumhā ca|

tipadamidaṃ| k‍vacisamāsantagatā dhanusaddā āpac‍cayau hauti, casaddaina dhammāditau ca, ‘‘vamaududantāna’’nti vakārau, gāṇṭhivadhanvā| aivaṃ pac‍cakkhadhammā|

k‍vacīti kiṃ? sahassathāmadhanu, pac‍cakkhadhammau, viditadhammau|

nānādumapatitapupphavāsitasānu ic‍catra – nānappakārā dumā nānādumā, nānādumaihi patitāni nānādumapatitāni, nānādumapatitāni ca tāni pupphāni cāti nānādumapatitapupphāni, taihi vāsitā nānādumapatitapupphavāsitā, nānādumapatitapupphavāsitā sānū yassa pabbatassa sauyaṃ nānādumapatitapupphavāsitasānu  pabbatau| ayaṃ pana kammadhārayatappurisagabbhau tulyādhikaraṇabahubbīhi|

tathā byālambau ambudharau byālambambudharau, tassa bindūni byālambambudharabindūni, taihi cumbitau byālambambudharabinducumbitau, tādisau kūṭau yassa sauyaṃ byālambambudharabinducumbitakūṭau ic‍cādi|

sattamiyatthai bahubbīhi yathā – sampan‍nāni sassāni yasmiṃ janapadai sauyaṃ sampan‍nasassau janapadau, sulabhau piṇḍau imasminti sulabhapiṇḍau daisau| ākiṇṇā manussā yassaṃ rājadhāniyaṃ sā ākiṇṇamanussā rājadhānī, bahavau tāpasā aitasminti bahutāpasau assamau, upacitaṃ maṃsalauhitaṃ asminti upacitamaṃsalauhitaṃ sarīraṃ, bahavau sāminau asminti bahussāmikaṃ nagaraṃ|

‘‘bahū nadiyau asmi’’nti atthai samāsādimhi katai –

samāsantaggahaṇaṃ, kappac‍cayau ca vattatai|

356. nadimhā ca|

samāsantagatā nadimhā kappac‍cayau hauti, casaddaina tuantā ca| nic‍catthaṃ vacanaṃ| nadīti caittha itthivācakānaṃ īkārūkārānaṃ parasamañ‍ñā, tatau ‘‘k‍vacādimajjhuttarāna’’ntiādinā nadisañ‍ñassa kappac‍cayai rassattaṃ, bahunadikau janapadau| aivaṃ bahujambukaṃ vanaṃ| bahunārikauti chaṭṭhībahubbīhinā siddhaṃ| bahavau kattārau asmiṃ, assāti vā bahukattukau daisau| aivaṃ bahubhattukau|

bhin‍nādhikaraṇau yathā – aikarattiṃ vāsau assāti aikarattivāsau, samānaina janaina saddhiṃ vāsau assāti samānavāsau  purisau| ubhatau byañ‍janamassa atthīti ubhabhaubyañ‍janakau, chattaṃ pāṇimhi assāti chattapāṇi purisau| aivaṃ daṇḍapāṇi, satthapāṇi, vajirapāṇi, khaggahatthau, satthahatthau, dānai ajjhāsayau assāti dānajjhāsayau, dānādhimuttikau, buddhabhattikau, saddhammagāravau ic‍cādi|

tipadau yathā – parak‍kamainādhigatā sampadā yaihi tai bhavanti parak‍kamādhigatasampadā mahāpurisā| aivaṃ dhammādhigatabhaugā, auṇītau pattatau pāṇi yaina sauyaṃ auṇītapattapāṇi, sīhassa pubbaddhaṃ viya kāyau assāti sīhapubbaddhakāyau, mattā bahavau mātaṅgā asminti mattabahumātaṅgaṃ vanaṃ ic‍cādi|

nanipātapubbapadau yathā – natthi aitassa samauti asamau bhagavā| idha ‘‘attaṃ nassa tappurisai’’ti suttai ‘‘attaṃ nassā’’ti yaugavibhāgaina nassa attaṃ| aivaṃ appaṭipuggalau, aputtakau, ahaitukau, ‘‘k‍vaci samāsanta’’ic‍cādinā kappac‍cayau, natthi saṃvāsau aitaisanti asaṃvāsā, na vij‍jatai vuṭṭhi aitthāti avuṭṭhikau janapadau, abhikkhukau vihārau, natthi aitassa uttarauti anuttarau, ‘‘sarai ana’’ti ana, tappurisaggahaṇamupalakkhaṇaṃ, athavā ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā nassa ana| aivaṃ natthi antau assāti anantaṃ, na vij‍janti āsavā aitaisanti anāsavā ic‍cādi|

paṭhamāyatthai sahapubbapadau yathā – saha haitunā yau vattataiti sahaitukau, sahaitu vā, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā  sahasaddassa sādaisau, ‘‘k‍vaci samāsanta’’ic‍cādinā kappac‍cayau ca, saha pītiyā imai vattantīti sappītikā| aivaṃ saha pac‍cayaihi vattantīti sappac‍cayā, sakilaisau, saupādānau, saparivārau, saha mūlaina uddhatau samūluddhatau rukkhau|

upamānapubbapadau paṭhamāyatthai tāva – upamānaupamaiyyabhāvappasiddhatthaṃ ivasaddappayaugau, kāyabyāmānaṃ samappamāṇatāya nigraudhau iva parimaṇḍalau yau rājakumārau sauyaṃnigraudhaparimaṇḍalau rājakumārau| ‘‘vuttatthānamappayaugau’’ti ivasaddassa appayaugau, saṅkhau viya paṇḍarau ayanti saṅkhapaṇḍarau, kākau viya sūrau ayanti kākasūrau, cakkhu iva bhūtau ayaṃ paramatthadassanatauti cakkhubhūtau bhagavā| aivaṃ atthabhūtau| dhammabhūtau, brahmabhūtau, andhau viya bhūtau ayanti andhabhūtau bālau| muñ‍japabbajamiva bhūtā ayanti muñ‍japabbajabhūtā kudiṭṭhi| tantākulakamiva jātā ayanti tantākulakajātā|

chaṭṭhiyatthai – suvaṇṇavaṇṇau viya vaṇṇau yassa sauyaṃ suvaṇṇavaṇṇau bhagavā| uttarapadalaupau, nāgassa viya assa gatīti nāgagati| aivaṃ sīhagati, nāgavik‍kamau, sīhavik‍kamau, sīhahanu, aiṇissa viya assa jaṅghāti aiṇijaṅghau, sīhassa pubbaddhaṃ viya assa kāyauti sīhapubbaddhakāyau, brahmunau viya aṭṭhaṅgasaman‍nāgatau sarau assāti brahmassarau|

vāsaddatthai  saṅkhyaubhayapadau yathā – dvai vā tayau vā pattā dvattipattā, ‘‘dvaikaṭṭhānamākārau vā’’ti dvisaddantassa āttaṃ, rassattaṃ, dvīhaṃ vā tīhaṃ vā dvīhatīhaṃ, cha vā pañ‍ca vā vācā chappañ‍cavācā| aivaṃ sattaṭṭhamāsā, aikayaujanadviyaujanāni|

disantarāḷatthau yathā – pubbassā ca dakkhiṇassā ca disāya yadantarāḷaṃ sāyaṃ pubbadakkhiṇā vidisā| aittha tulyādhikaraṇapadaparattābhāvā na pumbhāvātidaisau, ‘‘k‍vacādimajjhuttarāna’’ntiādinā disantarāḷatthai pubbapadassa rassattaṃ| aivaṃ pubbuttarā, aparadakkhiṇā, pacchimuttarā| yadā pana dakkhiṇā ca sā pubbā cāti kammadhārayasamāsau hauti, tadā pumbhāvātidaisau uttarapadatthappadhānattā, sabbanāmikavidhānampi nic‍caṃ bhavatiyaiva, yathā – dakkhiṇapubbassā, dakkhiṇapubbassamiti|

byatihāralakkhaṇau yathā – kaisaisu ca kaisaisu ca gahaitvā idaṃ yuddhaṃ pavattatīti kaisākaisi, daṇḍaihi ca daṇḍaihi ca paharitvā idaṃ yuddhaṃ pavattatīti daṇḍādaṇḍi, ‘‘k‍vacādimajjhuttarāna’’ntiādinā majjhaidīghau, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā antassikārau|

paṭhamāvibhatyatthabahubbīhi|

bahubbīhisamāsau niṭṭhitau|

dvandasamāsa

atha dvandasamāsau vuc‍catai|

sau ca duvidhau itarītarayauga samāhāratthabhaidaina|

tattha  itarītarayaugai tāva – ‘‘sāriputtamauggal‍lāna’’itīdha ubhayatthāpi paṭhamaikavacanaṃ, samuc‍cayajautanatthaṃ casaddappayaugau ca|

‘‘sāriputtau ca mauggal‍lānau cā’’ti viggahai –

357. nāmānaṃ samuc‍cayau dvandau|

nānānāmānamaiva aikavibhattikānaṃ yuttatthānaṃ yau samuc‍cayau, sau vibhāsā samāsau bhavati, dvandasañ‍ñau ca|

aittha ca samuc‍cayau nāma sampiṇḍanaṃ, sau pana atthavasaina kaivalasamuc‍cayau anvācayau itarītarayaugau samāhārau cāti catubbidhau|

tattha kaivalasamuc‍cayai, anvācayai ca samāsau na bhavati, kriyāsāpaikkhatāya nāmānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ ayuttatthabhāvatau, yathā – cīvaraṃ piṇḍapātañ‍ca pac‍cayaṃ sayanāsanaṃ adāsi, dānañ‍ca daihi, sīlañ‍ca rakkhāhi| itarītarayaugai, samāhārai ca samāsau bhavati, tattha nāmānaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ yuttatthabhāvatau|

dvai dvai padāni dvandā, dvandaṭṭhā vā dvandā, dvandasadisattā ayaṃ samāsaupi anvatthasañ‍ñāya dvandauti vuc‍cati, ubhayapadatthappadhānau hi dvandau|

nanu ca ubhayapadatthappadhānattai sati dvandai kathamaikatthībhāvau siyāti? vuc‍catai – sadisādiatthaipi saddappavattisambhavainadvin‍naṃ padānaṃ aikakkhaṇaiyaiva atthadvayadīpakattā na viraudhau, tañ‍ca dvandavisayamaiva, taisamatthadvayadīpakattā| yathā hi bhūsaddau anubhavaabhibhavādikai atthai anvabhiādiupasaggasahitauva dīpaiti, na kaivalau, aivaṃ ‘‘gavassaka’’ntiādīsu  gavādīnaṃ assādisaddantarasahitānamaiva atthadvayadīpanaṃ, na kaivalānanti tañ‍ca dvandavisayamaiva, na sabbatthāti daṭṭhabbaṃ| atha vā dvin‍nampi yathāvuttasamuc‍cayadīpakattā atthi dvandaipaikatthitāti na kauci viraudhau, tatau samāsasañ‍ñāvibhattilaupādi vuttanayamaiva, samāsainaiva catthassa vuttattā ‘‘vuttatthānamappayaugau’’ti casaddassa appayaugau|

idha dvandai ac‍citataraṃ pubbaṃ nipatati, parassaiva liṅgañ‍ca| itarītarayaugassa avayavappadhānattā sabbattha bahuvacanamaiva|

sāriputtamauggal‍lānā, sāriputtamauggal‍lānai, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaihi ic‍cādi, samaṇau ca brāhmaṇau ca samaṇabrāhmaṇā| aivaṃ brāhmaṇagahapatikā, khattiyabrāhmaṇā, daivamanussā, candimasūriyā, mātā ca pitā ca mātāpitarau, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā dvandai mātuādipubbapadukārassa ākārau| aivaṃ pitāputtā|

‘‘jāyā ca pati cāti jāyāpati’’itīdha –

‘‘k‍vacī’’ti vattatai|

358. jāyāya tudaṃ jāni patimhi|

jāyāsaddassa tudaṃ jāniic‍caitai ādaisā haunti patisaddai parai k‍vaci| tudaṃpati, jānipati, jayampatikā| aittha niggahītāgamau, ‘‘k‍vacā’’dinā rassattañ‍ca|

k‍vaci appasaraṃ pubbaṃ nipatati, yathā – candau ca sūriyau ca candasūriyā, nigamā ca janapadā ca nigamajanapadā, surā ca asurā  ca garuḷā ca manujā ca bhujagā ca gandhabbā ca surāsuragaruḷamanujabhujagagandhabbā|

k‍vaci ivaṇṇuvaṇṇantānaṃ pubbanipātau, yathā – aggi ca dhūmau ca aggidhūmā| aivaṃ gatibuddhibhujapaṭhaharakarasayā, dhātavau ca liṅgāni ca dhātuliṅgāni|

k‍vaci sarādiakārantānaṃ pubbanipātau, yathā – atthau ca dhammau ca atthadhammā| aivaṃ atthasaddā, saddatthā vā|

samāhārai pana – ‘‘cakkhu ca sautañ‍cā’’ti atthai ‘‘nāmānaṃ samuc‍cayau dvandau’’ti dvandasamāsaṃ katvā vibhattilaupādimhi katai –

‘‘napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ, aikattañ‍cā’’ti vattatai|

359. tathā dvandai pāṇi tūriya yaugga sainaṅga khuddajantuka vividha viruddha visabhāgatthādīnañ‍ca|

yathā digusamāsai, tathā samāhāradvandasamāsaipi pāṇi tūriya yaugga sainaṅgatthānaṃ, khuddajantukavividha viruddhavisabhāgatthaic‍caivamādīnañ‍ca aikattaṃ hauti, napuṃsakaliṅgattañ‍ca|

pāṇinau ca tūriyāni ca yauggāni ca sainā cāti pāṇitūriya yauggasainā, tāsamaṅgāni pāṇitūriyayauggasainaṅgāni, dvandatau parattā aṅgasaddau pac‍caikamabhisambajjhatai| khuddā ca tai jantukā caiti khuddajantukā, vividhainākāraina viruddhā vividhaviruddhā, nic‍caviraudhinau| samānau bhāgau yaisaṃ tai sabhāgā, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā samānassa saādaisau, vividhā ca tai lakkhaṇatau sabhāgā ca kic‍catauti visabhāgā| pāṇitūriyayauggasainaṅgāni ca khuddajantukā ca vividhaviruddhā ca visabhāgā cāti dvandau, idha bahuttā pubbanipātassa aniyamau, tai atthā yaisaṃ  tai pāṇitūriyayauggasainaṅgakhuddajantukavividhaviruddhavisabhāgatthā, tai ādayau yaisaṃ tai tadādayau|

ādiggahaṇaina añ‍ñauñ‍ñaliṅgavisaisita saṅkhyāparimāṇattha pacanacaṇḍālattha disatthādīnañ‍ca dvandai aikattaṃ, napuṃsakaliṅgattañ‍ca, iti pāṇyaṅgatthabhāvatau cakkhusautasaddānaṃ iminā aikattaṃ, napuṃsakaliṅgattañ‍ca katvā samāsattā nāmabyapadaisai katai syādyuppatti amādaisādi|

cakkhusautaṃ, hai cakkhusauta, cakkhusautaṃ, cakkhusautaina| aivaṃ sabbatthaikavacanamaiva| mukhañ‍ca nāsikā ca mukhanāsikaṃ, ‘‘sarau rassau napuṃsakai’’ti antassa rassattaṃ, hanu ca gīvā ca hanugīvaṃ| aivaṃ kaṇṇanāsaṃ, pāṇipādaṃ, chavimaṃsalauhitaṃ| hatthapādā maṃsalauhitānītiādīnaṃ pana itarītarayaugaina siddhaṃ| aivaṃ pāṇyaṅgatthai|

tūriyaṅgatthai gītañ‍ca vāditañ‍ca gītavāditaṃ, sammañ‍ca tāḷañ‍ca sammatāḷaṃ, sammanti kaṃsatāḷaṃ| tāḷanti hatthatāḷaṃ| saṅkhai ca paṇavau ca ḍiṇḍimau ca, saṅkhā ca paṇavā ca ḍaṃṇḍimā cāti vā saṅkhapaṇavaḍiṇḍimaṃ, paṇavādayau dvaipi bhairivisaisau|

yauggaṅgatthai yathā – phālau ca pācanañ‍ca phālapācanaṃ, yugañ‍ca naṅgalañ‍ca yuganaṅgalaṃ|

sainaṅgatthai hatthinau ca assā ca hatthiassaṃ, rathā ca pattikā ca rathapattikaṃ, asi ca cammañ‍ca asicammaṃ, cammanti saravāraṇaphalakaṃ| dhanu ca kalāpau ca dhanukalāpaṃ, kalāpauti tūṇīraṃ|

khuddajantukatthai ḍaṃsā ca makasā ca ḍaṃsamakasaṃ| aivaṃ kunthakipil‍likaṃ, kīṭapaṭaṅgaṃ, kīṭasarīsapaṃ| tattha kunthā sukhumakipil‍likā, kīṭā kapālapiṭṭhikapāṇā|

vividhaviruddhatthai  ahi ca nakulau ca, ahī ca nakulā cāti vā ahinakulaṃ| aivaṃ biḷāramūsikaṃ, antassa rassattaṃ, kākaulūkaṃ, sappamaṇḍūkaṃ, garuḷasappaṃ|

visabhāgatthai sīlañ‍ca pañ‍ñāṇañ‍ca sīlapañ‍ñāṇaṃ, samathau ca vipassanā ca samathavipassanaṃ| aivaṃ nāmarūpaṃ, hirauttappaṃ| satisampajañ‍ñaṃ, laubhamauhaṃ, dausamauhaṃ, ahirikānauttappaṃ, thinamiddhaṃ, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍camic‍cādi| ‘‘aṃmau niggahītaṃ jhalapaihī’’ti aittha ‘‘aṃmau’’ti niddaisadassanatau katthaci napuṃsakaliṅgattaṃ na hautīti daṭṭhabbaṃ, taina ādhipac‍caparivārau chandapārisuddhi paṭisandhippavattiyantiādi sijjhati|

añ‍ñauñ‍ñaliṅgavisaisitānaṃ dvandai dāsī ca dāsau ca dāsidāsaṃ, ‘‘k‍vacādī’’tiādinā majjhai rassattaṃ| aivaṃ itthipumaṃ, pattacīvaraṃ, sākhāpalāsamic‍cādi|

saṅkhyāparimāṇatthānaṃ dvandai aikakañ‍ca dukañ‍ca aikakadukaṃ, saṅkhyādvandai appasaṅkhyā pubbaṃ nipatati| aivaṃ dukatikaṃ, tikacatuk‍kaṃ, catuk‍kapañ‍cakaṃ, dīghau ca majjhimau ca dīghamajjhimaṃ|

pacanacaṇḍālatthānaṃ dvandai aurabbhikā ca sūkarikā ca aurabbhikasūkarikaṃ| aivaṃ sākuṇikamāgavikaṃ, sapākau ca caṇḍālau ca sapākacaṇḍālaṃ, puk‍kusachavaḍāhakaṃ, vainarathakāraṃ| tattha vainā tacchakā, rathakārā cammakārā|

disatthānaṃ dvandai pubbā ca aparā cāti atthai dvandasamāsaṃ, vibhattilaupañ‍ca katvā idhādiggahaṇaina aikattai, napuṃsakaliṅgattai ca katai ‘‘sarau rassau napuṃsakai’’ti rassattaṃ, pubbāparaṃ, hai pubbāpara, pubbāparaṃ, pubbāparaina, pubbāparassa ic‍cādi| aivaṃ puratthimapacchimaṃ, dakkhiṇuttaraṃ, adharuttaraṃ|

‘‘napuṃsakaliṅgaṃ, aikattaṃ, dvandai’’ti ca vattatai|

360. vibhāsā rukkha tiṇa pasu dhana dhañ‍ña janapadādīnañ‍ca|

rukkha tiṇa pasu dhana dhañ‍ña janapadādīnamaikattaṃ, napuṃsakaliṅgattañ‍ca vibhāsā hauti dvandai samāsai| aikattābhāvai bahuvacanaṃ, parassaiva liṅgañ‍ca|

tattha rukkhānaṃ dvandai assatthā ca kapitthā cāti atthai samāhārai dvandasamāsādimhi katai iminā vikappainaikattaṃ, napuṃsakaliṅgattañ‍ca| assatthakapitthaṃ, assatthakapitthā vā| aivaṃ ambapanasaṃ, ambapanasā vā, khadirapalāsaṃ, khadirapalāsā vā, dhavassakaṇṇakaṃ, dhavassakaṇṇakā vā|

tiṇānaṃ dvandai usīrāni ca bīraṇāni ca usīrabīraṇaṃ, usīrabīraṇāni vā| aivaṃ muñ‍japabbajaṃ, muñ‍japabbajā vā, kāsakusaṃ, kāsakusā vā|

pasūnaṃ dvandai ajā ca aiḷakā ca ajaiḷakaṃ, ajaiḷakā vā, hatthī ca gāvau ca assā ca vaḷavā ca hatthigavassavaḷavaṃ, hatthigavassavaḷavā vā, ‘‘k‍vacā’’tiādinā rassattaṃ, ‘‘ausarai cā’’ti avādaisau ca, gaumahiṃsaṃ, gaumahiṃsā vā, aiṇaiyyavarāhaṃ, aiṇaiyyavarāhā vā, sīhabyagghataracchaṃ, sīhabyagghataracchā vā|

dhanānaṃ dvandai hirañ‍ñañ‍ca suvaṇṇañ‍ca hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇaṃ, hirañ‍ñasuvaṇṇāni vā| aivaṃ jātarūparajataṃ, jātarūparajatāni vā, maṇimuttasaṅkhavaiḷuriyaṃ, maṇimuttasaṅkhavaiḷuriyā vā|

dhañ‍ñānaṃ  dvandai sālī ca yavā ca sāliyavaṃ, sāliyavā vā| aivaṃ tilamuggamāsaṃ, tilamuggamāsā vā|

janapadānaṃ dvandai kāsī ca kausalā ca kāsikausalaṃ, kāsikausalā vā, vaj‍jī ca mal‍lā ca vaj‍jimal‍laṃ, vaj‍jimal‍lā vā, aṅgā ca magadhā ca aṅgamagadhaṃ, aṅgamagadhā vā|
