ādiggahaṇaina añ‍ñauñ‍ñappaṭipakkhadhammānaṃ, sakuṇatthānañ‍ca dvandai vibhāsā aikattaṃ hauti, napuṃsakaliṅgattañ‍ca| kusalañ‍ca akusalañ‍ca kusalākusalaṃ, kusalākusalā vā, aivaṃ sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jaṃ, sāvaj‍jānavaj‍jā vā, hīnappaṇītaṃ, hīnappaṇītā vā, kaṇhasuk‍kaṃ, kaṇhasuk‍kā vā, sukhadukkhaṃ, sukhadukkhāni vā, paṭighānunayaṃ, paṭighānunayā vā, chāyātapaṃ, chāyātapā vā, ālaukandhakāraṃ, ālaukandhakārā vā, ratti ca divā ca rattindivaṃ, rattindivā vā, ahañ‍ca ratti ca ahaurattaṃ, ahaurattā vā, ‘‘k‍vaci samāsanta’’ic‍cādinā ākārikārānamattaṃ|

sakuṇānaṃ dvandai haṃsā ca bakā ca haṃsabakaṃ, haṃsabakā vā| aivaṃ kāraṇḍavacak‍kavākaṃ, kāraṇḍavacak‍kavākā vā, mayūrakauñ‍caṃ, mayūrakauñ‍cā vā, sukasālikaṃ, sukasālikā vā|

samāhāradvandau|

yaibhuyyaina caittha –

ac‍citappasaraṃ pubbaṃ, ivaṇṇuvaṇṇakaṃ k‍vaci|

dvandai sarādyakārantaṃ, bahūsvaniyamau bhavai||

dvandasamāsau niṭṭhitau|

pubbuttarubhayañ‍ñattha-ppadhānattā  catubbidhau|

samāsauyaṃ digu kamma-dhārayaihi ca chabbidhau||

duvidhau abyayībhāvau, navadhā kammadhārayau|

digu dudhā tappurisau, aṭṭhadhā navadhā bhavai|

bahubbīhi dvidhā dvandau, samāsau caturaṭṭhadhāti||

iti padarūpasiddhiyaṃ samāsakaṇḍau

catutthau|

5. taddhitakaṇḍa

apac‍cataddhita

atha  nāmatau aiva vibhatyantā apac‍cādiatthavisaisai taddhituppattīti nāmatau paraṃ taddhitavidhānamārabhīyatai|

tattha tasmā tividhaliṅgatau paraṃ hutvā hitā sahitāti taddhitā, ṇādipac‍cayānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ, taisaṃ vā nāmikānaṃ hitā upakārā taddhitāti anvatthabhūtaparasamañ‍ñāvasaināpi ṇādippac‍cayāva taddhitā nāma|

‘‘vasiṭṭhassa apac‍ca’’nti viggahai –

‘‘liṅgañ‍ca nipac‍catai’’ti itau liṅgaggahaṇamanuvattatai|

361. vā ṇa’pac‍cai|

chaṭṭhiyantatau liṅgamhā ṇappac‍cayau hauti vikappaina ‘‘tassa apac‍ca’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

sau ca –

362. dhātuliṅgaihi parā pac‍cayā|

dhātūhi, liṅgaihi ca pac‍cayā parāva hauntīti paribhāsatau apac‍catthasambandhiliṅgatau chaṭṭhiyantāyaiva parau hauti| paṭic‍ca aitasmā atthau aitīti pac‍cayau, patīyanti anaina atthāti vā pac‍cayau, ‘‘vuttatthānamappayaugau’’ti apac‍casaddassa appayaugau, ‘‘taisaṃ vibhattiyau laupā cai’’ti vibhattilaupau ca|

363. taisaṃ  ṇau laupaṃ|

taisaṃ taddhitappac‍cayānaṃ ṇānubandhānaṃ ṇakārau laupamāpaj‍jatai|

364. vuddhādisarassa vāsaṃyaugantassa saṇai ca|

ādisarassa vā ādibyañ‍janassa vā asaṃyaugantassa vuddhi hauti saṇakārappac‍cayai parai| saṃyaugau antau assāti saṃyaugantau, tadañ‍ñau asaṃyaugantau| atha vā vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau, tadā ca pakatibhūtai liṅgai sarānamādisarassa asaṃyaugantassa vuddhi hauti saṇakārai taddhitappac‍cayai paraiti atthau|

taina –

vāsiṭṭhādīsu nic‍cāyaṃ, anic‍cauḷumpikādisu|

na vuddhi nīlapītādau, vavatthitavibhāsatau||

casaddaggahaṇamavadhāraṇatthaṃ|

tassā vuddhiyā aniyamappasaṅgai niyamatthaṃ paribhāsamāha|

365. ayuvaṇṇānañ‍cāyau vuddhī|

taisaṃ akāraivaṇṇuvaṇṇānamaiva yathāk‍kamaṃ ā aiauic‍caitai vuddhiyau haunti| casaddaggahaṇamavuddhisampiṇḍanatthaṃ, avadhāraṇatthaṃ vā| i ca u ca yu, yu aiva vaṇṇā yuvaṇṇā, a ca yuvaṇṇā ca ayuvaṇṇā| ā ca ai ca au ca āyau| puna vuddhiggahaṇaṃ ‘‘naigamajānapadā’’tiādīsu uttarapadavuddhibhāvatthaṃ, aittha ca ‘‘ayuvaṇṇāna’’nti ṭhānaniyamavacanaṃ āyaunaṃ vuddhibhāvappasaṅganivattanatthaṃ|

yathā  hi katavuddhīnaṃ, puna vuddhi na hautiha|

tathā sabhāvavuddhīnaṃ, āyaunaṃ puna vuddhi na||

tatau akārassa ākārau vuddhi, ‘‘saralaupaumādaisappac‍cayādimhi saralaupai tu pakatī’’ti saralaupapakatibhāvā, ‘‘nayai paraṃ yuttai’’ti paranayanaṃ katvā taddhitattā ‘‘taddhitasamāsa’’ic‍cādinā nāmabyapadaisai katai purai viya syādyuppatti|

vāsiṭṭhau, vasiṭṭhassa puttau vā, vāsiṭṭhā ic‍cādi purisasaddasamaṃ| tassa nattupanattādayaupi tadupacāratau vāsiṭṭhāyaiva| aivaṃ sabbattha gauttataddhitai paṭhamappakatitauyaiva pac‍cayau hauti|

itthiyaṃ ṇappac‍cayantattā ‘‘ṇava ṇika ṇaiyya ṇa ntūhī’’ti vāsiṭṭhasaddatau īpac‍cayau| saralaupādiṃ katvā itthippac‍cayantattā ‘‘taddhitasamāsa’’ic‍cādisuttai caggahaṇaina nāmabyapadaisai katai syādyuppatti, vāsiṭṭhī kañ‍ñā, vāsiṭṭhī, vāsiṭṭhiyau ic‍cādi itthisaddasamaṃ|

napuṃsakai vāsiṭṭhaṃ apac‍caṃ, vāsiṭṭhāni apac‍cāni ic‍cādi cittasaddasamaṃ| aivaṃ uparipi taddhitantassa tiliṅgatā vaiditabbā|

bhāradvājassa puttau bhāradvājau, vaisāmittassa puttau vaisāmittau, gautamassa puttau gautamau| aittha ca ayuvaṇṇattābhāvā ākārādīnaṃ na vuddhi hauti| vasudaivassa apac‍caṃ vāsudaivau, baladaivau| ‘‘cittakau’’tiādīsu pana saṃyaugantattā vuddhi na bhavati|

‘‘vacchassa apac‍ca’’nti viggahai ṇamhi sampattai ‘‘vā ṇa’pac‍cai’’ti itau vāti taddhitavidhānai sabbattha vattatai, taina sabbattha vākyavuttiyau bhavanti| ‘‘apac‍cai’’ti padaṃ yāva saṃsaṭṭhaggahaṇāva vattatai|

366. ṇāyana  ṇāna vacchāditau|

vaccha kac‍caic‍caivamāditau chaṭṭhiyantatau gauttagaṇatau ṇāyana ṇānaic‍catai pac‍cayā haunti vā ‘‘tassāpac‍ca’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai| sabbattha ṇakārānubandhau vuddhatthau, saisaṃ pubbasamaṃ, saṃyaugantattā vuddhiabhāvauva visaisau|

vacchāyanau vacchānau vacchassa puttau vā| aivaṃ kac‍cassa puttau kac‍cāyanau kac‍cānau, mauggal‍lassa puttau mauggal‍lāyanau mauggal‍lānau| aivaṃ aggivaissāyanau aggivaissānau, kaṇhāyanau kaṇhānau, sākaṭāyanau sākaṭānau, muñ‍cāyanau muñ‍cānau, kuñ‍jāyanau kuñ‍jānau ic‍cādi| ākatigaṇau’yaṃ|

‘‘kattikāya apac‍ca’’nti viggahai –

367. ṇaiyyau kattikādīhi|

ādisaddauyaṃ pakārai vattatai| kattikā vinatārauhiṇīic‍caivamādīhi itthiyaṃ vattamānaihi liṅgaihi ṇaiyyappac‍cayau hauti vā ‘‘tassāpac‍ca’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai|

vibhattilaupai ‘‘pakati cassa sarantassā’’ti pakatibhāvau| kattikaiyyau, kattikāya puttau vā| aivaṃ vinatāya apac‍caṃ vainataiyyau, ikārassaikārau vuddhi, rauhiṇiyā puttau rauhiṇaiyyau, gaṅgāya apac‍caṃ gaṅgaiyyau, bhaginiyā puttau bhāginaiyyau, nadiyāputtau nādaiyyau| aivaṃ antaiyyau, āhaiyyau, kāmaiyyau| suciyā  apac‍caṃ saucaiyyau, aittha ukārassaukārau vuddhi, bālāya apac‍caṃ bālaiyyau ic‍cādi|

‘‘dakkhassāpac‍ca’’nti viggahai ṇamhi sampattai –

368. atau ṇi vā|

akārantatau liṅgamhā ṇipac‍cayau hauti vā ‘‘tassāpac‍ca’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai| dakkhi, dakkhī, dakkhayau, dauṇassa apac‍caṃ dauṇi| aivaṃ vāsavi, sakyaputti, nāṭaputti, dāsaputti, dāsarathi| vāruṇi, kaṇḍi, bāladaivi, pāvaki, jinadattassa apac‍caṃ jainadatti, suddhaudani, anuruddhi ic‍cādi|

puna vāggahaṇaina apac‍catthai ṇikappac‍cayau, aditiāditau ṇyappac‍cayau ca| yathā – sakyaputtassa puttau sakyaputtikau, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā kakārassa yakārau, sakyaputtiyau| aivaṃ nāṭaputtikau, jainadattikau, vimātuyā puttau vaimātikau|

‘‘aditiyā puttau’’ti atthai ṇyappac‍cayau, vuddhi ca|

369. avaṇṇau yai laupañ‍ca|

avaṇṇau taddhitabhūtai yappac‍cayai parai laupamāpaj‍jatai| casaddaina ivaṇṇaupīti ikāralaupau, ‘‘yavataṃ talanadakārānaṃ byañ‍janāni ca la ña jakāratta’’nti tyakārasaṃyaugassa cakārau, ‘‘paradvaibhāvau ṭhānai’’ti dvittaṃ, ādic‍cau| aivaṃ ditiyā puttau daic‍cau|

‘‘kuṇḍaniyā puttau’’ti atthai ṇyappac‍cayai katai –

‘‘k‍vacādimajjhuttaraisū’’ti vattatai|

370. taisu  vuddhilaupāgamavikāraviparītādaisā ca|

taisu ādimajjhuttaraisu avihitalakkhaṇaisu jinavacanānuparaudhaina k‍vaci vuddhi laupa āgama vikāra viparītaādaisā hauntīti saṃyaugantattaipi ādivuddhi, ikāralaupai nyassañādaisau, kauṇḍañ‍ñau, kurunau puttau kaurabyau, aitthāpi tainaiva ukārassa avādaisau, bhātunau puttau bhātabyau|

‘‘upagussa apac‍ca’’nti viggahai –

371. ṇavaupagvādīhi|

upagu manuic‍caivamādīhi ukārantaihi gauttagaṇaihi ṇavappac‍cayau hauti vā ‘‘tassāpac‍ca’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai| ādisaddassa caittha pakāravācakattā ukārantatauyaivāyaṃ| aupagavau, aupagavī, aupagavaṃ, manunau apac‍caṃ mānavau, ‘‘mānusau’’ti ṇappac‍cayai, sāgamai ca katai rūpaṃ, bhaggunau apac‍caṃ bhaggavau, paṇḍunau apac‍caṃ paṇḍavau, upavindussa apac‍caṃ aupavindavau ic‍cādi|

‘‘vidhavāya apac‍ca’’nti atthai –

372. ṇaira vidhavāditau|

vidhavāditau ṇairappac‍cayau hauti vā apac‍catthai| vigatau dhavau pati aitissāti vidhavā, vaidhavairau, bandhukiyā abhisāriṇiyā puttau bandhukairau, samaṇassa upajjhāyassa puttau puttaṭṭhāniyattāti sāmaṇairau, nāḷikairau ic‍cādi|

apac‍cataddhitaṃ|

saṃsaṭṭhādianaikatthataddhita

‘‘tilaina  saṃsaṭṭha’’nti viggahai –

373. yaina vā saṃsaṭṭhaṃ tarati carati vahati ṇikau|

yaina vā saṃsaṭṭhaṃ, yaina vā tarati, yaina vā carati, yaina vā vahati, tatau tatiyantatau liṅgamhā taisu saṃsaṭṭhādīsvatthaisu ṇikappac‍cayau hauti vā| tailikaṃ bhaujanaṃ, tilaina abhisaṅkhatanti atthau| tailikī yāgu| guḷaina saṃsaṭṭhaṃ aigāḷikaṃ| aivaṃ ghātikaṃ, dādhikaṃ, māricikaṃ, lauṇikaṃ|

nāvāya taratīti nāvikau, uḷumpaina taratīti auḷumpikau, vuddhiabhāvapakkhai uḷumpikau| aivaṃ kul‍likau, gaupucchikau| sakaṭaina caratīti sākaṭikau| aivaṃ pādikau, daṇḍikau, dhammaina carati pavattatīti dhammikau| sīsaina vahatīti sīsikau, vāggahaṇaina īkārassa vuddhi na hauti| aivaṃ aṃsikau, khandhikau, hatthikau, aṅgulikau|

puna vāggahaṇaina añ‍ñatthaisupi ṇikappac‍cayau, paradāraṃ gacchatīti pāradārikau, pathaṃ gacchatīti pathikau|

‘‘vinayamadhītai, avaic‍cādhītai’’ti vā viggahai –

‘‘ṇikau’’ti vattatai|

374. tamadhītai tainakatādisan‍nidhānaniyaugasippabhaṇḍajīvikatthaisu ca|

catuppadamidaṃ| tamadhītaiti atthai, taina katādīsvatthaisu ca tamhi san‍nidhānau, tattha niyuttau, tamassa sippaṃ, tamassa bhaṇḍaṃ, tamassa jīvikā ic‍caitaisvatthaisu ca dutiyādivibhatyantaihi liṅgaihi ṇikappac‍cayau hauti vā| vainayikau| aivaṃ suttantikau, ābhidhammikau|

‘‘byākaraṇamadhītai’’ti atthai ṇikappac‍cayādimhi katai –

‘‘vuddhādisarassa vāsaṃyaugantassa saṇai cā’’ti vattamānai –

375. māyūnamāgamau ṭhānai|

i uic‍caibhaisaṃ ādisarānaṃ asaṃyaugantānaṃ mā vuddhi hauti saṇai, tatraiva vuddhi āgamau hauti ca ṭhānaiti aikāravuddhāgamau|

‘‘ṭhānai’’ti vacanā caittha, yūnamādaisabhūtatau|

yavaihi pubbaiva aiau-vuddhiyau haunti āgamā||

yakārassa dvibhāvau|

vaiyyākaraṇikau, nyāyamadhītaiti naiyyāyikau| aivaṃ tak‍kikau, vaidikau, naimittikau, kāyaina katau payaugau kāyikau, kāyaina kataṃ kammaṃ kāyikaṃ, vacasā kataṃ kammaṃ vācasikaṃ| aivaṃ mānasikaṃ, aittha ca ‘‘sasarai vāgamau’’ti suttai vavatthitavāsaddaina pac‍cayai paraipi sāgamau, thairaihi katā saṅgīti thairikā| aivaṃ pañ‍casatikā, sattasatikā, aittha ‘‘ṇavaṇikā’’disuttai anuvattitavāggahaṇaina īpac‍cayau na hauti|

san‍nidhānatthai sarīrai san‍nidhānā vaidanā sārīrikā, sārīrikaṃ dukkhaṃ| aivaṃ mānasikā, mānasikaṃ|

niyuttatthai  dvārai niyuttau dauvārikau, aittha ‘‘māyūnamāgamau ṭhānai’’ti vakāratau pubbaiaukārāgamau| aivaṃ bhaṇḍāgārikau, nāgarikau, navakammikau, vanakammikau, ādikammikau, audarikau, rathikau, pathikau, upāyai niyuttau aupāyikau, caitasi niyuttā caitasikā|

sippatthai vīṇāvādanaṃ vīṇā, vīṇā assa sippaṃ vaiṇikau| aivaṃ pāṇavikau, maudiṅgikau, vaṃsikau|

bhaṇḍatthai gandhau assa bhaṇḍanti gandhikau| aivaṃ tailikau, gauḷikau, pūvikau, paṇṇikau, tambūlikau, lauṇikau|

jīvikatthai urabbhaṃ hantvā jīvati, urabbhamassa jīvikāti vā aurabbhikau| aivaṃ māgavikau, aittha vakārāgamau| sūkarikau, sākuṇikau, macchikau ic‍cādi|

‘‘taina katādī’’ti aittha ādiggahaṇaina taina hataṃ, taina baddhaṃ, taina kītaṃ, taina dibbati, sau assa āvudhau, sau assa ābādhau, tattha pasan‍nau, tassa santakaṃ, tamassa parimāṇaṃ, tassa rāsi, taṃ arahati, tamassa sīlaṃ, tattha jātau, tattha vasati, tatra viditau, tadatthāya saṃvattati, tatau āgatau, tatau sambhūtau, tadassa payaujananti aivamādiatthai ca ṇikappac‍cayau hauti| yathā – jālaina hatau, hanatīti vā jālikau| aivaṃ bāḷisikau, vākarikau, suttaina baddhau suttikau, varattāya baddhau vārattikau nāgau|

vatthaina  kītaṃ bhaṇḍaṃ vatthikaṃ| aivaṃ kumbhikaṃ, phālikaṃ, sauvaṇṇikaṃ, sātikaṃ| akkhaina dibbatīti akkhikau| aivaṃ sālākikau, tindukikau, ambaphalikau| cāpau assa āvudhauti cāpikau| aivaṃ taumarikau, muggarikau, mausalikau|

vātau assa ābādhauti vātikau| aivaṃ saimhikau, pittikau|

buddhai pasan‍nau buddhikau| aivaṃ dhammikau, saṅghikau| buddhassa santakau buddhikau| aivaṃ dhammikau, saṅghikau vihārau, saṅghikā bhūmi, saṅghikaṃ cīvaraṃ, puggalikaṃ|

kumbhau assa parimāṇanti kumbhikaṃ| aivaṃ khārikaṃ, dauṇikaṃ| kumbhassa rāsi kumbhikau| kumbhaṃ arahatīti kumbhikau| aivaṃ dauṇikau, aṭṭhamāsikau, kahāpaṇikau, āsītikā gāthā, nāvutikā, sātikaṃ, sāhassikaṃ| sandiṭṭhamarahatīti sandiṭṭhikau, ‘‘aihi passā’’ti imaṃ vidhiṃ arahatīti aihipassikau|

sīlatthai paṃsukūladhāraṇaṃ paṃsukūlaṃ, taṃ sīlamassāti paṃsukūlikau| aivaṃ taicīvarikau, aikāsanai bhaujanasīlau aikāsanikau, rukkhamūlai vasanasīlau rukkhamūlikau, tathā ārañ‍ñikau, sausānikau|

jātatthai apāyai jātau āpāyikau| aivaṃ nairayikau, sāmuddikau macchau, vassaisu jātau vassikau, vassikā, vassikaṃ pupphaṃ, sāradikau, haimantikau, vāsantikau, cātuddasikau, rājagahai jātau, rājagahai  vasatīti vā rājagahikau janau, magadhaisu jātau, vasatīti vā māgadhikau, māgadhikā, māgadhikaṃ, sāvatthiyaṃ jātau, vasatīti vā sāvatthikau, kāpilavatthikau, vaisālikau|

laukai viditau laukikau, laukāya saṃvattatītipi laukikau| tathā mātitau āgataṃ mātikaṃ, pititau āgataṃ paittikaṃ nāmaṃ|

sambhūtatthai mātitau sambhūtaṃ mattikaṃ| aivaṃ paittikaṃ| upadhitassa payaujanaṃ aupadhikaṃ|

sakatthaipi asaṅkhārauyaiva asaṅkhārikaṃ| aivaṃ sasaṅkhārikaṃ, nāmamaiva nāmikaṃ| aivaṃ ākhyātikaṃ, aupasaggikaṃ, naipātikaṃ, catumahārājai bhatti aitaisanti cātumahārājikā| aivaṃ añ‍ñatthaipi yaujaitabbaṃ|

‘‘kasāvaina ratta’’nti viggahai –

376. ṇa rāgā tainarattaṃ tassaidamañ‍ñatthaisu ca|

rāgatthavācakā liṅgamhā ‘‘taina ratta’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai, ‘‘tassai’’ti chaṭṭhiyantatau ‘‘ida’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai ca añ‍ñatthaisu ca ṇappac‍cayau hauti vā|

kāsāvaṃ vatthaṃ| aivaṃ kāsāyaṃ, kusumbhaina rattaṃ kausumbhaṃ, haliddiyā rattaṃ hāliddaṃ, pattaṅgaṃ, mañ‍jiṭṭhaṃ, kuṅkumaṃ, nīlaina rattaṃ nīlaṃ| aivaṃ pītaṃ|

idamatthai  mahiṃsassa idaṃ māhiṃsaṃ maṃsaṃ, dadhi sappi cammādikaṃ vā, sūkarassa idaṃ sūkaraṃ, kac‍cāyanassa idaṃ kac‍cāyanaṃ byākaraṇaṃ| aivaṃ saugataṃ sāsanaṃ|

‘‘isissa ida’’nti atthai ṇappac‍cayai katai vuddhimhi sampattai –

‘‘saṇai, yūnamāgamau ṭhānai’’ti ca vattatai|

377. āttañ‍ca|

i uic‍caitaisaṃ ādisarānaṃ āttañ‍ca hauti saṇakārappac‍cayai parai, casaddaina rikārāgamau ca ṭhānaiti ikārassa āttaṃ|

ṭhānādhikāratau āttaṃ, isūsabhaujādinaṃ|

isissa tu rikārāga-mau cāttānantarai bhavai||

ārisyaṃ, usabhassa idaṃ āsabhaṃ ṭhānaṃ, āsabhī vācā|

añ‍ñatthaggahaṇaina pana avidūrabhavau, tatra bhavau, tatra jātau, tatau āgatau, sau assa nivāsau, tassa issarau, kattikādīhi niyuttau māsau, sāssa daivatā, tamavaic‍cādhītai, tassa visayau daisau, tasmiṃ daisai atthi, taina nibbattaṃ, taṃ arahati, tassa vikārau, tamassa parimāṇanti ic‍caivamādīsvatthaisu ca ṇappac‍cayau| yathā – vidisāya avidūrai bhavau vaidisau gāmau, udumbarassa avidūrai bhavaṃ audumbaraṃ vimānaṃ|

bhavatthai manasi bhavaṃ mānasaṃ sukhaṃ, sāgamau| sarai bhavau sārasau sakuṇau, sārasā sakuṇī, sārasaṃ pupphaṃ, urasi bhavau aurasau puttau, urasi saṃvaḍḍhitattā, mittai bhavā maittā, maittī vā, purai bhavā paurī vācā|

jātādīsu pāvusai jātau pāvusau maighau, pāvusā ratti, pāvusaṃ abbhaṃ, saradai jātau sāradau māsau, sāradā ratti, sāradaṃ pupphaṃ| aivaṃ sisirau, haimantau, vasantau, vimhau, mathurāyaṃ jātau māthurau janau, māthurā gaṇikā, māthuraṃ vatthaṃ| mathurāya āgatau māthurau, mathurā assa nivāsauti māthurau, mathurāya issarau māthurau rājā| ‘‘sabbatau kau’’ti aittha puna sabbatauggahaṇaina taddhitataupi k‍vaci sasarakakārāgamau, māthurakau vā, rājagahai jātau, rājagahā āgatau, rājagahau assa nivāsauti vā, rājagahassa issarauti vā rājagahau, rājagahakau vā| aivaṃ sāgalau, sāgalakau vā, pāṭaliputtau, pāṭaliputtakau vā, vaisāliyaṃ jātautiādiatthai vaisālau, vaisālakau vā, kusinārai jātau kausinārau, kausinārakau vā| aivaṃ sākaitau, sākaitakau vā, kausambau, kausambakau vā, indapattau, indapattakau vā, kapil‍lau, kapil‍lakau vā, bhārukacchau, bhārukacchakau vā, nagarai jātau, nagarā āgatau, nagarai vasatīti vā nāgarau, nāgarakau vā| aivaṃ jānapadau|

janapadanāmaisu pana sabbattha bahuvacanamaiva bhavati| yathā – aṅgaisu jātau, aṅgaihi āgatau, aṅgā assa nivāsau, aṅgānaṃ issarau vā aṅgau, aṅgakau vā, māgadhau, māgadhakau vā, kausalau, kausalakau vā, vaidaihau, vaidaihakau vā, kambaujau, kambaujakau vā, gandhārau, gandhārakau vā, sauvīrau, sauvīrakau vā, sindhavau, sindhavakau vā, assakau, kāliṅgau, pañ‍cālau, sak‍kau, tathā suraṭṭhai jātau, suraṭṭhassa issarau vā sauraṭṭhau, sauraṭṭhakau vā| aivaṃ mahāraṭṭhau, mahāraṭṭhakau vā ic‍cādi|

nakkhattayaugai kattikāya puṇṇacandayuttāya yuttau māsau kattikau, magasiraina candayuttaina nakkhattaina yuttau māsau māgasirau| aivaṃ phussaina yuttau māsau phussau, maghāya yuttau māsau māghau, phagguniyā yuttau māsau phaggunau, cittāya yuttau māsau cittau, visākhāya yuttau māsau vaisākhau, jaiṭṭhāya yuttau māsau jaiṭṭhau, uttarāsāḷhāya yuttau māsau āsāḷhau, āsāḷhī vā, savaṇaina yuttau māsau sāvaṇau, sāvaṇī| bhaddaina yuttau māsau bhaddau, assayujaina yuttau māsau assayujau, buddhau assa daivatāti buddhau| aivaṃ saugatau, māhindau, yāmau, saumau|

byākaraṇaṃ avaic‍cādhītai vaiyyākaraṇau| aivaṃ mauhuttau, naimittau, aṅgavij‍jau, vatthuvij‍jau| vasātīnaṃ visayau daisau vāsātau, udumbarā asmiṃ padaisai santīti audumbarau daisau|

sahassaina nibbattā sāhassī parikhā, payasā nibbattaṃ pāyāsaṃ, sahassaṃ arahatīti sāhassī gāthā, ayasau  vikārau āyasau| aivaṃ sauvaṇṇau, purisau parimāṇamassāti paurisaṃ udakaṃ|

caggahaṇaina tattha jātau, tattha vasati, tassa hitaṃ, taṃ arahatītiādīsu ṇaiyyappac‍cayau| bārāṇasiyaṃ jātau, vasatīti vā bārāṇasaiyyakau, purai viya kakārāgamau| aivaṃ campaiyyakau, sāgalaiyyakau, mithilaiyyakau janau, gaṅgaiyyau macchau, silāya jātaṃ sailaiyyakaṃ, kulai jātau kaulaiyyakau sunakhau, vanai jātaṃ vānaiyyaṃ pupphaṃ| aivaṃ pabbataiyyau mānusau, pabbataiyyā nadī, pabbataiyyaṃ ausadhaṃ, pathassa hitaṃ pāthaiyyaṃ, sapatissa hitaṃ sāpataiyyaṃ dhanaṃ, padīpaiyyaṃ tailaṃ, mātu hitaṃ mattaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ paittaiyyaṃ| dakkhiṇamarahatīti dakkhiṇaiyyau ic‍cādi|

378. jātādīnamimiyā ca|

jātaic‍caivamādīnaṃ saddānaṃ atthai ima iyaic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti vā|

pacchā jātau pacchimau, pacchimā janatā, pacchimaṃ cittaṃ, antai jātau antimau, antimā, antimaṃ| aivaṃ majjhimau, purimau, uparimau, haiṭṭhimau, pac‍cantimau, gaupphimau, ganthimau|

tathā  iyappac‍cayai manussajātiyā jātau manussajātiyau, manussajātiyā, manussajātiyaṃ| aivaṃ assajātiyau, hatthijātiyau, baudhisattajātiyau, dabbajātiyau, samānajātiyau, laukiyau ic‍cādi|

ādiggahaṇaina tattha niyuttau, tadassa atthi, tattha bhavautiādīsvapi ima iyappac‍cayā haunti, casaddaina ikappac‍cayau ca| antai niyuttau antimau, antiyau, antikau, puttau assa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatīti puttimau, puttiyau, vuttikau, kappau assa atthīti kappiyau, jaṭā assa atthīti jaṭiyau, hānabhāgau assa atthīti hānabhāgiyau| aivaṃ ṭhitibhāgiyau, baudhissa pakkhai bhavā baudhipakkhiyā, pañ‍cavaggai bhavā pañ‍cavaggiyā| aivaṃ chabbaggiyā, udariyaṃ, attanau idanti attaniyaṃ, nakārāgamau|

casaddaggahaṇaina kiya yaṇyappac‍cayā ca| jātiyā niyuttau jātikiyau| aivaṃ andhakiyau, jac‍candhai niyuttau jac‍candhakiyau| sassa ayanti sakiyau| aivaṃ parakiyau|

yappac‍cayau sādhuhitabhavajātādiatthaisu| yathā – kammani sādhu kammañ‍ñaṃ| sabhāyaṃ sādhu sabbhaṃ, ‘‘yavataṃ talanā’’dinā ukārādi| aivaṃ maidhāya hitaṃ maijjhaṃ ghaṭaṃ| pādānaṃ hitaṃ paj‍jaṃ tailaṃ, rathassa hitā racchā, gāmai bhavau gammau, gavai bhavaṃ gabyaṃ, ‘‘ausarai cā’’ti suttai casaddaina yappac‍cayai paraipi avādaisau| kavimhi bhavaṃ kabyaṃ, divi bhavā dibyā, thanatau jātaṃ thañ‍ñaṃ, dhanāya saṃvattatīti dhañ‍ñaṃ|

ṇyappac‍cayau parisāyaṃ sādhu pārisaj‍jau, dakārāgamau, samaṇānaṃ hitā sāmañ‍ñā janā, brāhmaṇānaṃ hitā brāhmañ‍ñā, arūpai bhavā āruppā ic‍cādi|

‘‘rājaputtānaṃ samūhau’’ti viggahai –

379. samūhatthai kaṇaṇā|

chaṭṭhiyantatau ‘‘taisaṃ samūhau’’ti atthai kaṇaṇaic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti| rājaputtakau, rājaputtakaṃ vā, rājaputtau| aivaṃ mānussakau, mānussau, māthurakau, māthurau, paurisakau, paurisau, vuddhānaṃ samūhau vuddhakau, vuddhau| aivaṃ māyūrakau, māyūrau, kāpautau, kaukilau, māhiṃsakau, māhiṃsau, auṭṭhakau, aurabbhakau, aṭṭhan‍naṃ samūhau aṭṭhakau, rājānaṃ samūhau rājakau, bhikkhānaṃ samūhau bhikkhau, sikkhānaṃ samūhau sikkhau, dvin‍naṃ samūhau dvayaṃ, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā ikārassa ayādaisau| aivaṃ tiṇṇaṃ samūhau tayaṃ ic‍cādi|

‘‘samūhatthai’’ti vattatai|

380. gāmajanabandhusahāyādīhi tā|

gāmajanabandhusahāyaic‍caivamādīhi tāpac‍cayau hauti samūhatthai| gāmānaṃ samūhau gāmatā| aivaṃ janatā, bandhutā, sahāyatā, nāgaratā| ‘‘tā’’ti yaugavibhāgaina sakatthaipi daivauyaiva daivatā, tāpac‍cayantassa nic‍camitthiliṅgatā|

381. tadassa ṭhānamiyau ca|

‘‘tadassa ṭhāna’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai chaṭṭhiyantatau iyappac‍cayau hauti| madanassa ṭhānaṃ madaniyau, madaniyā, madaniyaṃ, bandhanassa ṭhānaṃ bandhaniyaṃ| aivaṃ mucchaniyaṃ| rajaniyaṃ, gamaniyaṃ, dassaniyaṃ, upādāniyaṃ, pasādaniyaṃ| casaddaina hitādiatthaipi upādānānaṃ hitā upādāniyā ic‍cādi|

382. upamatthāyitattaṃ|

upamatthai upamāvāciliṅgatau āyitattappac‍cayau hauti| dhūmau viya dissatīti dhūmāyitattaṃ| aivaṃ timirāyitattaṃ|

‘‘tadassa ṭhāna’’nti vattatai|

383. tan‍nissitatthai lau|

‘‘tan‍nissita’’nti atthai, ‘‘tadassaṭhāna’’nti atthai ca lappac‍cayau hauti| duṭṭhu nissitaṃ, duṭṭhu ṭhānaṃ vā duṭṭhul‍laṃ, duṭṭhul‍lā vācā, lassa dvibhāvau| aivaṃ vaidal‍laṃ|

‘‘abhijjhā  assa pakati, abhijjhā assa bahulā’’ti vā viggahai –

384. ālu tabbahulai|

paṭhamāvibhatyantatau āluppac‍cayau hauti ‘‘tadassa bahula’’mic‍caitasmiṃ atthai| abhijjhālu, abhijjhālū, abhijjhālavau| aivaṃ sītālu, dhajālu, dayālu| ‘‘sabbatau kau’’ti aittha puna sabbatauggahaṇaina kakārāgamau, abhijjhālukau, abhijjhālukā, abhijjhālukaṃ| aivaṃ sītālukau, dayālukau, tathā hīnauva hīnakau| aivaṃ pautakau, kumārakau, māṇavakau, mudukau, ujukau, appamattakaṃ, auramattakaṃ, sīlamattakaṃ ic‍cādi|

‘‘yadanupapan‍nā nipātanā sijjhantī’’ti iminā paṭibhāgakucchitasañ‍ñānukampādiatthaisu kappac‍cayau| paṭibhāgatthai hatthinau iva hatthikā| aivaṃ assakā| kucchitatthai kucchitau samaṇau samaṇakau| aivaṃ brāhmaṇakau, muṇḍakau, paṇḍitakau, vaiyyākaraṇakau| sañ‍ñāyaṃ katakau, bhaṭakau| anukampāyaṃ puttakau|

tathā kiṃyataitatau parimāṇatthai ttakavantuppac‍cayā| kiṃ parimāṇamassāti kittakaṃ| aivaṃ yattakaṃ, tattakaṃ, aittakaṃ| vantumhi āttañ‍ca, yaṃ parimāṇamassāti yāvā, yāvantau, guṇavantusamaṃ| aivaṃ tāvā, tāvantau| aitāvā, aitāvantau ic‍cādi|

‘‘suvaṇṇaina pakata’’nti viggahai –

385. tappakativacanai mayau|

tappakativacanatthai mayappac‍cayau hauti, pakarīyatīti pakati, taina pakati tappakati, tappakatiyā vacanaṃ kathanaṃ tappakativacanaṃ| suvaṇṇamayau rathau, sauvaṇṇamayau vā, suvaṇṇamayā bhājanavikati, suvaṇṇamayaṃ bhājanaṃ| aivaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ, rajatamayaṃ, jatumayaṃ, dārumayaṃ, mattikāmayaṃ, iddhiyā nibbattaṃ iddhimayaṃ|

manatau nipphan‍nā manaumayā, ayasāpakataṃ ayaumayaṃ| aittha ca ‘‘manaugaṇādīna’’nti vattamānai –

386. aitaisamau laupai|

aitaisaṃ manaugaṇādīnaṃ antau auttamāpaj‍jatai vibhattilaupai kataiti aukārau|

gavaina pakataṃ karīsaṃ, gautau nibbattanti vā gaumayaṃ| ‘‘mayau’’tiyaugavibhāgaina sakatthaipi dānamaiva dānamayaṃ, sīlamayaṃ ic‍cādi|

saṃsaṭṭhādianaikatthataddhitaṃ|

bhāvataddhita

‘‘alasassa  bhāvau’’ti viggahai –

387. ṇyattatā bhāvai tu|

chaṭṭhiyantatau ṇyattatāic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti ‘‘tassa bhāvau’’ ic‍caitasmiṃ atthai, tusaddaggahaṇaina ttanaṇaiyyādippac‍cayā ca| bhavanti aitasmā buddhisaddā iti bhāvau, saddappavattinimittaṃ vuc‍cati, vuttañ‍ca – ‘‘yassa guṇassa hi bhāvā dabbai saddanivaisau tadabhidhānaiṇyattatādayau’’ti| ṇyattattanantānaṃ nic‍caṃ napuṃsakattaṃ, tāpac‍cayantassa sabhāvatau nic‍camitthiliṅgatā| ṇyappac‍cayauyaṃ guṇavacanai brāhmaṇādīhi, tattha ‘‘avaṇṇau yai laupañ‍cā’’ti avaṇṇalaupau, ādivuddhi|

ālasyaṃ| aivaṃ āraugyaṃ, udaggassa bhāvau audagyaṃ, sakhinau bhāvau sakhyaṃ, aṇaṇassa bhāvau āṇaṇyaṃ, vidhavāya bhāvau vaidhabyaṃ, dubbalassa bhāvau dubbalyaṃ, capalassa bhāvau cāpalyaṃ|

viyattassa bhāvau vaiyyattiyaṃ, maccharassa bhāvau macchariyaṃ| aivaṃ issariyaṃ, ālasiyaṃ, muṇḍiyaṃ, mūḷhiyaṃ| aittha ‘‘vaiyyattiya’’ntiādīsu ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā yamhi ikārāgamau|

‘‘paṇḍitassa bhāvau paṇḍitya’’ntiādīsu ‘‘yavataṃ talanadakārānaṃ byañ‍janāni calañajakāratta’’nti tyakārasaṃyaugādīnaṃ calañajakārādaisā, dvittaṃ| paṇḍic‍caṃ, bahussutassa bhāvau bāhussac‍caṃ, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā ukārassa akārau, aivaṃ paurauhic‍caṃ, adhipatissa bhāvau ādhipac‍caṃ, muṭṭhassatissa bhāvau muṭṭhassac‍caṃ, ivaṇṇalaupau| kusalassa bhāvau kausal‍laṃ| aivaṃ vaipul‍laṃ, samānānaṃ bhāvau sāmañ‍ñaṃ, gilānassa bhāvau gailañ‍ñaṃ, ‘‘k‍vacādimajjhuttarā’’disuttaina saṃyaugai parai rassattaṃ|

suhadassa bhāvausauhaj‍jaṃ| aivaṃ vaisāraj‍jaṃ, kusīdassabhāvaukausaj‍jaṃ, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā īkārassa akārau| tathā ‘‘purisassa bhāvau paurisa’’ntiādīsu ‘‘yavataṃ talanā’’disuttai kāraggahaṇaina yavataṃ sakārakacaṭapavaggānaṃ sakārakacaṭapavaggādaisā| sumanassa bhāvau saumanassaṃ| aivaṃ daumanassaṃ, sauvacassaṃ, dauvacassaṃ, aittha sakārāgamau| tathā nipakassa bhāvau naipak‍kaṃ, dvittaṃ| aivaṃ ādhik‍kaṃ, dubhagassa bhāvau daubhaggaṃ, vāṇijassa bhāvau vāṇij‍jaṃ, rājinau bhāvau raj‍jaṃ, ‘‘k‍vacā’’dinā rassattaṃ| sarūpassa bhāvau sāruppaṃ| aivaṃ aupammaṃ, saukhummaṃ|

tathassa bhāvau tacchaṃ, dummaidhassa bhāvau dummaijjhaṃ, samaṇassa bhāvau sāmañ‍ñaṃ| aivaṃ brāhmañ‍ñaṃ, nipuṇassa bhāvau naipuñ‍ñaṃ| ‘‘taccha’’ntiādīsupi kāraggahaṇainaiva yavataṃ thadhaṇakārānaṃ chajhañakārādaisā|

ttatāpac‍cayaisu – paṃsukūlikassa bhāvau paṃsukūlikattaṃ, paṃsukūlikatā| aivaṃ taicīvarikattaṃ, taicīvarikatā, audarikattaṃ, audarikatā, manussattaṃ, manussatā jāti, nīlattaṃ, nīlatā guṇau, yācakattaṃ, yācakatā kriyā, daṇḍittaṃ, daṇḍitā dabbaṃ, sac‍cavāditā, pāramitā, katañ‍ñutā, sabbañ‍ñutā, ‘‘k‍vacā’’dinā tāpac‍cayai rassattaṃ, appicchatā, asaṃsaggatā, bhassārāmatā, niddārāmatā, lahutā ic‍cādi|

ttanapac‍cayai – puthuj‍janassa bhāvau puthuj‍janattanaṃ, vaidanattanaṃ, jāyattanaṃ|

ṇaiyyai – sucissa bhāvau saucaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ ādhipabhaiyyaṃ, kavissa bhāvaukāvaiyyaṃ, thainassa bhāvau thaiyyaṃ, mahāvuttinā nakārassa laupau|

‘‘ṇyattatā’’ti yaugavibhāgaina kammani, sakatthai ca ṇyādayau, vīrānaṃ bhāvau, kammaṃ vā vīriyaṃ, paribhaṭassa kammaṃ pāribhaṭyaṃ, pāribhaṭyassa bhāvau pāribhaṭyatā| aivaṃ sauvacassatā, bhisaggassa kammaṃ bhaisaj‍jaṃ, byāvaṭassa kammaṃ vaiyyāvac‍caṃ, saṭhassa bhāvau, kammaṃ vā sāṭhaiyyaṃ|

sakatthai pana – yathābhūtamaiva yathābhuc‍caṃ, karuṇāyaiva kāruñ‍ñaṃ, pattakālamaiva pattakal‍laṃ, ākāsānantamaiva  ākāsānañ‍caṃ, kāyapāguñ‍ñamaiva kāyapāguñ‍ñatā ic‍cādi|

‘‘visamassa bhāvau’’ti viggahai –

‘‘ttatā, bhāvai’’ti ca vattatai|

388. ṇa visamādīhi|

visamaic‍caivamādīhi chaṭṭhiyantaihi ṇappac‍cayau hauti, tta tā ca ‘‘tassa bhāvau’’ ic‍caitasmiṃ atthai| ākatigaṇauyaṃ| vaisamaṃ, visamattaṃ, visamatā| sucissa bhāvau saucaṃ, sucittaṃ, sucitā, garunau bhāvau gāravau, ādivuddhi, ‘‘au sarai cā’’ti suttai casaddaggahaṇaina ukārassa ca avādaisau| paṭunau bhāvau pāṭavaṃ, paṭuttaṃ, paṭutā|

ujunau bhāvau aj‍javaṃ, mudunau bhāvau maddavaṃ ic‍catra ‘‘āttañ‍cā’’ti ṇamhi ikārukārānaṃ āttaṃ, dvibhāvau, saṃyaugai ādirassattañ‍ca| ujutā, mudutā| aivaṃ isissa bhāvau ārisyaṃ, āsabhaṃ, kumārassa bhāvaukaumāraṃ, yuvassa bhāvau yaubbanaṃ, mahāvuttinā nakārāgamau, paramānaṃ bhāvau, kammaṃ vā pāramī dānādikriyā, ‘‘ṇavaṇikā’’disuttaina īpac‍cayau, samaggānaṃ bhāvau sāmaggī|

389. ramaṇīyāditau kaṇa|

ramaṇīyaic‍caivamāditau kaṇapac‍cayau hauti, tta tā ca bhāvatthai| ramaṇīyassa bhāvau rāmaṇīyakaṃ, ramaṇīyattaṃ, ramaṇīyatā| aivaṃ mānuñ‍ñakaṃ, manuñ‍ñattaṃ, manuñ‍ñatā, piyarūpakaṃ, piyarūpattaṃ, piyarūpatā, kalyāṇakaṃ, kalyāṇattaṃ, kalyāṇatā, caurakaṃ, caurikā vā, caurattaṃ, cauratā, aḍḍhakaṃ, aḍḍhattaṃ, aḍḍhatā ic‍cādi|

bhāvataddhitaṃ|

visaisataddhita

‘‘sabbai imai pāpā ayamimaisaṃ visaisaina pāpau’’ti viggahai –

390. visaisai taratamisikiyiṭṭhā|

visaisatthai tara tama isika iya iṭṭhaic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti| pāpatarau, pāpatarā, pāpataraṃ| tataupi adhikau pāpatamau, pāpatamā, pāpatamaṃ| pāpisikau, pāpisikā, pāpisikaṃ| pāpiyau, pāpiyā, pāpiyaṃ| pāpiṭṭhau, pāpiṭṭhā, pāpiṭṭhaṃ| atisayaina pāpiṭṭhau, pāpiṭṭhatarau| aivaṃ paṭutarau, paṭutamau, paṭisikau, paṭiyau, paṭiṭṭhau| sabbaisaṃ atisayaina varau varatarau, varatamau, varisikau, variyau, variṭṭhau| aivaṃ paṇītatarau, paṇītatamau|

‘‘sabbai imai vuḍḍhā ayamimaisaṃ visaisaina vuḍḍhau’’ti atthai iyaiṭṭhappac‍cayā haunti|

391. vuḍḍhassa  jau iyiṭṭhaisu|

sabbassaiva vuḍḍhasaddassa jau hauti iya iṭṭhaic‍caitaisu pac‍cayaisu| jaiyyau, jaiṭṭhau, aittha ca ‘‘saralaupādi’’suttai tuggahaṇaina laupamakatvā ‘‘sarā sarai laupa’’nti pubbasarai luttai ‘‘k‍vacāsavaṇṇaṃ luttai’’ti aikārau|

‘‘iyiṭṭhaisū’’ti adhikārau, ‘‘jau’’ti ca vattatai|

392. pasatthassa sau ca|

sabbassaiva pasatthasaddassa sādaisau hauti, jau ca iyiṭṭhaisu| ayañ‍ca pasatthau ayañ‍ca pasatthau sabbai imai pasatthā ayamimaisaṃ visaisaina pasatthauti saiyyau, saiṭṭhau, jaiyyau, jaiṭṭhau|

393. antikassa naidau|

sabbassaiva antikasaddassa naidādaisau hauti iyiṭṭhaisu| visaisaina antikauti naidiyau, naidiṭṭhau|

394. bāḷhassa sādhau|

sabbassaiva bāḷhasaddassa sādhādaisau hauti iyiṭṭhaisu| visaisaina bāḷhauti sādhiyau, sādhiṭṭhau|

395. appassa kaṇa|

sabbassa appasaddassa kaṇa hauti iyiṭṭhaisu| visaisaina appauti kaṇiyau, kaṇiṭṭhau|

‘‘visaisaina yuvā’’ti atthai ‘‘kaṇa’’iti vattatai|

396. yuvānañ‍ca|

sabbassa yuvasaddassa kaṇa hauti iyiṭṭhaisu| ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā ṇakārassa nakārau| kaniyau, kaniṭṭhau|

397. vantu mantu vīnañ‍ca laupau|

vantumantuvī ic‍caitaisaṃ pac‍cayānaṃ laupau hauti iyiṭṭhaisu| sabbai imai guṇavantau ayamimaisaṃ visaisaina guṇavāti guṇiyau, guṇiṭṭhau, visaisaina satimāti satiyau, satiṭṭhau, visaisaina maidhāvīti maidhiyau, maidhiṭṭhau ic‍cādi|

visaisataddhitaṃ|

assatthitaddhita

‘‘maidhā yassa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatī’’ti viggahai –

398. tadassatthīti vī ca|

paṭhamāvibhatyantā ‘‘tadassa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jati’’ ic‍caitaisvatthaisu vīpac‍cayau hauti| maidhāmāyā saddaihi cāyaṃ| maidhāvī, maidhāvinau| itthiyaṃ īkārantattā ‘‘patibhikkhurājīkārantaihi inī’’ti inī, maidhāvinī, maidhāviniyau| napuṃsakai maidhāvi kulaṃ| aivaṃ māyāvī, māyāvinī, māyāvi cittaṃ|

caggahaṇaina sau i lava ālādippac‍cayā ca| yathā – sumaidhā yassa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatīti sumaidhasau, rassattaṃ| aivaṃ laumasau| picchaṃ assa atthi, tasmiṃ  vā vij‍jatīti picchilau| aivaṃ phainilau, tuṇḍilau, jaṭilau| kaisā assa atthīti kaisavau, vācālau ic‍cādi|

‘‘tapau assa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatī’’ti viggahai –

‘‘tadassatthī’’ti adhikārau|

399. tapāditau sī|

tapaic‍caivamāditau sīpac‍cayau hauti ‘‘tadassatthi’’ ic‍caitasmiṃ atthai| sassa dvibhāvau| tapassī, tapassinau, tapassinī, tapassi| aivaṃ taijassī, yasassī, manassī, payassī|

‘‘daṇḍau assa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatī’’ti viggahai –

400. daṇḍāditau ikaī|

ādisaddauyaṃ pakāratthau, daṇḍaic‍caivamāditau avaṇṇantā ika ī ic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti ‘‘tadassatthi’’ ic‍caitasmiṃ atthai| daṇḍikau, daṇḍī, daṇḍinau, daṇḍinī| aivaṃ mālikau, mālī, mālinī, chattikau, chattī, rūpikau, rūpī, kaisikau, kaisī, saṅghī, ñāṇī, hatthī ic‍cādi|

401. madhvāditau rau|

madhuāditau rappac‍cayau hauti ‘‘tadassatthī’’ti atthai| madhu assa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatīti madhurau guḷau, madhurā sakkharā, madhuraṃ khīraṃ, kuñ‍jā hanū aitassa santīti kuñ‍jarau, sabbasmiṃ  vattabbai mukhamassa atthīti mukharau, susi assa atthīti susirau| aivaṃ rucirau, nagarau|

‘‘guṇau assa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatī’’ti viggahai –

402. guṇāditauvantu|

guṇaic‍caivamāditau vantuppac‍cayau hauti ‘‘tadassa atthī’’ti atthai| vibhattilaupai, nāmabyapadaisai ca katai syādyuppatti| guṇavantu si, ‘‘savibhattissa, ntussā’’ti adhikic‍ca ‘‘ā simhī’’ti āttaṃ, guṇavā purisau, saisaṃ ñaiyyaṃ| aivaṃ gaṇavā, kulavā ic‍cādayau| itthiyaṃ ‘‘ṇava ṇika ṇaiyyaṇantūhī’’ti īpac‍cayau, ‘‘vā’’ti vattamānai ‘‘ntussa tamīkārai’’ti takārau, guṇavatī, guṇavantī ic‍cādi| napuṃsakai ‘‘aṃ napuṃsakai’’ti savibhattissa ntussa amādaisau, guṇavaṃ ic‍cādi|

‘‘sati assa atthi, tasmiṃ vā vij‍jatī’’ti viggahai –

‘‘tadassatthī’’ti vattatai|

493. satyādīhi mantu|

satiic‍caivamādīhi avaṇṇantarahitaihi paṭhamāvibhatyantaihi liṅgaihi mantuppac‍cayau hauti ‘‘tadassatthī’’ti atthai| saisaṃ guṇavantusamaṃ| satimā, satimatī, satimantī, satimaṃ| aivaṃ dhitimā, gatimā ic‍cādayau|

tathā ‘‘āyu assa atthīti āyu mantu’’ic‍catra –

404. āyussukārāsamantumhi|

āyussa ukārau asa hauti mantumhīti asādaisau| āyasmā, saisaṃ samaṃ| gāvau assa santīti gaumā, gaumantau, gaumatī, gaumantī, gaumaṃ kulaṃ ic‍cādi|

‘‘saddhā assa atthī’’ti viggahai –

405. saddhāditau ṇa|

saddhā pañ‍ñāic‍caivamāditau ṇappac‍cayau hauti ‘‘tadassatthi’’ic‍caitasmiṃ atthai| saddhau purisau, saddhā kañ‍ñā, saddhaṃ kulaṃ| aivaṃ pañ‍ñau, amaccharau, tathā buddhaṃ, buddhi assa atthīti buddhau ic‍cādi|

assatthitaddhitaṃ|

saṅkhyātaddhita

‘‘pañ‍can‍naṃ pūraṇau’’ti viggahai –

406. saṅkhyāpūraṇai mau|

pūrayati saṅkhyā anaināti pūraṇau, saṅkhyāya pūraṇau saṅkhyāpūraṇau, tasmiṃ saṅkhyāpūraṇatthai chaṭṭhiyantatau mappac‍cayau hauti| pañ‍camau, pañ‍can‍naṃ pūraṇī pañ‍camī, ‘‘nadāditau vā ī’’ti īpac‍cayau| ‘‘itthiyamatau āpac‍cayau’’ti āpac‍cayau, pañ‍camā vīriyapāramī, pañ‍camaṃ jhānaṃ| aivaṃ sattamau, sattamī, sattamā, sattamaṃ, aṭṭhamau, aṭṭhamī, aṭṭhamā, aṭṭhamaṃ, navamau, navamī, navamā, navamaṃ, dasamau, dasamī, dasamā, dasamaṃ ic‍cādi|

‘‘saṅkhyāpūraṇai’’ti adhikārau|

407. catucchaihi thaṭhā|

catuchaic‍caitaihi thaṭhaic‍caitai pac‍cayā haunti saṅkhyāpūraṇatthai| catun‍naṃ pūraṇau catutthau, dvittaṃ, catutthī, catutthā, catutthaṃ, chan‍naṃ pūraṇau chaṭṭhau, chaṭṭhī, chaṭṭhā, chaṭṭhaṃ, chaṭṭhau aiva chaṭṭhamau|

chāhaṃ, chaḷāyatanaṃ ic‍catra –

408. sa chassa vā|

chassa sakārādaisau hauti vā saṅkhyānai| chāhamassa jīvitaṃ sāhaṃ, chāhaṃ vā, saḷāyatanaṃ|

‘‘dvin‍naṃ pūraṇau’’ti viggahai –

409. dvitīhi tiyau|

dvitiic‍caitaihi tiyappac‍cayau hauti saṅkhyāpūraṇatthai| vipariṇāmaina ‘‘dvi tiṇṇa’’nti vattamānai –

410. tiyai dutāpi ca|

dvitiic‍caitaisaṃ dutaic‍cādaisā haunti tiyappac‍cayai parai| dutiyau purisau, dutiyā, dutiyaṃ| aivaṃ tiṇṇaṃ pūraṇau tatiyau, tatiyā, tatiyaṃ| apiggahaṇaina añ‍ñatthāpi dvisaddassa duādaisau hauti, casaddaina di ca| dvai rattiyau durattaṃ, duvidhaṃ, duvaṅgaṃ, dirattaṃ, diguṇaṃ, digu|

411. taisamaḍḍhūpapadaina aḍḍhuḍḍhadivaḍḍhadiyaḍḍhaḍḍhatiyā|

taisaṃ catutthadutiyatatiyānaṃ aḍḍhūpapadānaṃ aḍḍhūpapadaina saha aḍḍhuḍḍhadivaḍḍhadiyaḍḍhaaḍḍhatiyādaisā haunti|

aittha ca –

aḍḍhūpapadapādāna-sāmatthā aḍḍhapubbakā|

taisaṃsaddaina gayhantai, catutthadutiyādayau||

aḍḍhaina catutthau aḍḍhuḍḍhau, aḍḍhaina dutiyau divaḍḍhau, diyaḍḍhau, aḍḍhaina tatiyau aḍḍhatiyau|

‘‘aikañ‍ca dasa cā’’ti atthai dvandasamāsai, ‘‘aikaina adhikā dasā’’ti atthai tappurisasamāsai vā katai ‘‘saṅkhyānai’’ti vattamānai ‘‘dvaikaṭṭhānamākārau vā’’ti āttaṃ| vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau|

taina caittha –

dvaikaṭṭhānaṃ dasai nic‍caṃ, dvissā’navutiyā navā|

itaraisa’masantañ‍ca, āttaṃ dīpaiti vāsuti||

‘‘aikāditau dasara saṅkhyānai’’ti rattaṃ|

rādaisau vaṇṇamattattā, vaṇṇamattappasaṅgipi|

siyā dasassa dassaiva, nimittāsan‍nabhāvatau||

tatau bahuvacanaṃ yau, ‘‘pañ‍cādīnamakārau’’ti savibhattissa antassa attaṃ| aikārasa, aikādasa, liṅgattayaipi samānaṃ|

‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

412. aikāditau  dasassī|

aikāditau parassa dasassa antai īpac‍cayau hauti vā pūraṇatthai|

dasassa pac‍cayāyaugā, laddhamantaiti atthatau|

tadantassa sabhāvaina, itthiyaṃyaiva sambhavau||

aikādasan‍naṃ pūraṇī aikādasī, añ‍ñatra aikādasamau, aikādasamaṃ|

dvai ca dasa ca, dvīhi vā adhikā dasāti ‘‘dvi dasa’’ic‍catra ‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

‘‘vīsati dasaisu bā dvissa tū’’ti bādaisau, dassa rādaisau| bārasa, añ‍ñatra āttaṃ, dvādasa| dvādasan‍naṃ pūraṇau bārasamau, dvādasamau, dvādasī|

tayau ca dasa ca, tīhi vā adhikā dasāti tairasa, ‘‘taisu vuddhi laupā’’dinā tissa taiādasau ānavutiyā, tairasamau, tairasī|

cattārau ca dasa ca, catūhi vā adhikā dasāti catuddasa ic‍catra ‘‘gaṇanai dasassā’’ti ca vattamānai ‘‘catūpapadassa laupau tuttarapadādi cassa cucaupi navā’’ti tulaupau, cucau ca| cuddasa, cauddasa, catuddasa| cuddasamau, catuddasamau, cakuddasī, cātuddasī vā|

pañ‍ca ca dasa ca, pañ‍cahi vā adhikā dasāti pañ‍cadasa, ‘‘taisu vuddhi laupā’’dinā pañ‍casaddassa dasa vīsaisu pan‍napaṇṇaādaisāpi, ‘‘aṭṭhāditau cā’’ti rattaṃ| pan‍narasa, pañ‍cadasa| pan‍narasamau, pañ‍cadasamau, pan‍narasī, pañ‍cadasī|

‘‘cha  ca dasa ca, chahi vā adhikā dasā’’ti samāsai katai ‘‘chassā’’ti vattamānai ‘‘dasai sau nic‍cañ‍cā’’ti sau, ‘‘saṅkhyānaṃ, vā’’ti ca vattatai, ‘‘la darāna’’nti lattaṃ, vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau|

ḷau nic‍caṃ sauḷasai dassa, cattālīsai ca tairasai|

añ‍ñatra na ca hautāyaṃ, vavatthitavibhāsatau||

laḷānamavisaisau k‍vaci, sauḷasa| taiḷasa, cattālīsaṃ, cattārīsaṃ| sauḷasamau, sauḷasī|

‘‘vā, dasara saṅkhyānai’’ti adhikic‍ca ‘‘aṭṭhāditau cā’’ti rattaṃ| aṭṭhārasa, aṭṭhādasa, āttaṃ| aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ pūraṇau aṭṭhārasamau, aṭṭhādasamau| aivaṃ sattarasa, sattadasa| sattarasamau, sattadasamau|

aṭṭhāditauti kimatthaṃ? catuddasa|

aikaina ūnā vīsatīti tappurisau, aikūnavīsati| aikūnavīsatādayau ānavutiyā aikavacanantā, itthiliṅgā ca daṭṭhabbā, tai ca saṅkhyānai, saṅkhyaiyyai ca vattantai, yadā saṅkhyānai vattantai, tadā bhikkhūnamaikūnavīsati tiṭṭhati, bhauti bhikkhūnamaikūnavīsati tiṭṭhatu, bhikkhūnamaikūnavīsatiṃ passa, bhikkhūnamaikūnavīsatiyā kataṃ ic‍cādi|

saṅkhyaiyyai pana aikūnavīsati bhikkhavau tiṭṭhanti, bhauntau aikūnavīsati bhikkhavau tiṭṭhatha, aikūnavīsatiṃ bhikkhū passa, aikūnavīsatiyā bhikkhūhi kataṃ ic‍cādi| aivaṃ vīsatādīsupi yaujaitabbaṃ, aikūnavīsatiyā pūraṇau aikūnavīsatimau|

dasa ca dasa cāti atthai dvandasamāsaṃ katvā ‘‘dasadasā’’ti vattabbai ‘‘sarūpānamaikasaisvasaki’’nti aikasaisai katai  dasasaddatau paṭhamābahuvacanaṃ yau| ‘‘dasa yau’’itīdha –

413. gaṇanai dasassa dviti catu pañ‍ca cha satta aṭṭhanavakānaṃ vīti cattāra pañ‍ñā cha sattāsa navā yausu yaunañ‍cī samāsaṃ ṭhiri tītuti|

gaṇanai dasassa sambandhīnaṃ dvika tika catuk‍ka pañ‍caka chak‍kasattaka aṭṭhaka navakānaṃ kataikasaisānaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ vīti cattāra pañ‍ñā cha satta asa nava ic‍cādaisā haunti yausu paraisu, yaunañ‍ca īsaṃ āsaṃ ṭhi riti īti utiic‍caitai ādaisā hauntīti dvidasatthavācakassa dasassa vīādaisau hauti, yauvacanassa īsañ‍ca, saralaupādi|

‘‘saṅkhyānaṃ, vā, antai’’ti ca vattatai|

414. ti ca|

tāsaṃ saṅkhyānamantai tikārāgamau hauti vā| vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau|

vibhāsā vīsa tiṃsāna-mantai hauti tiāgamau|

añ‍ñattha na ca hautaiva, vavatthitavibhāsatau||

‘‘byañ‍janai cā’’ti niggahītalaupau, puna taddhitattā nāmabyapadaisai syādyuppatti| bhikkhūnaṃ vīsati, vīsaṃ vā, vīsati bhikkhū, vīsaṃ vā ic‍cādi| vīsatimau| tathā aikavīsati kusalacittāni, aikavīsaṃ vā| aikavīsatimau| bāvīsati, bāvīsaṃ vā| bāvīsatimau| dvāvīsati, dvāvīsaṃ vā| dvāvīsatimau| taivīsati, taivīsaṃ vā| taivīsatimau| catuvīsati, catuvīsaṃ vā| catuvīsatimau| paṇṇavīsati, paṇṇavīsaṃ vā| paṇṇavīsatimau| pañ‍cavīsati, pañ‍cavīsaṃ vā| pañ‍cavīsatimau| chabbīsati, chabbīsaṃ vā| chabbīsatimau| sattavīsati, sattavīsaṃ vā| sattavīsatimau| aṭṭhavīsati, aṭṭhavīsaṃ vā| aṭṭhavīsatimau| aikūnatiṃsati, aikūnatiṃsaṃvā| aikūnatiṃsatimau|

dasa ca dasa ca dasa cāti ‘‘dasa dasa dasā’’ti vattabbai aikasaisai katai ‘‘gaṇanai dasassā’’tiādinā tiīsamādaisā, ‘‘k‍vacā’’dinā rassattaṃ, niggahītāgamau ca, saisaṃ vīsatisamaṃ| tiṃsati, tiṃsaṃ, tiṃsa vassāni, niggahītalaupau, tiṃsaṃ, tiṃsāya ic‍cādi| aikatiṃsati, aikatiṃsaṃ vā, bāttiṃsaṃ, dvattiṃsaṃ, taittiṃsaṃ ic‍cādi|

catudasatthavācakassa kataikasaisassa dasassa cattāra, yauvacanassa īsaṃ, cattālīsaṃ, ‘‘la darāna’’nti rassa lattaṃ, tālīsaṃ vā| cattālīsatimau| aikacattālīsaṃ, dvācattālīsaṃ, dvicattālīsaṃ, taicattālīsaṃ, ticattālīsaṃ ic‍cādi|

pañ‍cadasatthavācakassa dasassa pañ‍ñā, yauvacanassa āsañ‍ca| pañ‍ñāsaṃ, ‘‘taisu vuddhi laupā’’dinā paṇṇādaisau, paṇṇāsaṃ vā| aikapañ‍ñāsaṃ, dvaipañ‍ñāsaṃ, dvipañ‍ñāsaṃ|

chadasatthavācakassa dasassa cha, yauvacanassa ṭhiādaisau, ‘‘sa chassa vā’’ti sakārādaisau, saṭṭhi, dvāsaṭṭhi, dvaisaṭṭhi, dvisaṭṭhi, taisaṭṭhi, tisaṭṭhi|

sattadasatthavācakassa dasassa satta, yauvacanassa ri, ti ca| sattari, sattati, dvāsattari, dvāsattati, dvisattari, disattati, taisattati, tisattati ic‍cādi|

aṭṭhadasatthavācakassa dasassa asa, yauvacanassa ītiādaisau ca| asīti, aikāsīti, dvaiasīti, taiasīti, caturāsīti, ‘‘k‍vacā’’dinā dīghau|

navadasatthavācakassa  dasassa nava, yauvacanassa uti ca, navuti, dvānavuti, dvainavuti, dvinavuti, tainavuti, tinavuti, catunavuti, chan‍navutiyā, chan‍navutīnaṃ pāsaṇḍānaṃ|

‘‘gaṇanai, dasassā’’ti ca vattatai|

415. dasadasakaṃ sataṃ dasakānaṃ sataṃ sahassañ‍ca yaumhi|

gaṇanai pariyāpan‍nassa dasadasakatthavācakassa dasasaddassa sataṃ hauti, satadasakatthavācakassa dasassa sahassaṃ hauti yaumhi| iminā nipātanaina yaulaupau, taddhitattā puna nāmabyapadaisai syādyuppatti, niggahītassa laupau, ‘‘si’’nti amādaisau, yaujanānaṃ sataṃ, sahassaṃ| sataṃ napuṃsakamaikavacanantañ‍ca, tathā sahassaṃ, vaggabhaidai sabbattha bahuvacanampi bhavati| dvai vīsatiyau| aivaṃ tiṃsādīsupi, dvai satāni, bahūni satāni, dvai sahassāni, bahūni sahassāni|

satassa dvikanti atthai chaṭṭhītappurisaṃ katvā ‘‘sataṃ dvika’’nti vattabbai ‘‘dvikādīnaṃ taduttarapadānañ‍ca nipac‍cantai’’ti vuttiyaṃ vacanatau iminā nipātanaina uttarapadassa pubbanipātau, kakāralaupau ca hauti| dvisataṃ| aivaṃ satassa tikaṃ tisataṃ, tathā catusataṃ, pañ‍casataṃ, chasataṃ, sattasataṃ, aṭṭhasataṃ, navasataṃ, dasasataṃ sahassaṃ hauti| atha vā dvai satāni dvisatanti digusamāsau| aivaṃ tisataṃ, catusataṃ ic‍cādi|

416. yāva taduttari dasaguṇitañ‍ca|

yāva tāsaṃ saṅkhyānamuttari, tāva dasaguṇitañ‍ca kātabbaṃ, aittha dakārau sandhijau| yathā – dasassa gaṇanassa dasaguṇitaṃ  sataṃ hauti, satassa dasaguṇitaṃ sahassaṃ, sahassassa dasaguṇitaṃ dasasahassaṃ, idaṃ nahutantipi vuc‍cati, dasasahassassa dasaguṇitaṃ satasahassaṃ, taṃ lakkhantipi vuc‍cati, satasahassassa dasaguṇitaṃ dasasatasahassaṃ|

‘‘yadanupapan‍nā nipātanā sijjhantī’’ti vattatai|

417. sakanāmaihi|

yāsaṃ pana saṅkhyānaṃ aniddiṭṭhanāmadhaiyyānaṃ yāni rūpāni, tāni sakaihi nāmaihi nipac‍cantai| satasahassānaṃ sataṃ kauṭi, itthiliṅgā, aikavacanantā ca, vaggabhaidai bahuvacanañ‍ca bhavati, kauṭisatasahassānaṃ sataṃ pakauṭi, pakauṭisatasahassānaṃ sataṃ kauṭippakauṭi| aivaṃ nahutaṃ, nin‍nahutaṃ, akkhaubhinī, bindu, abbudaṃ, nirabbudaṃ, ahahaṃ, ababaṃ, aṭaṭaṃ, saugandhikaṃ, uppalaṃ, kumudaṃ, puṇḍarīkaṃ, padumaṃ, kathānaṃ, mahākathānaṃ, asaṅkhyaiyyanti|

ic‍caivaṃ ṭhānatau ṭhānaṃ, satalakkhaguṇaṃ mataṃ|

kauṭippabhutinaṃ vīsa-saṅkhyānañ‍ca yathāk‍kamaṃ||

‘‘dvai parimāṇāni aitassā’’ti viggahai –

418. dvāditau kaunaikatthai ca|

dviic‍caivamāditau gaṇanatau kappac‍cayau hauti anaikatthai| dvikau rāsi dvikaṃ| aivaṃ tikaṃ, catuk‍kaṃ, pañ‍cakaṃ, chak‍kaṃ, sattakaṃ, aṭṭhakaṃ, navakaṃ, dasakaṃ, paṇṇāsakaṃ, satakaṃ, sahassakaṃ ic‍cādi|

saṅkhyātaddhitaṃ|

abyayataddhita

‘‘aikasmiṃ  vārai bhuñ‍jati, dvivārai bhuñ‍jatī’’ti viggahai –

419. aikāditau sakissa kkhattuṃ|

aikadvitiic‍caivamāditau gaṇanatau sakissa ṭhānai vāratthai kkhattuṃpac‍cayau hauti| aikakkhattuṃ, dvikkhattuṃ bhuñ‍jati, ‘‘sabbāsamāvusau’’tiādinā silaupau| aivaṃ tikkhattuṃ, catukkhattuṃ, pañ‍cakkhattuṃ, chakkhattuṃ, sattakkhattuṃ, aṭṭhakkhattuṃ, navakkhattuṃ, dasakkhattuṃ, satakkhattuṃ, sahassakkhattuṃ, bahukkhattuṃ, katikkhattuṃ|

‘‘aikaina vibhāgainā’’ti viggahai –

maṇḍūkagatiyā saṅkhyāggahaṇamanuvattatai|

420. vibhāgai dhā ca|

vibhāgatthai aikādisaṅkhyātau dhāpac‍cayau hauti| casaddaina aikadvitau jjha ca, suttāditau sau ca| aikadhā| dvīhi vibhāgaihi dvidhā, dudhā vā, dvaidhā| tīhi vibhāgaihi tidhā, taidhā vā, ‘‘taisu vuddhī’’tiādinā ikārassaikārau| aivaṃ catudhā, pañ‍cadhā, chadhā, sattadhā, aṭṭhadhā, navadhā, dasadhā, satadhā, sahassadhā, katidhā, bahudhā|

jjhappac‍cayai aikadhā karautīti aikajjhaṃ| aivaṃ dvaijjhaṃ|

saupac‍cayai suttaina vibhāgaina suttasau| aivaṃ byañ‍janasau, padasau, atthasau, bahusau, sabbākāraina sabbasau, upāyasau, haitusau, ṭhānasau, yaunisau|

421. sabbanāmaihi pakāravacanai tu thā|

sabbanāmaihi pakāravacanatthai thāpac‍cayau hauti, tusaddaina thattāpac‍cayau ca| sāmañ‍ñassa bhaidakau visaisau pakārau, tassābhidhānaiti atthau, sau pakārau tathā, taṃ pakāraṃ tathā, taina pakāraina tathā, yaina pakāraina yathā| aivaṃ sabbathā, añ‍ñathā, itarathā, ubhayathā, thattāpac‍cayai taina pakāraina tathattā| aivaṃ yathattā, añ‍ñathattā|

kau pakārauti atthai –

422. kimimaihi thaṃ|

kiṃimaic‍caitaihi thaṃpac‍cayau hauti pakāravacanatthai| ‘‘kissa ka vai cā’’ti aittha casaddaina kissa kādaisau| kathaṃ, kaṃ pakāraṃ kathaṃ, kaina pakāraina kathaṃ, ayaṃ pakārau itthaṃ, imaṃ pakāraṃ itthaṃ| anaina pakāraina itthaṃ, ‘‘imassi thaṃ dāniha tau dhaisu cā’’ti imasaddassa ikārau, dvittaṃ| aittha hi kkhattuṃ ādithaṃpariyausānappac‍cayantānaṃ abyayataddhitattā nāmabyapadaisaṃ katvā vibhattimhi katai ‘‘sabbāsamāvusau’’tiādinā vibhattilaupau, ‘‘k‍vaci tau pañ‍camyatthai’’tiādinā vuttatauādippac‍cayantā ca idhaiva abyayataddhitai saṅgayhanti|

423. yadanupapan‍nā  nipātanā sijjhanti|

yai saddā lakkhaṇaina anupapan‍nā aniddiṭṭhalakkhaṇā akkharāditau, nāmaupasagganipātatau vā samāsataddhitāditau vā, tai nipātanā sijjhanti|

taddhitatau tāva –

imasmā j‍ja siyā kālai, samānāparatau j‍ju ca|

imasaddassa’kārau ca, samānassa ca sau siyā||

imasmiṃ kālai, imasmiṃ divasai vā aj‍ja, samānai kālai saj‍ju, aparasmiṃdivasai aparaj‍ju| nipātaihi bhavatthai tanappac‍cayau| aj‍ja bhavaṃ aj‍jatanaṃ, aj‍ja bhavā aj‍jatanī, svai bhavaṃ svātanaṃ| aivaṃ purātanaṃ, hiyyau bhavaṃ hiyyattanaṃ, hiyyau bhavā hiyyattanī ic‍cādi|

abyayataddhitaṃ|

sāmañ‍ñavuttibhāvatthā-byayatau taddhitaṃ tidhā|

tatrādi catudhāpac‍cā-naikatthassatthisaṅkhyātau||

iti padarūpasiddhiyaṃ taddhitakaṇḍau

pañ‍camau|

6. ākhyātakaṇḍa

bhūvādigaṇa

vibhattividhāna

atha  ākhyātavibhattiyau kriyāvācīhi dhātūhi parā vuc‍cantai|

tattha kriyaṃ ācikkhatīti ākhyātaṃ, kriyāpadaṃ| vuttañhi ‘‘kālakārakapurisaparidīpakaṃ kriyālakkhaṇamākhyātika’’nti| tattha kālauti atītādayau, kārakamiti kammakattubhāvā, purisāti paṭhamamajjhimuttamā, kriyāti gamanapacanādikau dhātvatthau, kriyālakkhaṇaṃ sañ‍ñāṇaṃ aitassāti kriyālakkhaṇaṃ, atiliṅgañ‍ca|

vuttampi caitaṃ –

‘‘yaṃ tikālaṃ tipurisaṃ, kriyāvāci tikārakaṃ|

atiliṅgaṃ dvivacanaṃ, tadākhyātanti vuc‍catī’’ti||

kālādivasaina dhātvatthaṃ vibhajantīti vibhattiyau, tyādayau, tā pana vattamānā pañ‍camī sattamī paraukkhāhiyyattanī aj‍jatanī bhavissantī kālātipatti cāti aṭṭhavidhā bhavanti|

kriyaṃ dhāraintīti dhātavau, bhūvādayau, khādidhātuppac‍cayantā ca, tai pana atthavasā dvidhā bhavanti sakammakā, akammakā cāti| tatra sakammakā yai dhātavaukammāpaikkhaṃ kriyaṃ vadanti, yathā – kaṭaṃ karauti, gāmaṃ gacchati, audanaṃ pacatītiādayau, akammakā yai kammanirapaikkhaṃ kriyaṃ vadanti, yathā – acchati, saiti, tiṭṭhatītiādayau|

tai  pana sattavidhā bhavanti vikaraṇappac‍cayabhaidaina, kathaṃ? avikaraṇā bhūvādayau| niggahītapubbakaavikaraṇā rudhādayau, yavikaraṇā divādayau, ṇuṇā uṇāvikaraṇā svādayau, nāppaṇhāvikaraṇā kiyādayau, auyiravikaraṇā tanādayau, sakatthai ṇai ṇayantā curādayauti|

tattha paṭhamaṃ avikaraṇaisu bhūvādīsu dhātūsu paṭhamabhūtā akammakā bhūic‍caitasmā dhātutau tyādayau parā yaujīyantai|

bhūsattāyaṃ, ‘‘bhū’’ic‍cayaṃ dhātu sattāyamatthai vattatai, kriyāsāmañ‍ñabhūtai bhavanai vattataiti atthau|

‘‘bhū’’iti ṭhitai –

424. bhūvādayau dhātavau|

bhūic‍caivamādayau yai kriyāvācinau saddagaṇā, tai dhātusañ‍ñā haunti| bhū ādi yaisaṃ tai bhūvādayau, atha vā bhūvā ādī pakārā yaisaṃ tai bhūvādayau|

bhūvādīsu vakārauyaṃ, ñaiyyau āgamasandhijau|

bhūvāppakārā vā dhātū, sakammākammakatthatau||

‘‘k‍vaci dhātū’’tiāditau ‘‘k‍vacī’’ti vattatai|

425. dhātussantau laupaunaikassarassa|

anaikassarassa dhātussa antau k‍vaci laupau hauti|

k‍vaciggahaṇaṃ ‘‘mahīyati samathau’’tiādīsu nivattanatthaṃ, iti anaikassarattābhāvā idha dhātvantalaupau na hauti|

tatau  dhātvādhikāravihitānaikappac‍cayappasaṅgai ‘‘vatticchānupubbikā saddappaṭipattī’’ti katvā vattamānavacanicchāyaṃ –

426. vattamānā tianti, sitha, mima, taiantai, saivhai, aimhai|

tyādayau dvādasa vattamānāsañ‍ñā hauntīti tyādīnaṃ vattamānatthavisayattā vattamānāsañ‍ñā|

427. kālai|

ayamadhikārau|

itau paraṃ tyādivibhattividhānai sabbattha vattatai|

428. vattamānā pac‍cuppan‍nai|

pac‍cuppan‍nai kālai gamyamānai vattamānāvibhatti hauti, kālauti caittha kriyā, karaṇaṃ kārau, rakārassa lakārau, kālau|

tasmā –

kriyāya gamyamānāya, vibhattīnaṃ vidhānatau|

dhātūhaiva bhavantīti, siddhaṃ tyādivibhattiyau||

idha pana kālassa atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nāṇattiparikappakālābhipattivasaina chadhā bhin‍nattā ‘‘pac‍cuppan‍nai’’ti visaisaiti| taṃ taṃ kāraṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppan‍nau pac‍cuppan‍nau, paṭiladdhasabhāvau, na tāva atītauti atthau|

pac‍cuppan‍nasamīpaipi, tabbauhārūpacāratau|

vattamānā atītaipi, taṃkālavacanicchayāti||

tasmiṃ pac‍cuppan‍nai vattamānāvibhattiṃ katvā, tassā ṭhānāniyamai ‘‘dhātuliṅgaihi parā pac‍cayā’’ti paribhāsatau dhātutau  paraṃ vattamānappac‍cayai katvā, taisamaniyamappasaṅgaisati ‘‘vatticchānupubbikā saddappaṭipattī’’ti parassapadavacanicchāyaṃ –

429. atha pubbāni vibhattīnaṃ cha parassapadāni|

atha taddhitānantaraṃ vuc‍camānānaṃ sabbāsaṃ vattamānādīnaṃ aṭṭhavidhānaṃ vibhattīnaṃ yāni yāni pubbakāni cha padāni, tāni tāni atthatau aṭṭhacattālīsamattāni parassapadasañ‍ñāni hauntītiādimhi chan‍naṃ parassapadasañ‍ñā, parassatthāni padāni parassapadāni, tabbāhul‍latau tabbauhārau|

‘‘dhātūhi ṇai ṇaya’’ic‍cāditau ‘‘dhātūhī’’ti vattamānai –

430. kattari parassapadaṃ|

kattarikārakai abhidhaiyyai sabbadhātūhi parassapadaṃ hautīti parassapadaṃ katvā, tassāpyaniyamappasaṅgai vatticchāvasā –

vipariṇāmaina ‘‘parassapadānaṃ, attanaupadāna’’nti ca vattatai|

431. dvai dvai paṭhamamajjhimuttamapurisā|

tāsaṃ vibhattīnaṃ parassapadāna’mattanaupadānañ‍ca dvai dvai vacanāni yathāk‍kamaṃ paṭhamamajjhimuttamapurisasañ‍ñāni haunti| taṃ yathā? ti antiiti paṭhamapurisā, si thaiti majjhimapurisā, mi maiti uttamapurisā| attanaupadaisupi tai antaiiti paṭhamapurisā, sai vhaiiti majjhimapurisā, ai mhaiiti uttamapurisā| aivaṃ saisāsu sattasu vibhattīsupi yaujaitabbanti| aivaṃ aṭṭhavibhattivasaina chan‍navutividhai ākhyātapadai dvattiṃsa dvattiṃsa paṭhamamajjhimauttamapurisā hauntīti vattamānaparassapadādimhi dvin‍naṃ paṭhamapurisasañ‍ñā|

432. nāmamhi payuj‍jamānaipitulyādhikaraṇai paṭhamau|

tumhāmhasaddavaj‍jitai tulyādhikaraṇabhūtai sādhakavācakai nāmamhi payuj‍jamānaipi appayuj‍jamānaipi dhātūhi paṭhamapurisau hautīti paṭhamapurisaṃ katvā, tassāpyaniyamappasaṅgai kriyāsādhakassa kattunau aikattai vattumicchitai ‘‘aikamhi vattabbai aikavacana’’nti vattamānaparassapadapaṭhamapurisaikavacanaṃ ti|

‘‘parau, pac‍cayau, dhātū’’ti ca adhikārau, ‘‘yathā kattari cā’’ti itau ‘‘kattarī’’ti vikaraṇappac‍cayavidhānai sabbattha vattatai|

433. bhūvāditau a|

bhūic‍caivamāditau dhātugaṇatau parau apac‍cayau hauti kattari vihitaisu vibhattippac‍cayaisu paraisu| sabbadhātukamhiyaivāyamissatai|

‘‘asaṃyaugantassa, vuddhī’’ti ca vattatai|

434. añ‍ñaisu ca|

kāritatau añ‍ñaisu pac‍cayaisu asaṃyaugantānaṃ dhātūnaṃ vuddhi hauti| caggahaṇaina ṇuppac‍cayassāpi vuddhi hauti| aittha ca ‘‘ghaṭādīnaṃ vā’’ti itau vāsaddau anuvattaitabbau, sau ca vavatthitavibhāsatthau| taina –

ivaṇṇuvaṇṇantānañ‍ca, lahūpantāna dhātunaṃ|

ivaṇṇuvaṇṇānamaiva, vuddhi hauti parassa na||

yuvaṇṇānampi  ya ṇu ṇā-nāniṭṭhādīsu vuddhi na|

tudādissāvikaraṇai, na chaitvādīsu vā siyā||

tassāpyaniyamappasaṅgai – ‘‘ayuvaṇṇānañ‍cāyau vuddhī’’ti paribhāsatau ūkārassaukārau vuddhi|

vipariṇāmaina ‘‘dhātūna’’nti vattatai|

435. au ava sarai|

aukārassa dhātvantassa sarai parai avādaisau hauti| ‘‘saralaupau mādaisa’’ic‍cādinā saralaupādimhi katai ‘‘nayai paraṃ yuttai’’ti paranayanaṃ kātabbaṃ|

sau purisau sādhu bhavati, sā kañ‍ñā sādhu bhavati, taṃ cittaṃ sādhu bhavati|

aittha hi –

kattunaubhihitattāva, ākhyātaina na kattari|

tatiyā paṭhamā hauti, liṅgatthaṃ panapaikkhiya||

satipi kriyāyaikattai kattūnaṃ bahuttā ‘‘bahumhi vattabbai bahuvacana’’nti vattamānaparassapadapaṭhamapurisabahuvacanaṃ anti, purai viya appac‍cayavuddhiavādaisā, saralaupādi| tai purisā bhavanti, appayuj‍jamānaipi bhavati, bhavanti|

‘‘payuj‍jamānaipi, tulyādhikaraṇai’’ti ca vattatai|

436. tumhai majjhimau|

tulyādhikaraṇabhūtai tumhasaddai payuj‍jamānaipi appayuj‍jamānaipi dhātūhi majjhimapurisau hautīti vattamānaparassapadamajjhimapurisaikavacanaṃsi, saisaṃ purimasamaṃ| tvaṃ bhavasi, tumhai bhavatha, appayuj‍jamānaipi bhavasi, bhavatha|

tulyādhikaraṇaiti  kimatthaṃ? tayā pac‍catai audanau|

tasmiṃyaivādhikārai –

437. amhai uttamau|

tulyādhikaraṇabhūtai amhasaddai payuj‍jamānaipi appayuj‍jamānaipi dhātūhi uttamapurisau hautīti vattamānaparassapadauttamapurisaikavacanaṃ mi, appac‍cayavuddhiavādaisā|

438. akārau dīghaṃ himimaisu|

akārau dīghamāpaj‍jatai himimaic‍caitāsu vibhattīsu| ahaṃ bhavāmi, mayaṃ bhavāma| bhavāmi, bhavāma|

‘‘vibhattīnaṃ, chā’’ti ca vattatai|

439. parāṇyattanaupadāni|

sabbāsaṃ vattamānānaṃ aṭṭhavidhānaṃ vibhattīnaṃ yāni yāni parāni cha padāni, tāni tāni attanaupadasañ‍ñāni hauntīti taiādīnaṃ attanaupadasañ‍ñā|

‘‘dhātūhi, attanaupadānī’’ti ca vattatai|

440. kattari ca|

kattari ca kārakai abhidhaiyyai dhātūhi attanaupadāni haunti| caggahaṇaṃ katthaci nivattanatthaṃ, saisaṃ parassapadai vuttanayainaiva vaiditabbaṃ| bhavatai, bhavantai, bhavasai, bhavavhai, bhavai, bhavāmhai|

paca pākai, dhātusañ‍ñāyaṃ dhātvantalaupau, vuttanayainaiva tyādyuppatti, ivaṇṇuvaṇṇānamabhāvā vuddhiabhāvauvaittha visaisau| sau daivadattau audanaṃ pacati, pacanti, pacasi, pacatha, pacāmi, pacāma, sau audanaṃ pacatai, tai pacantai, tvaṃ pacasai, tumhai pacavhai, ahaṃ pacai, mayaṃ pacāmhai|

paṭhamapurisādīnamaikajjhappavattippasaṅgai paribhāsamāha –

441. sabbaisamaikābhidhānai parau purisau|

sabbaisaṃ paṭhamamajjhimānaṃ, paṭhamuttamānaṃ, majjhimuttamānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ vā purisānaṃ aikataubhidhānai kātabbai parau purisau yaujaitabbau| aikakālānamaivābhidhānai cāyaṃ| sau ca pacati, tvañ‍ca pacasīti pariyāyappasaṅgai tumhai pacathāti bhavati| aivaṃ sau ca pacati, ahañ‍ca pacāmīti mayaṃ pacāma, tathā tvañ‍ca pacasi, ahañ‍ca pacāmi, mayaṃ pacāma, sau ca pacati, tvañ‍ca pacasi, ahañ‍ca pacāmi, mayaṃ pacāma| aivaṃ sabbattha yaujaitabbaṃ|

aikābhidhānaiti kimatthaṃ? ‘‘sau ca pacati, tvañ‍ca pacissasi, ahaṃ paciṃ’’ aittha bhin‍nakālattā ‘‘mayaṃ pacimhā’’ti na bhavati|

gamu sappa gatimhi, purai viya dhātusañ‍ñāyaṃ dhātvantalaupau|

kattari tyādyuppatti|

442. gamissantau cchau vā sabbāsu|

gamuic‍caitassa dhātussantau makārau cchau hauti vā sabbāsu vibhattīsu, sabbaggahaṇaina mānanta ya kāritappac‍cayaisu ca| vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau| taināyaṃ –

vidhiṃ nic‍cañ‍ca vāsaddau, māna’ntaisu tu kattari|

dīpaitānic‍camañ‍ñattha, paraukkhāyamasantakaṃ||

appac‍cayaparanayanāni, sau purisau gāmaṃ gacchati, tai gacchanti, ‘‘k‍vaci dhātū’’tiādinā garupubbarassatau parassa paṭhamapurisabahuvacanassa rai vā hauti, gaccharai| tvaṃ gacchasi, tumhai gacchatha| ahaṃ gacchāmi, mayaṃ gacchāma|

cchādaisābhāvai ‘‘laupañ‍caittamakārau’’ti appac‍cayassa aikārau| gamaiti, gamainti, saralaupau| gamaisi, gamaitha| gamaimi, gamaima|

attanaupadaipi sau gāmaṃ gacchatai, gacchantai, gaccharai| gacchasai, gacchavhai| gacchai, gacchāmhai|

‘‘kutau nu tvaṃ āgacchasi, rājagahatau āgacchāmī’’tiādīsu pana pac‍cuppan‍nasamīpai vattamānavacanaṃ|

‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

443. gamissa ghammaṃ|

gamuic‍caitassa dhātussa sabbassa ghammādaisau hauti vā| ghammati, ghammanti ic‍cādi|

bhāvakammaisu pana –

444. attanaupadāni bhāvai ca kammani|

bhāvai ca kammani ca kārakai abhidhaiyyai attanaupadāni haunti, casaddaina kammakattaripi| bhavanaṃ bhāvau, sau ca kārakantaraina asaṃsaṭṭhau kaivalau bhavanalavanādikau dhātvatthau| karīyatīti kammaṃ| akammakāpi dhātavau saupasaggā sakammakāpi bhavanti, tasmā kammani anupubbā bhūdhātutau vattamānattanaupadapaṭhamapurisaikavacanaṃ tai|

‘‘dhātūhi  ṇai ṇaya’’ic‍cāditau ‘‘dhātūhī’’ti vattamānai –

445. bhāvakammaisu yau|

sabbadhātūhi parau bhāvakammaisu yappac‍cayau hauti| attanaupadavisayaivāyamissatai, ‘‘añ‍ñaisu cā’’ti suttai anuvattitavāggahaṇaina yappac‍cayai vuddhi na bhavati, anubhūyatai sukhaṃ daivadattaina|

ākhyātaina avuttattā, tatiyā hauti kattari|

kammassābhihitattā na, dutiyā paṭhamāvidha||

anubhūyantai sampattiyau tayā| anubhūyasai tvaṃ daivadattaina, anubhūyavhai tumhai| ahaṃ anubhūyai tayā, mayaṃ anubhūyāmhai|

‘‘k‍vaci dhātu’’ic‍cāditau ‘‘k‍vacī’’ti vattamānai –

446. attanaupadāni parassapadattaṃ|

attanaupadāni k‍vaci parassapadattamāpaj‍jantai, akattariyaivaitaṃ| yakārassa dvittaṃ, anubhūyyati mayā sukhaṃ, anubhūyyatai vā, anubhūyyanti| anubhūyyasi, anubhūyyatha| anubhūyyāmi, anubhūyyāma| dvittābhāvai – anubhūyati, anubhūyanti|

k‍vacīti kiṃ? anubhūyatai|

bhāvai adabbavuttinau bhāvassaikattā aikavacanamaiva, tañ‍ca paṭhamapurisassaiva, bhūyatai daivadattaina, daivadattaina sampati bhavananti atthau|

pacadhātutau  kammani attanaupadai yappac‍cayai ca katai –

vipariṇāmaina ‘‘yassā’’ti vattamānai –

447. tassa cavaggayakāravakārattaṃ sadhātvantassa|

tassa bhāvakammavisayassa yappac‍cayassa cavaggayakāravakārattaṃ hauti dhātvantaina saha yathāsambhavaṃ| aittha ca ‘‘ivaṇṇāgamau vā’’ti itau sīhagatiyā vāsaddau anuvattaitabbau, sau ca vavatthitavibhāsatthau| taina –

cavaggau ca ta vaggānaṃ, dhātvantānaṃ yavattanaṃ|

ravānañ‍ca sayappac‍ca-yānaṃ hauti yathāk‍kamanti||

dhātvantassa cavaggādittā cakārai katai ‘‘paradvaibhāvau ṭhānai’’ti cakārassa dvittaṃ| pac‍catai audanau daivadattaina, ‘‘k‍vaci dhātū’’tiādinā garupubbarassatau parassa paṭhamapurisabahuvacanassa k‍vaci rai hauti| pac‍carai, pac‍cantai| pac‍casai, pac‍cavhai| pac‍cai, pac‍cāmhai|

parassapadādaisai pac‍cati, pac‍canti| pac‍casi, pac‍catha| pac‍cāmi, pac‍cāma| tathā kammakattari pac‍catai audanau sayamaiva, pac‍cantai| pac‍cati, pac‍canti vā ic‍cādi|

gamitau kammani attanaupadai, yappac‍cayai ca katai –

‘‘dhātūhi, tasmiṃ, yai’’ti ca vattatai|

448. ivaṇṇāgamau vā|

sabbaihi dhātūhi tasmiṃ bhāvakammavisayai yappac‍cayai parai ivaṇṇāgamau hauti vāti īkārāgamau| vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau| cchādaisau, gacchīyatai gāmau daivadattaina, gacchīyantai| gacchīyasai, gacchīyavhai| gacchīyai, gacchīyāmhai|

cchādaisābhāvai –

‘‘dhātūhi, yau, vā’’ti ca vattatai|

449. pubbarūpañ‍ca|

haiṭṭhānuttaihi parassaivaidaṃ, taina kaṭapavaggayakāralasantaihaiva dhātūhi parau yappac‍cayau pubbarūpamāpaj‍jatai vāti makārā parassa yakārassa makārau| gammatai, gamīyatai, gammantai, gamīyantai| gammasai, gamīyasai, gammavhai, gamīyavhai| gammai, gamīyai, gammāmhai, gamīyāmhai|

parassapadattai – gacchīyyati, gacchīyyanti| gacchīyati, gacchīyanti vā| gammati, gammanti| gamīyati, gamīyanti| ikārāgamai gamiyyati, gamiyyanti| tathā ghammīyati, ghammīyanti ic‍cādi|

vattamānāvibhatti|

450. pañ‍camī tu antu, hi tha, mi ma, taṃ antaṃ, ssuvhau, ai āmasai|

tvādayau dvādasa pañ‍camīsañ‍ñā haunti|

451. āṇatyāsiṭṭhainuttakālai pañ‍camī|

āṇatyatthai ca āsīsatthai anuttakālai pañ‍camīvibhatti hauti|

satipi kālādhikārai puna kālaggahaṇaina vidhinimantanājjhaisanānumatipatthanāpattakālādīsu ca pañ‍camī| āṇāpanamāṇatti, āsīsanamāsiṭṭhau, sau ca iṭṭhassa asampattassa  atthassa patthanaṃ, tasmiṃ āṇatyāsiṭṭhai| anu samīpai uttakālau anuttakālau, pac‍cuppan‍nakālauti atthau, na uttakālauti vā anuttakālau, tasmiṃ anuttakālai, kālamanāmasitvā hautīti atthau|

tattha āsīsanatthai bhūdhātutau pañ‍camīparassapadapaṭhamapurisaikavacanaṃ tu, appac‍cayavuddhiavādaisā| sau sukhī bhavatu, tai sukhitā bhavantu|

vipariṇāmaina ‘‘akāratau’’ti vattatai|

452. hilaupaṃ vā|

akāratau parau hivibhatti laupamāpaj‍jatai vā| tvaṃ sukhī bhava, bhavāhi vā, himhi dīghau| tumhai sukhitā bhavatha| ahaṃ sukhī bhavāmi, mayaṃ sukhinau bhavāma|

attanaupadai sau sukhī bhavataṃ, tai sukhitā bhavantaṃ| tvaṃ sukhī bhavassu, tumhai sukhitā bhavavhau| ahaṃ sukhī bhavai, mayaṃ sukhitā bhavāmasai|

kammani anubhūyataṃ tayā, anubhūyantaṃ| anubhūyassu, anubhūyavhau| anubhūyai, anubhūyāmasai| parassapadattai anubhūyyatu, anubhūyyantu| anubhūyatu, anubhūyantu vā, anubhūyyāhi ic‍cādi| bhāvai bhūyataṃ|

āṇattiyaṃ kattari daivadattau dāni audanaṃ pacatu, pacantu| paca, pacāhi, pacatha| pacāmi, pacāma| pacataṃ, pacantaṃ| pacassu, pacavhau| pacai, pacāmasai|

kammani yappac‍cayacavaggādi, pac‍cataṃ audanau daivadattaina, pac‍cantaṃ| pac‍cassu, pac‍cavhau| pac‍cai, pac‍cāmasai| parassapadattai pac‍catu, pac‍cantu| pac‍ca, pac‍cāhi, pac‍catha| pac‍cāmi, pac‍cāma|

tathā  sau gāmaṃ gacchatu, gacchantu| gaccha, gacchāhi, gacchatha| gacchāmi, gacchāma| gamaitu, gamaintu| gama, gamāhi, gamaitha| gamaimi, gamaima| gacchataṃ, gacchantaṃ| gacchassu, gacchavhau| gacchai, gacchāmasai| ghammādaisai ghammatu, ghammantu ic‍cādi|

kammani gacchīyataṃ, gacchīyatu, gamīyataṃ, gamīyatu, gammataṃ, gammatu ic‍cādi|

vidhimhi idha pabbatau hautu, ayaṃ pāsādau suvaṇṇamayau hautūtiādi|

nimantanai adhivāsaitu mai bhantai bhagavā bhaujanaṃ, idha nisīdatu bhavaṃ|

ajjhaisanai daisaitu bhantai bhagavā dhammaṃ|

anumatiyaṃ pucchatu bhavaṃ pañhaṃ, pavisatu bhavaṃ, aittha nisīdatu|

patthanā yācanā, dadāhi mai gāmavarāni pañ‍ca, aikaṃ mai nayanaṃ daihi|

pattakālai sampattau tai kālau kaṭakaraṇai, kaṭaṃ karautu bhavaṃ ic‍cādi|

pañ‍camīvibhatti|

453. sattamī aiyya aiyyuṃ, aiyyāsi aiyyātha, aiyyāmiaiyyāma, aitha airaṃ, aithau aiyyāvhau, aiyyaṃaiyyāmhai|

aiyyādayau dvādasa sattamīsañ‍ñā haunti|

‘‘anuttakālai’’ti vattatai|

454. anumatiparikappatthaisu  sattamī|

anumatyatthai ca parikappatthai ca anuttakālai sattamīvibhatti hauti|

atthaggahaṇaina vidhinimantanādīsu ca sattamī| kattumicchatau parassa anujānanaṃ anumati, parikappanaṃ parikappau, ‘‘yadi nāma bhavaiyyā’’ti sal‍lakkhaṇaṃ nirūpanaṃ, haitukriyāya sambhavai phalakriyāya sambhavaparikappau ca|

tattha parikappai sattamīparassapadapaṭhamapurisaikavacanaṃ aiyya, appac‍cayavuddhādi purimasamaṃ, ‘‘k‍vaci dhātu vibhattī’’tiādinā aiyya aiyyāsi aiyyāmi aiyyaṃic‍caitaisaṃ vikappaina aikārādaisau| sau dāni kiṃ nu khau bhavai, yadi sau paṭhamavayai pabbajaiyya, arahā bhavaiyya, sacai saṅkhārā nic‍cā bhavaiyyuṃ, na nirujjhaiyyuṃ| yadi tvaṃ bhavaiyyāsi, tumhai bhavaiyyātha| kathamahaṃ daivau bhavaiyyāmi, kiṃ nu khau mayaṃ bhavaiyyāma| tathā bhavaitha, bhavairaṃ| bhavaithau, bhavaiyyāvhau|

patthanai tu ahaṃ sukhī bhavai, buddhau bhavaiyyaṃ, bhavaiyyāmhai|

kammani sukhaṃ tayā anubhūyaitha, anubhūyairaṃ| anubhūyaithau, anubhūyaiyyāvhau| anubhūyai, anubhūyaiyyaṃ, anubhūyaiyyāmhai| parassapadattai anubhūyaiyya, anubhūyaiyyuṃ| anubhūyaiyyāsi ic‍cādi| bhāvai bhūyaitha|

vidhimhi sau audanaṃ pacai, pacaiyya, pacaiyyuṃ| tvaṃ pacai, pacaiyyāsi, tumhai pacaiyyātha| ahaṃ pacai, pacaiyyāmi, mayaṃ pacaiyyāma| pacaitha, pacairaṃ| pacaithau, pacaiyyāvhau| pacai, pacaiyyaṃ, pacaiyyāmhai|

kammani  pac‍caitha, pac‍cairaṃ| pac‍caithau, pac‍caiyyāvhau| pac‍cai, pac‍caiyyaṃ, pac‍caiyyāmhai| parassapadattai pac‍cai, pac‍caiyya, pac‍caiyyuṃ| pac‍caiyyāsi ic‍cādi|

anumatiyaṃ sau gāmaṃ gacchai, gacchaiyya, ‘‘k‍vaci dhātū’’tiādinā aiyyussa uṃ vā, gacchuṃ, gacchaiyyuṃ| tvaṃ gacchai, gacchaiyyāsi, gacchaiyyātha| gacchai, gacchaiyyāmi, gacchaiyyāma| gamai, gamaiyya, gamuṃ, gamaiyyuṃ| gamai, gamaiyyāsi, gamaiyyātha| gamai, gamaiyyāmi, gamaiyyāma| gacchaitha, gacchairaṃ| gacchaithau, gacchaiyyāvhau| gacchai, gacchaiyyaṃ, gacchaiyyāmhai| gamaitha, gamairaṃ ic‍cādi|

kammani gacchīyaitha, gamīyaitha, gacchīyairaṃ, gamīyairaṃ ic‍cādi| parassapadattai gacchīyaiyya, gamīyaiyya, gammaiyya, gammaiyyuṃ ic‍cādi| tathā ghammai, ghammaiyya, ghammaiyyuṃ ic‍cādi|

sattamīvibhatti|

pac‍cuppan‍nāṇattiparikappakālikavibhattinayau|

455. hiyyattanī āū, auttha, aṃmhā, tthatthuṃ, saivhaṃ, iṃmhasai|

āādayau dvādasa hiyyattanīsañ‍ñā haunti|

‘‘appac‍cakkhai, atītai’’ti ca vattatai|

456. hiyyaupabhuti pac‍cakkhai hiyyattanī|

hiyyaupabhuti atītai kālai pac‍cakkhai vā appac‍cakkhai vā hiyyattanīvibhatti hautīti hiyyattanīparassapadapaṭhamapurisaikavacanaṃ ā|

‘‘k‍vaci dhātu’’ic‍cāditau ‘‘k‍vaci, dhātūna’’nti ca vattatai|

457. akārāgamau  hiyyattanī aj‍jatanīkālātipattīsu|

k‍vaci dhātūnamādimhi akārāgamau hauti hiyyattanīaj‍jatanīkālātipattiic‍caitāsu tīsu vibhattīsu| kathamayamakārāgamau dhātvādimhīti cai?

satissaraipi dhātvantai, punakārāgamassidha|

niratthattā payaugānu raudhā dhātvāditau ayaṃ||

appac‍cayavuddhiavādaisasaralaupādi vuttanayamaiva|

abhavā, abhavū| abhavau, ‘‘k‍vaci dhātū’’tiādinā aukārassa aādaisau vā, abhava, abhavattha| abhavaṃ, abhavamhā| abhavattha, abhavatthuṃ| abhavasai, abhavavhaṃ| abhaviṃ, abhavamhasai|

kammani yappac‍cayau, tayā sukhamanvabhūyattha, akārāgamābhāvai anubhūyattha, ‘‘k‍vaci dhātū’’tiādinā tthassa thādaisau, anvabhūyatha, anubhūyatha, anvabhūyatthuṃ, anubhūyatthuṃ| anvabhūyasai, anubhūyasai, anvabhūyavhaṃ, anubhūyavhaṃ| anvabhūyiṃ, anubhūyiṃ, anvabhūyamhasai, anubhūyamhasai| parassapadattai anvabhūyā, anubhūyā ic‍cādi| bhāvai anvabhūyattha|

tathā sau audanaṃ apacā, pacā, apacū, pacū| apacau, pacau, apacattha, pacattha| apacaṃ, pacaṃ, apacamhā, pacamhā| apacattha, pacattha, apacatthu, pacatthuṃ| apacasai, pacasai, apacavhaṃ, pacavhaṃ| apaciṃ, paciṃ, apacamhasai, pacamhasai|

kammani  apac‍catha, apac‍cattha, apac‍catthuṃ| apac‍casai, apac‍cavhaṃ| apac‍ciṃ, apac‍camhasai| apac‍cā, apac‍cū ic‍cādi|

tathā agacchā, agacchū| agacchau, agaccha, agacchattha| agacchaṃ, agacchamhā| agacchattha, agacchatthuṃ| agacchasai, agacchavhaṃ| agacchiṃ, agacchamhasai| agamā, agamū| agamau, agama, agamattha| agamaṃ, agamamhā| agamattha, agamatthuṃ| agamasai, agamavhaṃ| agamiṃ, agamamhasai|

kammani agacchīyattha, gacchīyattha, agamīyattha, gamīyattha, agacchīyatthuṃ, gacchīyatthuṃ, agamīyatthuṃ, gamīyatthuṃ ic‍cādi| tathā aghammā, aghammū ic‍cādi|

hiyyattanīvibhatti|

458. hiyyattanī sattamī pañ‍camī vattamānā sabbadhātukaṃ|

hiyyattanādayau catassau vibhattiyau sabbadhātukasañ‍ñā hauntīti hiyyattanādīnaṃ sabbadhātukasañ‍ñattā ‘‘ikārāgamau asabbadhātukamhī’’ti vuttau ikārāgamau na bhavati|

sabbadhātukaṃ|

459. paraukkhā a u, ai ttha, aṃ mha, ttha rai, thau vhau, iṃmhai|

aādayau dvādasa paraukkhāsañ‍ñā haunti| akkhānaṃ indriyānaṃ paraṃ paraukkhā, taddīpakattā ayaṃ vibhatti paraukkhāti vuc‍cati|

460. apac‍cakkhai  paraukkhātītai|

apac‍cakkhai vattunau indriyāvisayabhūtai atītai kālai paraukkhāvibhatti hauti| atik‍kamma itauti atītau, hutvā atik‍kantauti atthau|

haiṭṭhā vuttanayaina paraukkhāparassapadapaṭhamapurisaikavacanaṃ a| ‘‘bhū a’’itīdha –

vipariṇāmaina ‘‘dhātūna’’nti vattatai|

461. k‍vacādivaṇṇānamaikassarānaṃ dvaibhāvau|

dhātūnamādibhūtānaṃ vaṇṇānamaikassarānaṃ k‍vaci dvaibhāvau hauti| vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ k‍vacisaddau, taina –

kha cha saisu paraukkhāyaṃ, dvaibhāvau sabbadhātunaṃ|

appac‍cayai juhautyādi-ssapi kic‍cādikai k‍vaci||

‘‘bhū bhū a’’itīdha –

462. pubbaubbhāsau|

dvaibhūtassa dhātussa yau pubbau avayavau, sau abbhāsasañ‍ñau hautīti abbhāsasañ‍ñā|

abbhāsaggahaṇamanuvattatai|

463. antassivaṇṇākārau vā|

abbhāsassa antassa ivaṇṇau hauti vā, akārau ca| vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau| taina –

kha  cha saisu avaṇṇassa,

ikārau sagupussa ī|

vāssa bhūssa paraukkhāyaṃ,

akārau nāparassimaiti||

ūkārassa akārau|

464. dutiyacatutthānaṃ paṭhamatatiyā|

abbhāsagatānaṃ dutiyacatutthānaṃ vaggabyañ‍janānaṃ yathāk‍kamaṃ paṭhamatatiyā hauntīti bhakārassa bakārau|

465. brūbhūnamāhabhūvā paraukkhāyaṃ|

brūbhūic‍caitaisaṃ dhātūnaṃ āhabhūvaic‍caitai ādaisā haunti paraukkhāvibhattiyanti bhūsaddassa bhūvaādaisau, ‘‘saralaupau mādaisappac‍cayādimhī’’tiādinā saralaupādi, sau kira rājā babhūva, tai kira babhūvu| tvaṃ kira babhūvai|

‘‘dhātūhī’’ti vattatai, sīhagatiyā k‍vaciggahaṇañ‍ca|

466. ikārāgamau asabbadhātukamhi|

sabbasmiṃ asabbadhātukamhi parai k‍vaci dhātūhi parau ikārāgamau hauti|

asabbadhātukai byañ‍ja-nādimhai vāyamāgamau|

k‍vacādhikāratau byañ‍ja-nādaupi k‍vaci nau siyā||

aittha ca ‘‘na sabbadhātukaṃ asabbadhātuka’’miti katvā ‘‘hiyyattanī sattamī pañ‍camī vattamānā sabbadhātuka’’nti hiyyattanīādīnaṃ sabbadhātukasañ‍ñāya vuttattā tadañ‍ñā catassau vibhattiyau asabbadhātukanti vuc‍cati|

tumhai  kira babhūvittha| ahaṃ kira babhūvaṃ, mayaṃ kira babhūvimha| attanaupadai sau babhūvittha, babhūvirai| babhūvitthau, babhūvivhau| babhūviṃ, babhūvimhai|

kammani attanaupadai īkārāgamayappac‍cayikārāgamā, anubabhūvīyittha, yappac‍cayassa asabbadhātukamhi ‘‘k‍vaci dhātū’’tiādinā laupai katai ivaṇṇāgamau na bhavati, tayā kira anubabhūvittha, anubabhūvirai ic‍cādi| bhāvai babhūvīyittha, babhūvittha vā|

tathā papaca, papacū| papacai, papacittha| papacaṃ, papacimha| papacittha, papacirai| papacitthau, papacivhau| papaciṃ, papacimhai|

kammani papac‍cittha, papac‍cirai ic‍cādi| tathā apac‍ca, apac‍cū ic‍cādi|

gamimhi ‘‘k‍vacādivaṇṇāna’’ntiādinā dvaibhāvau, ‘‘pubbaubbhāsau’’ti abbhāsasañ‍ñā|

‘‘abbhāsai’’ti vattatai|

467. kavaggassa cavaggau|

abbhāsai vattamānassa kavaggassa cavaggau hautīti vakārassa jakārau, ‘‘k‍vaci dhātū’’tiādinā anabbhāsassa paṭhamapurisaikavacanamhi dīghau| sau gāmaṃ jagāma kira, jagama vā, jagamu| jagamai, jagamittha| jagamaṃ, jagamimha| jagamittha, jagamirai| jagamitthau, jagamivhau| jagamiṃ, jagamimhai|

kammani jagamīyittha, jagamittha vā ic‍cādi|

paraukkhāvibhatti|

468. aj‍jatanī  ī uṃ, au ttha, iṃ mhā, ā ū, sai vhaṃ, amhai|

īādayau dvādasa aj‍jatanīsañ‍ñā haunti| aj‍ja bhavau aj‍jatanau, taddīpakattā ayaṃ vibhatti aj‍jatanīti vuc‍cati|

‘‘apac‍cakkhai, atītai, pac‍cakkhai’’ti ca vattatai|

469. samīpaij‍jatanī|

samīpai samīpatau paṭṭhāya aj‍jappabhuti atītai kālai pac‍cakkhai ca apac‍cakkhai ca aj‍jatanīvibhatti hautīti aj‍jatanīparassapadapaṭhamapurisaikavacanaṃ ī|

purai viya akārāgamau, vuddhādi ca, ‘‘k‍vaci dhātuvibhattī’’tiādinā īmhādivibhattīnaṃ k‍vaci rassattaṃ, auāavacanānaṃ itthaamādaisā ca, saralaupādi, sau abhavi, abhavī vā, akārāgamābhāvai bhavi|

maṇḍūkagatiyā ‘‘vā’’ti vattatai|

470. sabbatau uṃ iṃ su|

sabbaihi dhātūhi uṃvibhattissa iṃsvādaisau hauti vā|

tai abhaviṃsu, bhaviṃsu vā, abhavuṃ, bhavuṃ vā| tvaṃ abhavi, bhavi vā, abhavau, bhavau vā, tumhai abhavittha, bhavittha vā, ikārāgamau| ahaṃ abhaviṃ, bhaviṃ vā, mayaṃ abhavimha, bhavimha vā, abhavimhā, bhavimhā vā| sau abhavittha, bhavittha vā, abhavā, bhavā vā, abhavū, bhavū vā| abhavisai, bhavisai vā, abhavivhaṃ, bhavivhaṃ vā| abhavaṃ, bhavaṃ vā, abhava, bhava vā, abhavimhai, bhavimhai vā|

kammani yappac‍cayalaupai vuddhiavādaisādi, sukhaṃ tayā anubhavittha, anvabhūyittha, anubhūyittha vā ic‍cādi| parassapadattai tayā anvabhūyi, anubhūyi, anvabhūyī, anubhūyī vā, anvabhūyiṃsu, anubhūyiṃsu, anvabhūyuṃ, anubhūyuṃ| tvaṃ anvabhūyi, anubhūyi, tumhai anvabhūyittha, anubhūyittha| ahaṃ anvabhūyiṃ, anubhūyiṃ, mayaṃ anvabhūyimha, anubhūyimha, anvabhūyimhā, anubhūyimhā vā| bhāvai abhavittha, abhūyittha tayā|

471. māyaugai sabbakālai ca|

484. kālātipatti ssā ssaṃsu, ssai ssatha, ssaṃssāmhā, ssatha ssisu, ssasai ssavhai, ssiṃssāmhasai|

475. kriyātipan‍naitītai kālātipatti|

kriyātipan‍namattai atītai kālai kālātipattivibhatti hauti| kriyāya atipatanaṃ kriyātipan‍naṃ, taṃ pana sādhakasattivirahaina kriyāya ac‍cantānuppatti| aittha ca kiñ‍cāpi na kriyā atītasaddaina vauharitabbā, tathāpi tak‍kiriyuppattippaṭibandhakarakriyāya kālabhaidaina atītavauhārau labbhataivāti daṭṭhabbaṃ|

sau cai paṭhamavayai pabbaj‍jaṃ alabhissa, arahā abhavissa, bhavissa, abhavissā, bhavissāvā, tai cai taṃ alabhissaṃsu, arahantau abhavissaṃsu, bhavissaṃsu| aivaṃ tvaṃ abhavissa, bhavissa, abhavissai vā, tumhai abhavissatha, bhavissatha| ahaṃ abhavissaṃ, bhavissaṃ, mayaṃ  abhavissamha, bhavissamha, abhavissāmhā, bhavissāmhā vā| sau abhavissatha, abhavissisu| abhavissasai, abhavissavhai| abhavissiṃ, abhavissāmhasai|

476. isuyamūnamantau cchau vā|

isuyamuic‍caitaisaṃ dhātūnaṃ antau cchau hauti vā| vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau, ‘‘antau cchau vā’’ti yaugavibhāgaina āsassapi| sau saggaṃ icchati, icchanti| icchasi, icchatha| icchāmi, icchāma| cchādaisābhāvai asaṃyaugantattā ‘‘añ‍ñaisu cā’’ti vuddhi, aisati, aisanti ic‍cādi|

kammai attanaupadassa yaibhuyyaina parassapadattamaiva payaujīyati, taina caittha attanaupadai rūpāni saṅkhipissāma| sau icchīyati, aisīyati, issatai, issati, yakārassa  pubbarūpattaṃ| tathā icchatu, aisatu| icchaiyya, aisaiyya| paraukkhāhiyyattanīsu pana rūpāni sabbattha payaugamanugamma payaujaitabbāni, icchi, aisi| icchissati, aisissati| icchissā, aisissā icchādi|

kammai niyacchīyati, niyamīyati, niyammati, sañ‍ñamīyati vā| tathā niyacchatu, sañ‍ñamatu| niyacchaiyya, sañ‍ñamaiyya| niyacchī, sañ‍ñamī| niyacchissati, sañ‍ñamissati| niyacchissa, sañ‍ñamissa ic‍cādi|

477. labhasmā ī iṃnaṃ tthatthaṃ|

478. vaca vasa vahādīnamukārau vassa yai|

vaca vasa vahaic‍caivamādīnaṃ dhātūnaṃ vakārassa ukārau hauti yappac‍cayai parai, ādisaddaina vaḍḍhassa ca| ‘‘vassa a va’’iti samāsaina dutiyañ‍caittha vaggahaṇaṃ icchitabbaṃ, taina akārassapi ukārau hauti, purimapakkhai paralaupau| ‘‘tassa cavagga’’ic‍cādinā sadhātvantassa yakārassa cakārau, dvittaṃ| uc‍catai, uc‍cantai| vuc‍catai, vuc‍cantai| vuc‍cati, vuc‍canti vā ic‍cādi| tathā vacatu, vuc‍catu| vacaiyya, vuc‍caiyya| avacā, avac‍cā, avacū, avac‍cū| avaca, avacau, avacuttha| avaca, avacaṃ, avacamhā| avacuttha ic‍cādi|

479. vacassaj‍jatanimhi  makārau au|

480. kusasmā dī cchi|

481. ha vipariyayau lau vā|

hakārassa vipariyayau hauti yappac‍cayai parai, yappac‍cayassa ca lakārau hauti vā| vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau, taina ‘‘gayhatī’’tiādīsu lattaṃ na hauti, nimittabhūtassa yakārassaivaitaṃ lattaṃ, ‘‘vacavasa’’ic‍cādinā uttaṃ| vuyhati, vulhati, vuyhanti| vahatu, vuyhatu| vahaiyya, vuyhaiyya| avahī, avuyhittha, avahittha| avahissati, vuyhissati| avahissa, avuyhissa ic‍cādi|

482. jaramarānaṃ jīrajīyyamīyyā vā|

jaramara ic‍caitaisaṃ dhātūnaṃ jīrajīyyamīyyādaisā haunti vā, saralaupādi| jīrati, jīranti| jīyyati, jīyyanti| ‘‘k‍vacā’’disuttaina aikayakārassa k‍vaci laupau hauti| jīyati, jīyanti|

mara pāṇacāgai, mīyyādaisau, mīyyati, mīyyanti| mīyati, mīyanti vā| marati, maranti ic‍cādi|

483. disassa  passa dissa dakkhā vā|

disaic‍caitassa dhātussa passa dissa dakkhaic‍caitai ādaisā haunti vā| vavatthitavibhāsatthauyaṃ vāsaddau, taina dissādaisau kammani sabbadhātukai aiva| passati, passanti| dakkhati, dakkhanti|

kammani yakāralaupau, dissatai, dissantai| dissati, dissanti| vipassīyati, dakkhīyati| passatu, dakkhatu, dissatu| passaiyya, dakkhaiyya, dissaiyya|

hiyyattaniyaṃ ‘‘k‍vaci dhātū’’tiādinā dhātuikārassa attaṃ, addasā, addasa| kammani adissa|

tathā apassi, passi, apassiṃsu, passiṃsu| apassi, passi, apassittha, passittha| apassiṃ, passiṃ, apassimha, passimha| addasi, dasi, addasaṃsu, dasaṃsu| kammani adissaṃsu| addakkhi, addakkhiṃsu|

sada visaraṇagatyāvasānaisu|
